Conclusion: 空间位置感知的视觉调制与以发现为中心的对比表征学习可有效提升放射报告生成的发现级准确性，且具备一定跨数据集泛化能力；该方法为诊断驱动报告生成中"预测发现如何真正作用于视觉特征"这一问题提供了无需区域标注的解决思路。

Abstract: Radiology report generation models can produce fluent text while still containing finding-level inaccuracies. Diagnosis-driven methods improve generation by conditioning on predicted findings, but these predictions do not directly modify the visual patch features provided to the decoder, and global gating applies the same modulation across spatial locations. We introduce a Position-Aware Gate (PAG) that uses predicted finding representations to modulate visual patches spatially without region supervision. We also propose a Decoder-level Supervised Contrastive Loss (DSCL) that structures decoder representations using shared positive findings rather than instance identity. On MIMIC-CXR, PAG+DSCL improves clinical efficacy (CE) F1 from 0.484 to 0.502 over a matched global-gate reference, while PAG and DSCL individually reach 0.491 and 0.495. Without additional fine-tuning, the combined model achieves 0.226 CE F1 on IU X-Ray, compared with 0.211 reported by PromptMRG.

</details>


### [35] [Referring Multi-Object Tracking in Moving-Camera Videos via Global Motion Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04185)
*Hsin-Chen Pai,Jyun-Kai Wang,Yi-Cheng Peng,Wei-Ta Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在驾驶视角视频中估计相机运动、提取残差运动（补偿后的真实物体运动），与查询文本做运动匹配度计算，并以晚期融合方式接入现有RMOT方法，将运动补偿作为即插即用模块验证跨方法性能增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考多目标跟踪（RMOT）依赖外观线索关联文本，而许多跟踪需求实际指向"物体如何运动"。在运动相机拍摄的视频中，相机引起的表观位移会与物体自身运动混淆：静止车辆可能看似在动，行驶车辆可能几乎无位移。已有RMOT方法虽将运动与文本关联，却未显式去除相机诱导运动，导致运动语义匹配不可靠。

Method: 1) 在driver-view视频中估计帧间相机运动（相机运动补偿）；2) 由原始光流/轨迹减去相机诱导分量，得到残差运动（物体真实运动）；3) 将残差运动特征与查询语言表达进行运动特性/运动匹配程度比对；4) 将运动匹配得分与宿主RMOT方法的预测结果通过late fusion整合，模块保持即插即用（plug-in）形式。

Result: 在多个不同RMOT基座（host）上接入运动补偿模块并进行评测，验证了性能增益；实验以跨方法一致提升的形式证明该模块作为插件对不同RMOT主机具有通用有效性。

Conclusion: 显式消除相机诱导运动、仅以残差运动与文本做匹配，是提升RMOT运动语义理解的有效且通用策略；运动补偿可设计为轻量插件，无需改动宿主框架即可获得稳定增益，为语言引导跟踪中的运动线索建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Referring multi-object tracking (RMOT) takes a video and a language expression as input and tracks all referred objects. Many tracking requirements involve how an object moves rather than how it appears. However, in a video captured by a moving camera, a parked vehicle may appear to move, while a moving vehicle may show little displacement. Existing RMOT methods relate motion with text but do not explicitly remove camera-induced motion. In this paper, we propose extracting residual motion across frames by estimating camera motion in driver-view videos and compare motion characteristics with the query expression. We consider the motion-matching extent and integrate it with the RMOT method's prediction result through late fusion. In the evaluation, we verify the performance gain of taking the motion compensation module as a plug-in across different RMOT hosts.

</details>


### [36] [CellSplat4D: PSF-Aware 4D Gaussian Splatting for Sparse Robotic Live-Cell Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04199)
*Yingda Tao,Guoyu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将稀疏采样的4D显微成像问题转化为连续时间的4D高斯blob重建：从硬件实际获取的少量体积中拟合携带位置、形状与寿命的发光blob云，从而按需渲染任意缺失时刻的体积，解耦"分析频率"与"可负担采样频率"，并在两个Cell Tracking Challenge序列上实现最高的细胞核保真度与最平坦的跨间隔衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人显微镜观察活细胞时面临光子预算与时间预算的硬约束：采得越密，对样本的光毒性越大、对其它孔位占用的时间越多。稀疏采样造成的损失并非分析软件的问题，而是采集时序的缺陷——它恰好打断了"哪个细胞在相邻体积中是同一个、哪个细胞分裂成了哪两个"这一身份追踪记录。因此需要一种方法在采集计划层面之外补全缺失的时间点，而不是仅依赖下游追踪算法。

Method: 将每个细胞核建模为4D高斯blob云，每个blob具有位置、形状（协方差）与寿命（lifetime），时间上连续，可按需渲染任意缺失体积。关键设计有三点：(1) 从数据本身测量显微镜点扩散函数（PSF），而非采用采集元数据（会高估模糊）或交给优化器（在"较小blob+更宽模糊"与"较大blob+窄模糊"之间存在歧义，无法恢复）；(2) 每个blob的寿命以"帧"而非录制总时长的比例存储，保证在不同长度序列上代表固定物理时长；(3) 对未测时间步，用3D U-Net在粗尺度上进行监督，直接预测中间体积而不估计运动场——因为细胞分裂是"一个变两个"，任何运动场都无法描述这一事件。

Result: 在两个Cell Tracking Challenge序列（线虫C. elegans胚胎与CHO细胞）上，以逐个细胞核的保真度计分：该重建方法在与任一已获取帧的任意时间距离上都保持最高的细胞核保真度，在间隔内的衰减曲线最平坦，并且对从未被采样过的焦平面也能以平滑退化的方式最好地恢复。

Conclusion: 稀疏采样导致的追踪断点应在采集时序层面通过连续时间重建来弥补，而非寄望于事后分析软件。4D高斯blob表示配合从数据估计的PSF、以帧为单位的寿命编码以及无运动场的U-Net粗尺度监督，可在极稀疏的显微体积序列上高保真地重建细胞核身份与分裂事件，为长时程、低光毒性的活细胞成像提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: A robotic microscope watching living cells cannot afford to look as often as it would like. Every volume it acquires costs photons the specimen does not get back, and time owed to other wells. What such a platform exists to produce is a record of individual cells through time: which cell is which from one volume to the next, and which cell divided into which two. Sampling sparsely breaks that record exactly where it matters, and the fault lies in the acquisition schedule rather than in the analysis software. We fill the gaps by reconstructing them, fitting a 4D Gaussian model to whatever volumes the hardware could afford. The model is a cloud of light-emitting blobs, each carrying a position, a shape and a lifetime. Being continuous in time, it renders any missing volume on demand, decoupling how often the robot analyses from how often it can afford to look. The microscope's point-spread function is measured from the data rather than inherited from acquisition metadata or left to the optimizer, because metadata inflates it and the optimizer cannot recover it at all: a wider blur around a smaller blob fits the images equally well. Each blob's lifetime is stored in frames rather than as a fraction of the recording, so that it denotes a fixed duration on any sequence. Unmeasured timesteps are supervised at coarse scale by a 3D U-Net that predicts the intermediate volume directly and estimates no motion field, since a dividing cell becomes two and no motion describes that. On two Cell Tracking Challenge sequences, a C. elegans embryo and a Chinese Hamster Ovarian (CHO), with fidelity scored per cell nucleus, our reconstruction holds the highest nucleus fidelity at every distance from an acquired frame, has the flattest decay across the gap, and best recovers focal planes it was never shown with graceful degradation across the gap.

</details>


### [37] [Sparse-GS2Mesh: 3D Gaussian Splatting Guided by Novel Stereo Views and 2DGS for Sparse View Surface Reconstruction}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04203)
*Younghyun Noh,Minje Kim,Tae-Kyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sparse-GS2Mesh: 结合极线深度先验初始化3DGS与立体匹配网络微调（自适应基线选择、深度梯度回传、2D/3D协同正则），在稀疏视角下实现表面重建，little-overlap场景较SOTA提升约15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角下几何线索有限：SDF体渲染过平滑，3DGS虽高效但缺乏可靠深度监督且仅从输入视角优化，导致几何不完整；已有3DGS+立体匹配方案仅适用于稠密视角。

Method: 用极线深度先验初始化3DGS（缓解过拟合）；(I) 自适应基线选择为每个稀疏视角确定合成基线；(II) 用立体匹配网络回传深度精化梯度微调3DGS；(III) 2D/3D协同正则稳定重建，应对近距立体视角几何线索弱的问题。

Result: little-overlap设置下较SOTA提升15%，large-overlap设置下性能相当；代码将公开。

Conclusion: 将立体匹配与3DGS结合并扩展至稀疏视角，是提升稀疏视角表面重建几何完整性的有效路径。

Abstract: Surface reconstruction under sparse-view settings remains challenging due to limited geometric cues. Volume rendering methods based on signed distance functions often produce over-smoothed surfaces, while 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), though time-efficient, suffers from incomplete geometry due to the lack of reliable depth supervision and the limitation of being optimized only from given input views. In this paper, we present Sparse-GS2Mesh, a stereo-aware framework for surface reconstruction from sparse views. While 3DGS and stereo matching have been leveraged for surface reconstruction under dense view settings, we extend them to operate effectively under sparse view conditions by first initializing 3DGS using epipolar depth priors to mitigate the 3DGS overfitting problem, followed by our three key components: (I) adaptive baseline selection, (II) fine-tuning with a stereo matching network, and (III) 2D/3D co-regularized fine-tuning. Given a warmed-up 3DGS initialized with epipolar depth, the adaptive baseline selection automatically determines a baseline to synthesize for each sparse view. We then fine-tune 3DGS by backpropagating depth-refining gradients from the stereo matching network, effectively specializing the 3DGS for stereo matching. The 2D/3D co-regularization further helps obtain stable reconstruction, addressing weak geometric cues in close stereo views. Sparse-GS2Mesh achieves a 15\% improvement over state-of-the-art methods in little-overlap settings and comparable results in large-overlap settings. Codes will be publicly available.

</details>


### [38] [FlashGaze: Training-Free Multi-Scale Patch Pruning For Efficient Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04225)
*Ziye Zhu,Yanghao Zhou,Lixing Tan,Jialiang Kang,Shuxuan Li,Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashGaze：一种免训练方法，在ViT编码前通过四叉树动态规划联合优化patch丢弃、合并与保留，以减少长高分辨率视频的时空冗余，在保持精度同时大幅提速降显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在视频理解上表现强，但长高分辨率视频处理效率低。视频存在大量时空冗余，现有方法多在ViT编码中或编码后剪枝，无法解决编码开销；少数编码前剪枝方法依赖可学习辅助网络，带来额外训练与推理开销。

Method: 提出FlashGaze，免训练、无辅助网络的编码前视觉token缩减方法。以像素空间差异作为信息损失代理，采用Quadtree Dynamic Programming在固定预算下联合优化patch的丢弃、合并与保留三种决策。

Result: 在两个MLLM骨干、多个基准上验证：Qwen3-VL-8B上LongVideoBench保留98%全输入基线精度，ViT编码提速最高5.4x、MLLM prefill提速17x，峰值GPU显存降为1/1.8；同硬件下可处理更多帧数与更高分辨率。

Conclusion: 编码前、免训练、无辅助网络的空间冗余削减可有效提升长视频MLLM推理效率并保持精度，扩展了同等硬件下可处理的视频规模。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong performance in video understanding, yet efficiently processing long, high-resolution videos remains challenging. Such videos often contain substantial spatiotemporal redundancy, and processing redundant visual tokens can incur avoidable computational overhead. Many existing methods prune visual tokens during or after vision transformer (ViT) encoding, leaving much of the encoding cost unaddressed. Some approaches prune patches before encoding but rely on learned auxiliary networks for patch selection, incurring additional training and inference overhead. To address these limitations, we propose FlashGaze, a training-free method that reduces spatiotemporal redundancy before ViT encoding without introducing auxiliary networks. FlashGaze uses pixel-space differences as a proxy for information loss and employs Quadtree Dynamic Programming to jointly optimize patch dropping, merging, and keeping under a fixed budget. Experiments on two MLLM backbones across multiple benchmarks demonstrate substantial efficiency gains while largely preserving accuracy. On Qwen3-VL-8B, FlashGaze retains 98% of the full-input baseline accuracy on LongVideoBench while achieving up to 5.4x and 17x speedups in ViT encoding and MLLM prefill, respectively, and reducing peak GPU memory usage by a factor of 1.8. These efficiency gains enable the model to process videos with more frames and higher resolutions on the same GPU hardware, unlocking video understanding at scales previously out of reach.

</details>


### [39] [OctMesh: A Unified Octree-Hierarchical Framework for Lossless Triangle Mesh Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04281)
*Shiyu Feng,Xihua Sheng,Lingyu Zhu,Chunyang Fu,Shiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OctMesh 提出在共享八叉树层级上联合无损编码网格几何与连接关系的可学习框架，将子边按端点父节点类型分组为四类小规模预测任务，结合神经预测器与算术编码，在 MPEG 测试集上取得平均 7.033 bits/face，较 V-Mesh 降低 12.8%，并支持九级渐进式顶点与边细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 八叉树已成功用于学习式点云几何编码与渐进细化，但推广到三角网格时缺乏与之兼容的连接性表示。体素只有 8 个占用决策，而一条父边可衍生出多种子连接模式，候选数量与形态不固定，难以用紧凑的先验建模，因此需要新的边细化表示与预测方式。

Method: 核心观察是八叉树池化产生的父节点只有两种形态：仅含 1 个子节点，或含 2–8 个子节点。据此将子边按端点父节点类型及是否共享父节点分组，每组候选只涉及一个父节点或两个相连父节点，形成四类形状固定的小规模预测任务。父图唯一确定的连接无需比特直接继承，其余三类候选由神经预测器估计概率并驱动算术编码。父内连接的二值化预测为父间连接预测提供上下文；图感知父特征提取器融合局部几何、父连接性与全局形状；连接模型在粗层级使用独立权重、细层级共享权重；残差边与最细层级的面选择载荷完成重建。

Result: 在 8 个 MPEG V-DMC 测试序列的 256 帧上，OctMesh 无损还原最细层顶点坐标、边与无向面集合，平均 7.033 bits/face，较 V-Mesh 降低 12.8%；同一层级表示支持九级渐进式顶点与边细化。

Conclusion: 通过把连接性建模约束在父节点形态决定的固定形状候选组上，八叉树层级可以同时承载几何与连接性的学习式无损编码，并天然支持渐进式细化，为动态网格压缩提供了统一表示。

Abstract: Lossless triangle mesh compression must preserve both vertex positions and connectivity. Octrees support learned point cloud geometry coding and progressive refinement, but extending them to meshes requires a compatible connectivity representation. Unlike the eight occupancy decisions of a voxel, a parent edge can develop into varied child connections, making edge refinement difficult to model with a compact prediction prior. We propose OctMesh, a learned framework that codes geometry and connectivity on a shared octree hierarchy. Its key observation is that octree pooling produces parents with either one child or two to eight children. Child edges are then grouped by their endpoint parents' types and whether the endpoints share a parent. Each candidate group contains children from just one parent or two connected parents. The resulting four categories define small, fixed-shape prediction tasks: connections uniquely determined by the parent graph are inherited without bits, while three neural predictors estimate probabilities for the remaining candidates. These probabilities guide arithmetic coding of the actual edge symbols. Binarized predictions of within-parent connections provide context for predicting connections between different parents. A graph-aware parent feature extractor combines local geometry, parent connectivity and global shape. The connectivity models use dedicated weights at coarse levels and share weights at fine levels. Residual edges and a finest-level face-selection payload complete the reconstruction. On 256 frames from eight MPEG V-DMC test sequences, OctMesh losslessly recovers the finest-level vertex-coordinate, edge and unoriented face sets at an average of 7.033 bits per face, 12.8% below V-Mesh. The same hierarchical representation supports nine levels of progressive vertex-and-edge refinement.

</details>


### [40] [A Geometric-Transformation Feature-Adaptive Manifold Restoration Method for Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation of Remote Sensing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04300)
*Jianzheng Wang,Huan Ni,Xiaonan Niu,Danfeng Hong,Haiyan Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于二面体群几何变换的特征自适应流形修复方法，通过多尺度谐波引导D4视图选择、原视图锚定自适应流形修复和SAM3像素解码器测试时适应，提升遥感开放词汇语义分割的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像中目标的语义信息对二面体群D4的几何变换通常具有不变性，但基于SAM3的开放词汇语义分割方法对不同几何变换的响应往往不一致，导致预测不稳定。作者希望利用这一D4不变性先验来增强模型的稳健性。

Method: 1) 多尺度谐波引导D4视图选择（MH-D4VS）：从几何变换视图集合中挑选互补的候选视图；2) 原视图锚定自适应流形修复（OAMR）：以原视图为锚点，利用可靠的跨视图信息选择性地修复局部不可靠的视觉特征；3) 像素解码器测试时适应（PD-TTA）：推理时仅在线微调SAM3中GroupNorm层参数，以适应样本级分布漂移。

Result: 在八个遥感语义分割基准上平均mIoU达55.6%，在不同基于SAM3的推理框架下均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过D4几何先验结合视图选择、流形特征修复与轻量测试时适应，可有效缓解SAM3在遥感开放词汇分割中的变换响应不一致问题，且方法具有跨框架的通用性与即插即用特性。

Abstract: The semantic information of objects in remote sensing images is typically invariant to geometric transformations from the dihedral group D4. However, SAM3-based open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) methods often exhibit inconsistent responses to different geometric transformations. To exploit this property and improve the stability of OVSS for remote sensing images, we propose a feature-adaptive manifold repair method based on dihedral-group geometric transformations. First, we introduce multi-scale harmonic-guided D4 view selection (MH-D4VS) to select complementary candidate views from a set of geometrically transformed views. Next, we propose original-view-anchored adaptive manifold repair (OAMR), which uses the original view as an anchor and reliable cross-view information to selectively repair locally unreliable visual features. Finally, we develop pixel decoder test-time adaptation (PD-TTA) for SAM3, which fine-tunes only the parameters of the GroupNorm layers online during inference, thereby enhancing the model's ability to adapt to sample-level distribution shifts. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves an average mIoU of 55.6% across eight remote sensing semantic segmentation benchmarks and delivers consistent performance improvements under different SAM3-based inference frameworks.

</details>


### [41] [Rethinking Long-Video Efficiency: A Joint Allocation Perspective on Frames, Pixels, and Front-End Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04318)
*Sixun Dong,Wei Li,Andong Deng,Qi Qian,Victor Zhu,Zhengping Ji,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出LoHi，一个免训练、单次前向的长视频理解框架，通过"稠密低分辨率视频流 + 稀疏高分辨率图像帧"经VLM原生video/image双路径融合，并给出分辨率-时间覆盖权衡的实证结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频VLM理解通常在固定原生分辨率下做帧或视觉token选择，忽略"每帧分辨率可与时间覆盖度互换"这一维度；且前端解码延迟取决于候选池大小而非最终token预算，时钟级瓶颈未被充分研究。

Method: 三项实证发现：(1)相同token预算下稠密低分辨率采样优于稀疏原生分辨率采样；(2)分辨率敏感任务需少量高分辨率帧；(3)小时级视频墙钟时间由前端解码主导。据此设计LoHi：稠密低分辨率流走video路径，稀疏高分辨率帧走image路径。LoHi-Anchor利用编解码I帧元数据选取高分辨率帧；LoHi-SemDiv基于CLIP特征上的查询相关性与视觉多样性选取。

Result: 在三个长视频基准上，等token预算下平均准确率较原生分辨率基线提升10.6个百分点，较最强先前效率方法提升5.2个百分点；小时级视频前端解码延迟最多降低7倍。

Conclusion: LoHi以零训练、单次前向方式实现分辨率与时间覆盖的联合优化，证明该权衡是长视频VLM高效理解的有效且通用维度，并显著改善精度-延迟帕累托前沿。

Abstract: Efficient long-video understanding with vision-language models (VLMs) is often framed as selecting informative frames or visual tokens at a fixed native resolution. We show that per-frame resolution can instead be traded for denser temporal coverage, while front-end decoding latency depends on the size of the candidate pool rather than the final token budget. An empirical study across multiple VLMs and long-video benchmarks yields three findings: dense low-resolution sampling outperforms sparse native-resolution sampling at matched token budgets; resolution-sensitive tasks benefit from selected high-resolution frames; and front-end decoding dominates wall time for hour-long videos. Motivated by these findings, we introduce LoHi, a training-free, single-pass framework that combines a dense low-resolution video stream with sparse high-resolution image frames through the VLM's native video and image pathways. LoHi-Anchor selects high-resolution frames using codec I-frame metadata, while LoHi-SemDiv uses query relevance and visual diversity over CLIP features. Across three long-video benchmarks, LoHi improves average accuracy by 10.6 percentage points over the native-resolution baseline at a matched token budget and by 5.2 percentage points over the strongest prior efficiency method. It also reduces front-end decoding latency by up to 7x on hour-long videos. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [42] [Trust the View That Sees the Target: Mining Cross-View Conflicts for Reliability-Gated Disaster Damage Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04327)
*Yifan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究双视图建筑损毁评估中的"冲突样本"（单视图模型预测不一致），发现理想化"选对视图"的oracle显著优于所有融合方法（0.37-0.41准确率），并提出可见性条件的可靠性门控模型部分恢复该差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有建筑损毁评估方法对称地融合俯视瓦片与地面照片，默认两视图对每栋建筑同等可靠；但在两独立单视图模型预测冲突的样本上，该假设失效，作者希望揭示并量化这一系统性缺陷。

Method: 从三个配对数据集中挖掘冲突样本（占10-33%）：2025 Eaton野火巡检照片和Hurricanes Ian、Milton街景全景，各匹配超高分辨率俯视瓦片。提出"可见性条件可靠性门控"：用建筑可见性特征、逐视图校准置信度与分歧本身构成线性模型来决定信任哪个视图；并做控制性视场（FOV）实验，以及无标签瓦片级损毁预测。

Result: oracle比所有测试的融合方法在冲突样本上高0.37-0.41准确率，且该差距在更长训练、校准和骨干更换后依然存在。门控在野火数据上是唯一显著优于校准概率平均（+0.051，p=0.0001）与端到端融合（+0.072，p<1e-4）的方法，在全景数据上持平。裁剪全景朝向建筑使融合收益翻倍，而相同尺寸随机裁剪无此效果。冲突空间密度可无标签预测瓦片级损毁（Spearman r=0.615，p=0.001）。

Conclusion: 冲突挖掘将"融合是否有用"转化为"在何处、为何、应信任哪个视图"；对称融合存在系统性缺陷，可见性条件可靠性门控提供了部分可解释的修复方案。

Abstract: After a disaster, building damage is assessed from overhead tiles and ground-level photographs, and most methods fuse the two views symmetrically, trusting both equally for every building. This paper focuses on the samples where that assumption fails: the conflict cases, on which two independently trained single-view models disagree. We mine such cases from three paired collections (inspection photographs from the 2025 Eaton wildfire and street-view panoramas from Hurricanes Ian and Milton, each matched to very-high-resolution overhead tiles), where they make up 10-33% of the data. On these samples an oracle that simply trusts the correct view beats every fusion method we tested by 0.37-0.41 accuracy, and the gap survives longer training, calibration, and backbone changes. We recover part of it with a visibility-conditioned reliability gate: a linear model that decides which view to trust from building-visibility features, calibrated per-view confidences, and the disagreement itself. On the wildfire data the gate is the only method that significantly beats calibrated probability averaging (+0.051 on conflicts, p=0.0001) and end-to-end fusion (+0.072, p<10^-4); on the panoramic datasets it matches them. A controlled field-of-view experiment explains why: cropping panoramas toward the building doubles the benefit of fusion, whereas random crops of the same size do not. Finally, the spatial density of conflicts predicts tile-level damage without labels (Spearman r=0.615, p=0.001). Mining conflicts turns "does fusion help?" into "which view should be trusted, where, and why?".

</details>


### [43] [Synthetic-to-Real ViT-Based Pose Estimation of a Noncooperative UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04335)
*Krishnanujam Srinivas,Hanish Acharla,Brij Agrawal,Leonardo Herrera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作研究基于DINOv2自监督ViT骨干的无人机单目位姿估计模型的合成到真实（sim-to-real）泛化能力，仅在合成标注数据上训练，在真实数据上评估，并引入位姿歧义感知策略与α-β滤波提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非合作无人机无法预先加装传感器或受控，只能远程基于图像估计其位姿；深度学习方法受限于大规模带有精确位姿标签的真实数据获取成本高、难度大。合成图像可替代，但需克服合成到真实的域差距。

Method: 采用自监督DINOv2骨干的Vision Transformer，仅用带标签合成图像训练；训练与推理阶段引入位姿歧义感知策略，处理三维目标投影到二维图像平面及目标对称性导致的歧义；推理阶段集成α-β滤波器平滑/修正位姿估计。

Result: 在包含77,077张带标注无人机图像的真实数据集上评估。滤波前平均角度误差(MAE)为19.18°，推理时间13.25 ms；滤波后MAE降至8.74°，推理时间13.42 ms。

Conclusion: ViT/DINOv2模型具备一定的合成到真实泛化能力，结合位姿歧义感知与α-β滤波可在几乎不增加推理开销的前提下显著降低角度误差，满足近实时的无人机位姿估计需求。

Abstract: Remote pose estimation of noncooperative Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) from imagery is critical, as they cannot be influenced or instrumented in advance. Deep-learning-based approaches offer a promising solution; however, their development is constrained by the cost and difficulty of acquiring large-scale real-world datasets with accurate pose labels. Synthetic imagery provides an alternative, but models trained on synthetic data must overcome the synthetic-to-real domain gap to generalize to real-world imagery. This work investigates the inherent synthetic-to-real generalization capability of a Vision Transformer (ViT)-based model for monocular UAV pose estimation. The proposed approach employs a self-supervised DINOv2 backbone and is trained exclusively on labeled synthetic imagery while being evaluated on labeled real-world imagery. Pose ambiguity-aware strategies are incorporated during training and inference to address ambiguities arising from the projection of a three-dimensional target onto a two-dimensional image plane and from target symmetries. An $\alpha$-$\beta$ filter is further integrated during inference to improve pose estimations. To assess the model under operational requirements, it is evaluated in terms of Mean Angular Error (MAE) and inference time, both before and after filtering, using a real-world dataset containing 77,077 labeled UAV images. Before filtering, the model achieves an MAE of $19.18^{\circ}$ and an inference time of $13.25$ ms, whereas after filtering, these values are $8.74^{\circ}$ and $13.42$ ms, respectively.

</details>


### [44] [A differentiable Lagrangian-coupled 3D Gaussian Splatting-SPH model for forward simulation and inverse analysis in solid mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04336)
*Tian Xu,Soroush Atashi,Tianju Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可微拉格朗日耦合的3DGS-SPH模型，用于可变形固体的正向仿真与逆向参数辨识，实现从图像观测估计本构与边界参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅等重建技术能恢复物体的外观与几何，但难以超越"看似合理的动画"，缺乏力学可解释性（本构行为、边界条件、参数反演）。作者希望把渲染模型与物理仿真耦合，从图像数据中辨识力学参数。

Method: 先用多视角标定数据集重建3DGS模型；通过包络式流程生成独立的SPH支撑粒子，避免直接把渲染基元当作力学粒子；再用参考构型下的拉格朗日传输将SPH变形映射到高斯的位置与协方差，保持可微计算路径。SPH支持线弹性、超弹性与Kelvin–Voigt粘弹性，以及固定、自由和Robin型边界条件。

Result: 数值实验将SPH响应与有限元结果对比验证，并与以高斯中心作为表面SPH粒子的传统方案比较精度和效率；在梁、桥、肝脏形状算例上完成正向仿真；逆向分析从渲染变形观测中估计本构与边界参数，含噪声情形，验证了基于图像数据进行力学参数辨识的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作将3DGS渲染与SPH固体力学以可微方式耦合，支撑正向仿真与逆向参数辨识，为从视觉数据构建力学可解释的数字模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative world models have increased interest in digital models that reproduce both the appearance of real objects and their response to physical interaction. Three-dimensional reconstruction techniques, including 3D Gaussian Splatting, capture detailed surface geometry and appearance from images and videos. However, extending these representations beyond plausible animation to mechanically interpretable models for constitutive behavior, boundary conditions, and inverse parameter identification remains less explored. In this work, a differentiable Lagrangian-coupled 3DGS-smoothed particle hydrodynamics (SPH) model is proposed for forward simulation and inverse analysis of deformable solids. The observed object is first reconstructed from multi-view calibrated visual dataset as a 3DGS rendering model. An envelope-based procedure then generates an independent SPH support for the solid-mechanics model, avoiding the direct use of rendering primitives as mechanical particles. A reference-configuration Lagrangian transfer maps SPH deformation to Gaussian positions and covariances, thereby coupling the physical model and the image observation model while preserving a differentiable computational path. The SPH formulation supports linear elastic, hyperelastic, and Kelvin--Voigt viscoelastic responses, together with fixed, free, and Robin-type boundary conditions. Numerical studies validate the SPH response against finite-element results, assess accuracy and efficiency against a conventional model using Gaussian centers as surface SPH particles, and demonstrate forward simulations on beam, bridge, and liver-shaped examples. Inverse analyses further estimate constitutive and boundary parameters from rendered deformation observations, including noisy cases, demonstrating the feasibility of the proposed model for mechanics-based parameter identification from image data.

</details>


### [45] [Any-scale Object Detection using Arbitrary-scaled Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04346)
*Kazutoshi Akita,Norimichi Ukita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出任意尺度目标检测框架，通过任意尺度超分对目标图像进行连续重缩放，并预测尺度候选图以抑制独立超分检测带来的大量误检。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多尺度目标检测依赖离散重缩放的外观表征，尺度覆盖受限；而直接对图像施加超分再独立送入检测器会引入大量误检，需要一种机制在连续尺度空间中兼顾尺度覆盖与假阳性抑制。

Method: 采用任意尺度超分辨率生成连续尺度的目标外观；针对每个超分尺度预测尺度候选图（scale proposal map），表示该尺度下适合检测的像素集合，据此筛选各尺度检测结果以抑制假阳性。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果；方法在连续尺度重缩放设定下缓解了朴素超分检测的误检问题，但缺乏实验数据与指标支撑。

Conclusion: 该工作将任意尺度超分引入目标检测，提出以尺度候选图为核心的误检抑制思路，属于检测/超分交叉的方法性探索，但证据与验证细节不足。

Abstract: This paper proposes any-scale object detection using arbitrary-scale super-resolution for continuously rescaling object images, while general multi-scale object detection uses discretely rescaled appearance representations. However, a naive usage of super-resolution produces many false-positive detections if many super-resolution images are independently fed into an object detector. Our method suppresses these false positives by predicting scale proposal maps, each of which represents a set of pixels appropriate for each super-resolution scale.

</details>


### [46] [LoCoSplat: Real-Time Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting with Minimal 3D Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04351)
*Sinan Wang,Jinjin He,Yuchen Sun,Duowen Chen,Shenyifan Lu,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoCoSplat 通过"局部上下文平均"替代重型学习式三维网络，用16维点特征的可微 splatting + 0.14M 参数点式MLP实现前馈3DGS，在RealEstate10K多视角指标全面超越现有方法，并将6视角重建加速到33ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3DGS方法普遍依赖重型学习式三维网络来聚合多视角证据，计算与显存开销大、推理慢。作者观察到高斯是局部基元：深度预测后所需附加属性（尺度、旋转、不透明度）仅取决于每个锚点周围的点云局部邻域，邻域的固定局部平均即可提供，无需重型网络。

Method: LoCoSplat 将点特征的16维线性投影 splat 到一个细网格和一个粗网格，再在每个锚点上把两者读回，经0.14M参数的点式MLP输出高斯属性。整个编码器不含学习式三维网络、无动态稀疏计算，可作为单个fp16 CUDA图执行。

Result: 在RealEstate10K上，于6/12/24视角均超过所有前馈方法（PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS），且优势随视角加密而扩大（24视角较 VolSplat 高 +3.3 PSNR）；零样本迁移到ACID及在ScanNet上微调仍有提升。单张 RTX PRO 6000 GPU 上6视角场景重建33ms，为七种前馈方法中最快，比此前SOTA快4.2倍；训练快2.7倍（24视角时5.9倍），推理显存少6.7倍。

Conclusion: 以"高斯为局部基元"的归纳偏置替代重型三维网络，可在多视角前馈3DGS中同时获得更高精度与显著更快的推理/训练/更低显存，验证了轻量局部平均聚合路线的有效性。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) increasingly aggregates multi-view evidence with heavy learned 3D networks. We propose LoCoSplat (Local-Context Splatting), motivated by the observation that a Gaussian is a local primitive: once depth is predicted, what the 3D stage must add (scale, rotation, opacity) depends on the point cloud around each anchor, and a fixed local average of that neighbourhood is enough to supply it, no heavy network required. LoCoSplat realises exactly this average: it splats a 16-d linear projection of the point features into a fine and a coarse grid and reads both back at each anchor with a 0.14M-parameter pointwise MLP; with no learned 3D network and no dynamic sparse computation, its whole encoder runs as one fp16 CUDA graph. On RealEstate10K, LoCoSplat outperforms every prior feed-forward method on PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS at 6, 12, and 24 views, with a margin that widens as views densify (+3.3 PSNR over VolSplat, the prior voxel-aligned state of the art, at 24 views) and grows further under zero-shot transfer to ACID and fine-tuning on ScanNet. It reconstructs a 6-view scene in 33 ms on one NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 GPU, the fastest of seven feed-forward methods and $4.2\times$ faster than the previous state of the art, trains $2.7\times$ faster ($5.9\times$ at 24 views), and uses $6.7\times$ less inference memory.

</details>


### [47] [SelectOccFlow: Selective Spatiotemporal Aggregation for 3D Occupancy and Scene Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04356)
*Yuhang Wang,Kai Luo,Yuanfan Zheng,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SelectOccFlow：面向自动驾驶占用与场景流的择优时空聚合框架，含语义引导采样、状态条件时序聚合、范围感知空间聚合，OpenOcc OccScore 44.9 (+4.2%)，nuScenes-C 鲁棒性 +11.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机占位与场景流预测对不可靠的时空聚合敏感，原因有三：图像特征语义不兼容、历史观测未对齐、体素结构不完整。

Method: 提出选择性时空聚合框架，在图像、时序、体素三个域逐步精化上下文证据：(1) Semantic-Guided Sampling 用语义先验调节特征采样以获得语义兼容的图像证据；(2) State-Conditioned Temporal Aggregation 依据体素状态选择性检索历史证据，缓解时序不一致；(3) Extent-Aware Spatial Aggregation 利用方向性结构支撑精化前景几何，提升体素结构完整性。

Result: OpenOcc 上 OccScore 44.9，较此前最优提升 +4.2%；在 Occ3D-nus 上保持有竞争力的占位性能；nuScenes-C 损坏下平均 OccScore 提升 +11.1%，视觉损坏鲁棒性增强。代码将开源。

Conclusion: 通过跨图像-时序-体素的选择性聚合可有效提升相机占位与场景流预测的精度和鲁棒性，但验证限于自动驾驶数据集，未涉及机器人本体实验。

Abstract: Comprehensive 3D scene understanding for autonomous driving requires modeling geometry, semantics, and motion. However, camera-based occupancy and scene flow prediction are sensitive to unreliable spatial and temporal aggregation, caused by semantically incompatible image features, misaligned historical observations, and incomplete voxel structures. To address this issue, we propose SelectOccFlow, a selective spatiotemporal aggregation framework that progressively refines contextual evidence across image, temporal, and voxel domains. To obtain semantically compatible image evidence, we design Semantic-Guided Sampling (SGS) to regulate feature sampling with semantic priors. Since reliable image evidence alone cannot resolve temporal inconsistency, we then present State-Conditioned Temporal Aggregation (SCTA) to selectively retrieve historical evidence according to voxel states. To further enhance the structural completeness of voxel representations, we introduce Extent-Aware Spatial Aggregation (ESA), which exploits directional structural support to refine foreground geometry. Experiments on OpenOcc demonstrate that SelectOccFlow achieves a state-of-the-art OccScore of 44.9, improving the previous best by +4.2%. It also maintains competitive occupancy performance on Occ3D-nus and improves the mean OccScore under nuScenes-C corruptions by +11.1%, demonstrating improved robustness to visual corruptions. The source code will be made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [48] [TRIM-ReID: Duplication-Aware Token Reduction and Modality-Aligned Interaction for Multi-Modal Object Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04361)
*Wanke Xia,Ruiding Zhu,Xingguo Xu,Zhengbo Zhang,Dongxia Liu,Yuan Jin,Taojie Zhu,Yiting Zhao,Yihang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TRIM-ReID 多模态目标重识别框架，统一稠密特征提取、模态内 token 削减与模态间对齐交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态重识别方法沿用面向全局图文对齐的视觉编码器，并以学习到的重要性分数选择 token，既无法保留细粒度身份线索，也未显式处理 token 冗余，导致局部证据被弱化、重复 token 引发噪声且代价高昂的跨模态交互。

Method: 由 DIR（基于 DINOv3）提取语义丰富、空间连贯的稠密 patch 特征；TDM 挖掘互补局部证据，抑制重复 patch 并构建紧凑的模态特定 token 集；MRI 融合保留 token 实现跨模态信息交换；三角对齐损失在独立 token 选择条件下正则化联合关系以维持跨模态语义一致性。

Result: 在 RGBNT201、RGBNT100、MSVR310 三个基准上取得 SOTA 性能（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 通过稠密表示、token 多样性挖掘与对齐交互的组合，可有效缓解局部证据不足与 token 冗余问题，提升 RGB-NIR-TIR 多模态重识别精度。

Abstract: Multi-modal object re-identification exploits complementary RGB, near-infrared (NIR), and thermal-infrared (TIR) observations to retrieve target objects. However, existing methods commonly employ visual encoders optimized for global image-text alignment and select tokens using learned importance scores. Such designs fail to preserve fine-grained identity cues or explicitly account for token redundancy, resulting in underrepresented local evidence and duplicated tokens that lead to noisy and costly cross-modal interaction. To address this gap, we propose TRIM-ReID, a compact framework that unifies dense feature extraction, intra-modal token reduction, and inter-modal aligned interaction. Specifically, semantically rich and spatially coherent patch features are extracted by Dense Identity Representation (DIR), which leverages DINOv3 to preserve fine-grained identity information. We then introduce Token Diversity Mining (TDM) to identify complementary local evidence and construct compact modality-specific token sets by suppressing repetitive patches while preserving informative diversity. Retained tokens are subsequently fused by Modal Relational Interaction (MRI) to enable effective information exchange across modalities, while a triangular alignment loss explicitly regularizes their joint relationships to maintain cross-modal semantic consistency under independent token selection. Extensive experiments on RGBNT201, RGBNT100, and MSVR310 demonstrate that TRIM-ReID achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [49] [Beyond Plausibility: Verifiable Fine-Grained Image Editing on Structured Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04381)
*Muyao Wang,Chen Zhu,Shiqi Yang,DongHyun Hwan,Hideki Nakayama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VeriEdit-Bench：基于 153 个 SVG 源代码（图形、图表、网页界面、幻灯片）编译的 1,740 条精细图像编辑评测，通过受控源代码编辑获得精确目标图、像素级编辑掩码与显式编辑规范，实现四轴确定性打分（编辑保真度、保持性、定位、幅度），并揭示现有编辑器与评测体系的能力与失效盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有精细图像编辑评测在"真实感"与"可验证性"之间存在缺口：基于真实图像的基准依赖人工或 VLM 主观判断，可重复性差、不确定性高；而确定性评测多局限于合成形状画板，应用型扩展又基本限于图表。因此难以精确量化编辑执行了多少、哪里发生了非预期改动，以及模型间微小差异是真实能力差别还是评测噪声。

Method: 构建 VeriEdit-Bench：从 153 个 SVG 资产的源代码出发（图形、图表、网页界面、演示幻灯片），通过受控源代码编辑生成 1,740 个评测用例。每条用例同时给出精确目标图像、像素级编辑掩码与显式编辑描述，从而在真实结构化视觉语境下实现确定性、可复现的四轴评分：编辑保真度、内容保持、定位精度、变更幅度。对 11 个编辑模型进行评测与横向对比分析。

Result: 即使最强模型也远未达到满分；同一"重新着色"操作在图表与 SVG 图形之间的模型排名发生反转；具有相似像素级准确率画像的输出，在定位与变更幅度上仍可能显著不同。四轴分解可给出分级、可验证的反馈，并暴露整体分数或依赖评测者判断的方式所掩盖的模型特异性能力与失效模式。

Conclusion: 将评测从主观判断转向基于源代码的确定性四轴度量，可在真实结构化资产上实现可复现、细粒度的编辑能力诊断；结果说明单一整体指标易掩盖模型差异，按轴分解的验证性反馈对精细图像编辑研究更具指导意义。该工作为基准/数据集类贡献，方法学价值在于评测框架而非新的编辑算法。

Abstract: Fine-grained image editing requires more than producing a visually plausible result: an editor must execute the requested attribute change precisely while leaving everything else intact. However, existing benchmarks leave a critical gap between realism and verifiability: benchmarks built on realistic images typically rely on human or vision--language model judgments, while deterministic evaluation has largely focused on synthetic shape canvases, with application-oriented extensions primarily limited to charts. This makes it difficult to determine precisely how much of a requested edit was executed, where unintended changes occurred, and whether small differences between models reflect genuine editing capability or evaluator uncertainty. To bridge this gap, we present VeriEdit-Bench, a benchmark for fine-grained, instruction-faithful image editing across realistic structured assets with deterministic, four-axis evaluation. Its 1,740 cases are compiled from the source code of 153 Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) graphics, charts, web interfaces, and presentation slides. Controlled source-code edits preserve the original visual context while yielding exact target images, pixel-level edit masks, and explicit edit specifications, enabling reproducible scoring along four axes: edit fidelity, preservation, localization, and magnitude. Evaluating eleven editors, we find that even the strongest model remains far from full credit; rankings for the same recoloring operation reverse between charts and SVG graphics; and outputs with similar pixel-accuracy profiles can still differ substantially in localization and change magnitude. This decomposition yields graded, verifiable feedback and exposes model-specific capability and failure profiles that holistic scores or evaluator-dependent judgments may obscure.

</details>


### [50] [Detecting Defects that Matter: An Application-Driven Benchmark for Anomaly Detection in Manufacturing and Retail Logistics (VAND 4.0 Challenge)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04392)
*Lars Heckler-Kram,Dorian Henning,Ashwin Vaidya,Jan-Hendrik Neudeck,Ulla Scheler,Anton Milan,Samet Akcay,Paula Ramos,Sebastian Höfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VAND 4.0 挑战赛提出跨工业制造(MVTec AD 2)与零售物流(Kaputt 2)的隐藏测试、应用驱动异常检测基准,并引入效率指标与低发生率零售数据集 Kaputt-Rare。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测基准已饱和且不贴近真实部署场景,缺少对分布漂移鲁棒性、稀有缺陷、以及算力效率等实际部署关键维度的评估。

Method: 以隐藏测试集方式构建两个赛道的统一基准:工业赛道 MVTec AD 2(无监督异常分割、零样本方法、分布漂移鲁棒性、DINOv3 骨干对比);零售赛道 Kaputt 2(监督缺陷检测、现成 VLM 对比、参考图有效性、稀有缺陷分析)并新发布低发生率数据集 Kaputt-Rare;同时提出结合性能、吞吐、内存与功耗的效率度量指标。

Result: 工业赛道最佳常规方法像素级 SegF1 仅约 57%,零样本方法低约 15 点,DINOv3 骨干明显占优;零售赛道常见缺陷检测接近饱和,最佳现成 VLM 落后专用模型约 28 AP,参考图无帮助,稀疏溢出约 53 AP、缺件约 27 AP;顶尖方法普遍依赖重型架构,效率被忽视。

Conclusion: 社区需推进两方面工作:(a) 更注重效率的方法设计;(b) 面向稀有缺陷与分布漂移的真正异常检测方法。该工作定位为基准/数据集驱动的方向指引。

Abstract: Existing Anomaly Detection benchmarks are saturated and often unrealistic. As part of the VAND 4.0 Challenge, we introduce a hidden-test, application-driven benchmark across two deployment-critical domains: industrial manufacturing and retail logistics. In the Industrial Track (MVTec AD 2), the results reveal that unsupervised anomaly segmentation remains challenging: the best regular-setting method achieves only ~57\% pixel-level $SegF_1$, indicating substantial room for improvement. Zero-shot approaches trail by ~15 $SegF_1$ points, confirming that task-specific training on normal data remains essential for precise defect localization. Robustness to distribution shifts remains a key open challenge and DINOv3-backbones clearly dominate this track. In the Retail Track (Kaputt 2), the results reveal that (1) supervised defect detection is approaching saturation for common defect types; (2) the best off-the-shelf VLM approach trails specialized models by ~28 AP, confirming that currently VLMs cannot replace fine-tuned detectors, (3) reference images did not prove helpful for top-performing approaches. Performance collapses on rare defects (spillage ~53 AP, missing units ~27 AP), where the supervised ceiling is bounded by data availability. To drive future progress in this domain, we provide a new low-prevalence retail AD dataset (Kaputt-Rare). Across both tracks, computational efficiency is assessed as a first-class metric combining performance, throughput, memory, and power consumption. We introduce a novel metric for measuring efficiency and reveal that that top-performing methods rely on heavy architectures while efficiency is largely neglected. Overall, we conclude that the community needs (a) more efficiency-aware method development, and (b) true anomaly detection approaches for rare defects and shifting conditions. this https URL

</details>


### [51] [AgroGround: Multi-Granularity Grounded Recognition in Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04425)
*Abdulla Alshehhi,Zongyan Han,Rao Anwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建 AgroGround：将 8 个农业 VQA 数据集标签自动转为病害病灶与整体目标定位标注，得 794,850 条指令样本；联合"识别+定位"指令微调 VLM，并加入健康图像负监督，联合准确率由 19.2% 升至 43.3%，健康图弃答率达 95.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业视觉模型通常只被单独评估识别或定位能力，而可靠诊断需要同时判断"是什么"与"证据在哪里"。现有农业 VQA 数据虽有丰富语义标签，却极少与图像区域关联，而人工标注区域代价高昂、难以规模化。因此需要一个可规模化构建的"接地农业识别"数据集与评测基准。

Method: 1) 自动化流水线：把 8 个农业 VQA 数据集的标签转换为病灶级与整体目标级定位标注，生成 794,850 条指令样本；2) 健康图像作为负监督，训练模型对病害查询返回空预测；3) 微调共享视觉-语言模型，混合"已知目标接地"指令与"识别+定位"联合指令两种格式；4) 使用强化学习提升病灶级接地质量；5) 在 1,480 张人工核验、与全部训练数据不相交的图像上评估预测身份、区域、联合正确性与健康图弃答。

Result: 仅做接地微调会使识别准确率从 51.8% 降至 29.1%；加入"识别+定位"指令后回升至 72.6%。在图像与标注固定条件下，两种格式混用使联合准确率从 19.2% 提升到 43.3%，接地性能相当。健康负样本将健康图像弃答率提升至 95.0%，强化学习进一步改善病灶级接地。所得 2B 模型在本基准及外部 PlantSeg 测试集上的接地 F1 均超过其标注教师模型。

Conclusion: AgroGround 建立了接地农业识别的基准，同时衡量身份与定位的联合正确性以及健康图像上的弃答能力，并表明识别与定位指令的混合训练、健康负监督对联合诊断性能是必要的。代码已开源。

Abstract: Agricultural visual models are typically evaluated for either recognition or localization, but reliable diagnosis requires identifying what is present and localizing the evidence. Agricultural visual question answering (VQA) datasets carry rich semantic labels but rarely link them to image regions, and adding such annotations by hand is costly at scale. We introduce AgroGround, a large-scale dataset for grounded agricultural recognition: identifying plant diseases and other agricultural targets and localizing their image regions. An automated pipeline converts the labels of eight agricultural VQA datasets into annotations for disease lesions and whole objects, producing 794,850 instruction examples. Healthy images provide negative supervision for disease queries, teaching the model to return empty predictions. We fine-tune a shared vision-language model on known-target grounding instructions combined with instructions requiring both recognition and localization. We evaluate predicted identities, regions, joint correctness, and healthy-image abstention on 1,480 human-verified images disjoint from all training data. Grounding-only fine-tuning reduces recognition accuracy from 51.8\% to 29.1\%, while adding recognition-and-localization instructions raises it to 72.6\%. With images and annotations held fixed, combining the two formats raises joint accuracy from 19.2\% to 43.3\% at comparable grounding. Healthy negatives raise abstention on healthy images to 95.0\%, and reinforcement learning improves lesion-level grounding. The resulting 2B model exceeds its annotation teacher in grounding F1 on our benchmark and on the external PlantSeg test set. AgroGround establishes a benchmark for grounded agricultural recognition, measuring joint correctness of identity and localization along with abstention on healthy images. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [52] [Video2World: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Interactive World Modeling from Embodied Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04432)
*Jinzhou Tang,Zijun Zhang,Jing Yang,Yuchen Yan,Kun Zhou,Lingjun Mao,Ruobing Han,Jinglin Cao,Wenpeng Xu,Lukun He,Minghao Fu,Fan Feng,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Video2World基准（222个重建实例，源自189个机器人/人演示视频），将"自主视频到仿真"形式化为软件工程任务，评估9个前沿编码智能体在几何保真、动态保真与功能正确性上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从真实观测构建交互式仿真器是扩展具身数据的重要途径，但现有流程仍依赖大量人工环境搭建与标定。作者希望探究前沿基础模型与编码智能体能否端到端自动化这一过程。

Method: 将autonomous video-to-simulation定义为软件工程任务：智能体观察具身视频，构建对应的仿真环境与机器人行为，并通过执行反馈迭代精化。构建Video2World基准，含222个重建实例（来自189个机器人与人类演示视频），从几何保真、动态保真、功能正确性三方面评测，覆盖空间感知、物理推理与可执行交互；对9个前沿编码智能体系统进行评估。

Result: 从Claude Opus 5开始任务成功率出现明显跃升，从不足5%升至超过15%，但与人类辅助重建之间仍存在显著差距；同时发现视觉保真度更好并不必然带来更高任务成功率，"看起来更好"的世界可能"用起来更差"，呼应生成模型中感知真实性与事实正确性之间的差距。

Conclusion: 编码智能体已能初步自动化视频到仿真的重建，但能力仍有限；视觉保真度与功能正确性存在解耦，未来需以可执行交互和物理正确性为优化目标，而非单纯提升渲染真实感。

Abstract: Building interactive simulators from real-world observations is a promising way to scale embodied data, but current pipelines still rely heavily on manual environment construction and calibration. We study whether frontier foundation models and coding agents can automate this process end to end. We formulate \emph{autonomous video-to-simulation} as a software engineering task in which an agent observes an embodied video, constructs the corresponding simulated environment and robot behavior, and iteratively refines the result through execution feedback. To evaluate this capability, we introduce \textbf{Video2World}, a benchmark comprising 222 reconstruction instances derived from 189 robot and human demonstration videos. Video2World measures reconstructed worlds along geometric fidelity, dynamic fidelity, and functional correctness, capturing spatial perception, physical reasoning, and executable interaction. Evaluating 9 frontier coding-agent systems reveals a sharp improvement in Task success beginning with Claude Opus 5, rising from below 5\% to over 15\%, while substantial gaps to human-assisted reconstruction remain. We further find that worlds that look better could work worse: better visual fidelity does not always lead to higher task success. This echoes the broader gap between perceptual realism and factual correctness observed in generative models.

</details>


### [53] [Multi-Crop Leaf Disease Recognition: A Unified Benchmark and Cross-Region Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04456)
*Rosemary Nalwanga,Sebastian Bunda,Godliver Owomugisha,Luuk Spreeuwers,Estefania Talavera Martinez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出MLD多区域作物病害基准（6个公开数据集合并，18种作物、56类、167,427张图像），并设计层级式HiLeaD（先预测作物再预测病害）方法，揭示跨区域域偏移下的严重性能崩塌，指出当前瓶颈在数据覆盖与多样性而非模型设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作物叶片病害识别模型虽常报告近乎完美的准确率，但通常在受控实验室条件下采集的单一数据集上训练与评估，其在真实跨区域域偏移下的行为缺乏系统理解。作者希望通过统一的多区域基准与显式的跨域评估协议来量化这一差距，并检验利用作物—病害层级依赖是否能缓解跨域退化。

Method: 构建MLD统一基准：整合美国、亚洲、非洲的六个公开作物病害数据集，统一为层级分类体系（18种作物、56个作物-病害类、每种作物含一个健康类、共167,427张图像）。定义标准化的单源与多源池化评估协议以显式探测跨区域泛化。提出HiLeaD层级式建模：将病害预测条件化于作物预测之上，利用作物到病害的固有依赖关系。

Result: HiLeaD下，PlantVillage训练的模型域内病害F1达99.07%，但在PlantDoc上骤降至12.88%，暴露严重的跨区域域间差距。在池化MLD上训练可将PlantDoc的跨区域病害F1从12.88%恢复到39.64%（提升26.76个百分点）。层级式建模在MLD数据集上相对平铺基线带来1.71至4.88个百分点的稳定额外增益。

Conclusion: 跨区域泛化性能主要受限于数据覆盖与多样性，而非模型设计；层级式建模虽有稳定增益但幅度有限。该基准与协议为作物病害识别的真实场景鲁棒性评估提供了标准化工具，并指出未来方向应以扩充多区域数据多样性为核心。

Abstract: Deep learning models for crop leaf disease recognition routinely report near-perfect accuracy yet are typically trained and evaluated on a single dataset collected under controlled laboratory conditions, leaving their behavior under realistic cross-region domain shift poorly understood. We introduce MLD (Multi-crop Leaf Disease) dataset, a unified multi-region benchmark that combines six public crop-disease datasets from the USA, Asia, and Africa into a shared hierarchical taxonomy spanning 18 crops, 56 crop-disease classes (including one healthy class per crop) making 167,427 images. We define standardized single-source and pooled multi-source evaluation protocols that explicitly probe cross-region generalization. We also investigate whether exploiting the inherent crop-to-disease dependency via a hierarchical formulation (HiLeaD) that conditions disease prediction on the predicted crop improves recognition under cross-region shift. Under the HiLeaD, the model trained on PlantVillage achieves 99.07% in-domain disease F1 but collapses to 12.88% when tested on PlantDoc, exposing a severe cross-region domain gap. The model trained on the pooled MLD dataset partly recovers cross-region disease F1 from 12.88% to 39.64% on PlantDoc (HiLeaD), achieving a 26.76 percentage point improvement. The hierarchical formulation provides a consistent additional gain, ranging from 1.71 to 4.88 percentage points in disease F1 over the flat baseline under the MLD dataset indicating that progress in this area is currently limited more by data coverage and diversity than by model design.

</details>


### [54] [RPFQ-ViT: Rotated Phase-Frame Quantization for Extremely Low-Bit Weights in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04457)
*Mengyuan Fan,Bokai Huang,JiaMing Pan,Xiaokun Yuan,Peizhuang Cong,Zhewen Tan,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RPFQ-ViT 提出"旋转相位框架量化"，将成对通道映射到二维相位平面并以低比特码保留投影方向、用轻量缩放恢复幅度；作为即插即用 QAT 方案，在 W2/A4~A8 下使 ViT-B/16、Swin-T、DeiT-S 在 ImageNet-1K 保持较高精度，并在 iOS/Android 上实现约 5.4–7.1 倍压缩与 1.4–1.6 倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT 的高维线性投影与注意力计算带来显著存储与推理开销；极低比特量化虽可缓解，但传统实值标量码本与 Transformer 投影的方向性几何不匹配，导致精度严重下降。作者希望用与方向几何对齐的量化表示来提升极低比特下的精度—压缩—部署折衷。

Method: RPFQ-ViT（Rotated Phase-Frame Quantization）：将成对通道放入二维相位平面进行联合量化，低比特码主要编码投影方向，幅度由轻量缩放因子恢复；引入通道配对、可学习旋转、相位锚点学习与残差相位细化四个组件。方法作为 QAT 直接替换，不改动标准实值注意力、归一化与激活计算图。

Result: ImageNet-1K 上：ViT-B/16 在 W2/A4 达 79.33% Top-1 / 94.48% Top-5；Swin-T 在 W2/A8 达 79.30% / 94.79%；DeiT-S 在 W2/A8 达 77.41% / 93.11%（300 epoch 匹配配方，三次独立运行均值）。消融与相位几何、方向保持指标验证各组件有效性。2-bit 打包权重下模型体积较 FP32 缩小约 5.4–7.1 倍，原生 iOS/Android 端到端延迟降低 1.4–1.6 倍。

Conclusion: RPFQ-ViT 在极低比特 ViT 量化中给出精度、压缩率与移动端部署之间较优的折衷，具备实际端侧落地证据，但属于视觉模型压缩方向，与物理 AI/机器人核心议题关联度有限。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong performance on image recognition and mobile vision applications, but their high-dimensional linear projections and attention computations still impose substantial storage and inference costs. Extremely low-bit quantization is a promising solution, yet ViTs often suffer severe accuracy degradation because conventional real-valued scalar codebooks are poorly matched to the directional geometry of Transformer projections. We present RPFQ-ViT, a Rotated Phase-Frame Quantization method that quantizes paired channels in two-dimensional phase planes, enabling low-bit codes to better preserve projection directions while recovering magnitude with lightweight scaling. RPFQ-ViT serves as a drop-in QAT replacement for this http URL and does not modify the standard real-valued attention, normalization, or activation computation graph. On ImageNet-1K, RPFQ-ViT-B/16 reaches 79.33% Top-1 / 94.48% Top-5 under W2/A4, Swin-T reaches 79.30% Top-1 / 94.79% Top-5 under W2/A8, and DeiT-S reaches 77.41% Top-1 / 93.11% Top-5 under W2/A8. Ablations, phase-geometry analysis, and direction-preservation metrics show that channel pairing, learnable rotation, phase-anchor learning, and residual phase refinement each improve quantization quality. We further deploy RPFQ-ViT image-classification models on native iOS and Android runtime stacks; with 2-bit packed weights, model size shrinks by roughly $5.4$-$7.1\times$ relative to FP32 and end-to-end on-device latency drops by $1.4$-$1.6\times$. All ImageNet results trained in our codebase use a matched 300-epoch recipe and are reported as mean accuracies over three independent runs. These results show that RPFQ-ViT provides a favorable trade-off among accuracy, compression, and practical mobile deployment for extremely low-bit ViTs.

</details>


### [55] [Homogeneous Semantic Alignment and Hierarchical Expert Routing for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04499)
*Erjian Zhang,Jiayuan Ma,Liejun Wang,Yikemaiti Sataer,Xiaoming Tao,Zhiqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HSA-HER两阶段框架：先在潜空间施加同质分布约束消除视觉-文本跨模态分布偏移，提取纯化视觉特征作为疾病语义锚点；再以锚点引导分层专家路由，对视觉特征、局部实体、全局检索等多源证据动态激活专家并自适应融合。三个基准数据集达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射报告生成方法在跨模态交互与外部先验引入上已有进展，但底层表示的分布偏移与异构信息的无差别刚性耦合，导致微弱的视觉异常线索在解码时被海量文本先验和生成惯性稀释。

Method: 受认知科学启发提出两阶段框架。(1) 同质语义对齐：在底层潜空间引入显式同质分布约束，消除视觉与文本特征间的跨模态分布偏移，得到与疾病精确对齐的纯化视觉语义锚点。(2) 分层专家路由：以疾病语义锚点引导，对视觉特征、局部实体、全局检索三类异构临床证据动态激活专家网络，进行定向挖掘与语义重构，并自适应分配融合权重，替代无差别刚性耦合。

Result: 在三个主流基准数据集上的大量实验表明HSA-HER达到最优性能，能准确刻画复杂影像细节与关键诊断信息。

Conclusion: 通过分布对齐与锚点引导的专家路由解耦异构多源证据，可缓解视觉弱线索被文本先验稀释的问题，为医学报告生成提供有效范式。

Abstract: Radiology report generation (RRG) aims to convert medical images into diagnostic texts to assist in clinical decision-making and alleviate the workload of physicians. Although existing methods have made extensive progress in cross-modal interaction and the incorporation of external priors, the distribution shift of underlying representations and the undifferentiated rigid coupling of heterogeneous information cause weak visual abnormality cues to be easily diluted by massive text priors and generation inertia during decoding. To overcome this bottleneck, inspired by cognitive science, we propose a novel two-stage Homogeneous Semantic Alignment and Hierarchical Expert Routing (HSA-HER) framework. First, the model introduces an explicit homogeneous distribution constraint in the underlying latent space to effectively eliminate the cross-modal distribution shift between visual and textual features, thereby extracting purified visual features as semantic anchors that accurately align with diseases. Second, for heterogeneous clinical evidence composed of visual features, local entities, and global retrievals, we design a hierarchical expert routing mechanism guided by these disease semantic anchors. This mechanism abandons the undifferentiated rigid coupling paradigm. Specifically, it dynamically activates expert networks to perform targeted mining and semantic reconstruction on multi-source evidence, and adaptively allocates fusion weights. Extensive experiments on three mainstream benchmark datasets demonstrate that HSA-HER achieves state-of-the-art performance, accurately depicting complex imaging details and key diagnostic information.

</details>


### [56] [Localization Lens for Improving Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04502)
*Hasan Farooq,Murtaza Taj,Mehwish Nasim,Arif Mahmood*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向医学视觉语言模型的定位感知增强方案，在数据、架构、对齐三个层面分别引入 localization lens 专家标注表示、像素混洗（pixel shuffle）模块与解耦对比损失（DCL），在 Med-VQA 上最高提升 6.2% 准确率，且模型无关、可即插即用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉语言模型（Med-VLM）在临床任务上表现良好，但对解剖结构与空间定位的理解薄弱，而这是医学推理的关键。作者希望在不改变模型主体的前提下，提升 Med-VLM 的定位与空间推理能力。

Method: 在 Med-VLM 流程中做三层增强：(1) 数据层引入 localization lens——经专家验证的表示集合，为输入提供更丰富的解剖与位置上下文；(2) 架构层因输入复杂度上升而集成 pixel shuffle，对表示进行过滤与精炼，在保持解剖连续性的同时强化空间信息处理；(3) 对齐层在标准损失之外加入解耦对比损失（DCL），以提升特征判别力与鲁棒性，缓解医学数据有限的问题。

Result: 在多个 Med-VQA 数据集上评估，方法在不同 Med-VLM 架构上均改善定位驱动的表现；对定位类问题的分析显示解剖与空间推理的改进直接带来整体 Med-VQA 准确率最高 6.2% 的提升。

Conclusion: 所提方法模型无关，可无缝集成进现有 Med-VLM 流程；数据集、代码与训练模型将公开。属医学领域 CV 工作，与 Physical AI/机器人/3D 重建等核心方向关联弱。

Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Models (Med-VLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in clinical tasks. However, they often struggle to understand anatomical structures and spatial positioning, which are crucial for medical reasoning. To address this, we propose a localization-aware enhancement to the Med-VLM pipeline, introducing improvements at three levels: data,architecture, and alignment. First, we introduce localization lens, a set of expert-validated representations that provide richer anatomical and positional context. However, as these representations increase input complexity, we integrate pixel shuffle within the model architecture to filter and refine representations, enhancing spatial information processing while preserving anatomical continuity. Lastly, to effectively align the localization lens representations with textual features, we incorporate decoupled contrastive loss (DCL) alongside the standard loss function. This ensures better feature discrimination and robustness, particularly in data limited medical settings. Through extensive evaluations on medical visual question answering (Med-VQA) datasets, we show that our methodology improves localization-driven performance across different Med-VLM architectures. Our analysis of localization-based questions further reveals that improvements in anatomy and spatial reasoning directly enhance the overall accuracy of Med-VQA upto 6.2%. The proposed approach is model-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated into existing Med-VLM pipelines. The dataset, code, and trained models will be made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [57] [EgoExo-Next:Benchmarking Vision-Language Models on Visual-Option Next-State and Cross-View Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04506)
*Yutong Li,Molin Wang,Xiaotong Li,Yanyan Fang,Daoguo Dong,Ziyi Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EgoExo-Next 视觉选项基准，用于评测 VLM 在自我中心与跨视角场景下的动态视觉状态推理能力，包含 2,503 道四选一题目与四个互联子任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 视频推理基准多聚焦语义事件理解、时间关系或已观测视角间的对应关系，缺乏对"未来视觉状态"的直接推理评估，导致模型预测动态视觉状态的能力未被充分考察。

Method: 构建 EgoExo-Next 基准：从六个公开自我中心与 ego-exo 视频源中人工整理 2,503 道四选一视觉选项题，设四个子任务——自我中心下一状态预测、双向 ego-exo 状态对应、外中心下一状态预测，以及组合式 Ego-to-Exo 下一状态推理；对商用、开源及空间推理类 VLM 进行系统评测。

Result: 最优模型平均准确率 43.81%，人类为 98.55%，存在显著人机差距；性能下降最大出现在组合式 Ego-to-Exo 任务上。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 在动态视觉状态推理上仍受明显限制，尤其当需要同时组合时间推进与跨视角推理时；基准已公开。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly evaluated for egocentric and cross-view video reasoning, yet existing benchmarks largely focus on semantic event understanding, temporal relations, or correspondence between already observed views, leaving their ability to reason directly about future visual states underexplored. We introduce EgoExo-Next, a visual-option benchmark for dynamic visual-state reasoning, where models must identify how an observed action trajectory subsequently appears rather than predict only an action label or textual description. EgoExo-Next contains 2,503 human-curated four-choice questions from six public egocentric and ego--exo video sources and comprises four interconnected subtasks that evaluate egocentric next-state prediction, bidirectional ego--exo state correspondence, exocentric next-state prediction, and their composition in Ego-to-Exo Next-State. Extensive evaluation of proprietary, open-source, and spatial reasoning VLMs reveals a substantial human--model gap, with the best model achieving 43.81\% average accuracy compared with 98.55\% for humans, and the largest degradation occurring on the composed Ego-to-Exo task. These results suggest that current VLMs remain substantially limited in dynamic visual-state reasoning, particularly when temporal progression and cross-view reasoning must be composed. The benchmark is publicly available at \url{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [58] [TAME:Topology-Aware Text-Driven Motion Editing across Heterogeneous Humanoid Skeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04529)
*Qichen Zheng,Siyuan Yang,Chong Wang,Jun Liu,Shijian Lu,Alex Kot,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAME：一种基于flow-matching transformer的拓扑感知动作编辑方法，用TCSP约束骨骼注意力到一跳邻居、EFRA仅在编辑相关token上做自蒸馏对齐，并构建多拓扑基准TopoMotionFix，在未见骨架上实现可靠编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动动作编辑方法针对单一固定骨骼拓扑，无法适应动画流程中关节数与骨架层级不同的角色，限制了通用性；同时缺乏可训练、可比较的多拓扑评测设定。

Method: 将动作表示为逐关节逐帧token，以flow-matching transformer为主体，通过骨骼、时间、文本三路cross-attention建模交互；用Topology-Constrained Skeletal Propagation（TCSP）将骨骼注意力限制在骨架邻接矩阵的一跳运动学邻居内以适配不同层级；引入Edit-Focused Representation Alignment（EFRA），在编辑相关的关节-时间token上让student特征与更干净的EMA-teacher特征对齐；并构建TopoMotionFix多拓扑数据集与seen/unseen协议。

Result: 在MotionFix上编辑对齐与源动作保持优于既有方法；在TopoMotionFix上能可靠编辑未见拓扑骨架。

Conclusion: 拓扑约束注意力加编辑聚焦表征对齐可让动作编辑摆脱固定骨骼假设，具备跨骨架泛化能力，为通用动画/具身动作管线提供了可行方案与评测基准。

Abstract: Text-driven motion editing modifies an existing motion sequence according to a text instruction while preserving the content of the source motion. Existing methods are typically built for a single, fixed skeletal topology, which limits their use in animation pipelines where characters differ in joint count and skeletal hierarchy. We present Topology-Aware Motion Editor (TAME), a flow-matching transformer that edits motions on humanoid skeletons of varying topology. TAME represents motion as per-joint, per-frame tokens and models interactions among joints, across frames, and with the text instruction through skeletal, temporal, and text cross-attention layers. To make the skeletal attention follow each character's hierarchy, TAME replaces full joint attention with Topology-Constrained Skeletal Propagation (TCSP), which restricts attention to one-hop kinematic neighbors in the skeleton's adjacency matrix. We further introduce Edit-Focused Representation Alignment (EFRA), a self-distilled representation alignment strategy that aligns student features with cleaner EMA-teacher features exclusively on edit-relevant joint-time tokens, making edits faithful to the instruction. To make this setting trainable and comparable, we construct TopoMotionFix, a multi-topology extension of MotionFix with seen- and unseen-topology evaluation protocols. TAME outperforms previous methods in edit alignment and source preservation on MotionFix and reliably edits motions on unseen skeletons in TopoMotionFix.

</details>


### [59] [FASTER: Fast Adjoint Stochastic Transport for Endpoint Refinement in Reward-Guided Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04538)
*Yimiao Zhou,Zejia Zhong,Jingya Wang,Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出理论框架刻画奖励、源保持与先验偏好的联合分布，并据此设计FASTER：为每个源图与目标训练小型编辑网络，预训练与奖励模型仅作离线反馈，从而避免将大模型放入内层优化，实现6.91×–24.14×的编辑加速并在SD3上取得领先指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时奖励引导的图像编辑通常需要反复执行大型预训练生成过程，甚至对主干网络反向传播，导致调整成本极高、交互性差。作者希望将"目标分布"与"实现该分布的动力学"解耦，用廉价网络完成编辑而不牺牲奖励优化与内容保真。

Method: 1) 建立同时包含奖励项、源内容保持项与预训练先验偏好的理论框架，使期望输出分布可与采样动力学分离定义；2) 提出FASTER：针对每个源图和编辑目标训练一个小型编辑网络，预训练生成模型与奖励模型只对候选输出提供反馈（打分/评判），不参与内层梯度回传；3) 通过复用同一候选样本及其反馈进行多次小网络更新，减少重复采样与监督查询次数。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion 3上，FASTER在全部四项目标指标及多项验证指标上优于所评估方法；相比沿预训练生成轨迹优化控制量的基线，编辑阶段加速最高达SD3上6.91×、SD1.5上24.14×。

Conclusion: 将输出分布与实现动力学解耦、用轻量per-source/per-objective网络承担实际编辑，可在保持奖励与内容保真的同时大幅降低测试时编辑成本，为奖励引导编辑提供了高效可扩展的范式，但其适用范围仍限于图像编辑，未涉及三维、机器人或具身交互场景。

Abstract: Reward-guided image editing at test time seeks to improve a specified reward while preserving source content and visual plausibility. Many existing approaches optimize candidates through pretrained generation processes, making repeated adjustment depend on costly large-model execution and, in some cases, backbone backpropagation. We develop a theoretical framework that jointly accounts for reward, source preservation, and pretrained-prior preferences, allowing the desired output distribution to be specified separately from the dynamics used to realize it. Based on this framework, we introduce FASTER, which trains a small network for each source and objective to perform inexpensive editing, while pretrained and reward models provide feedback on candidate outputs. By reusing each candidate and its feedback across multiple small-network updates, FASTER reduces repeated sampling and supervision queries without placing the pretrained generative backbone inside the inner optimization loop. On SD3, FASTER leads all four target metrics and several validation metrics among the evaluated methods. Compared with the evaluated baseline that optimizes controls along pretrained generation trajectories, FASTER achieves editing-time speedups of up to \({6.91\times}\) on Stable Diffusion 3 and \({24.14\times}\) on Stable Diffusion 1.5.

</details>


### [60] [EagleDepth: Efficient Fine-Grained Depth Estimation via Pixel Diffusion Decoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04554)
*Bowen Chai,Tianbao Zhang,Shuyu Wu,Dexin Zuo,Zhaoxin Fan,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EagleDepth：结合潜空间扩散几何先验与像素空间扩散解码的高效高分辨率单目深度估计框架，在5个常用数据集及Synth4K上达到SOTA，推理更快、细节与边界更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率图像中恢复精细几何对感知至关重要，但现有潜空间建模+VAE重建方法会损失几何细节，且从潜码解码带来大量推理开销。

Method: 保留深度感知的潜表示作为引导，直接在像素空间生成深度图。分两阶段顺序训练：先以RGB-深度配对监督微调预训练潜扩散模型；再将预训练像素扩散解码器PiD适配为以潜特征为条件预测深度。像素分支从1024分辨率开始训练并扩展至4K多分辨率；潜分支处理降采样的低分辨率RGB，像素分支在目标分辨率输出深度，绕过原VAE解码器。

Result: 在5个常用深度估计数据集与高分辨率Synth4K上取得SOTA性能，推理更快，细结构与物体边界保持更好。

Conclusion: 潜引导+像素空间扩散解码的双分支设计可在不牺牲效率的前提下保持几何知识并提升高分辨率深度细节，为高效高分辨率单目深度估计提供可行范式。

Abstract: Recovering detailed geometry from high-resolution images is critical for precise perception of the surroundings and objects. However, existing methods which use latent-space modeling and VAE reconstruction can compromise geometric details. Furthermore, decoding from latent codes introduces substantial inference overhead. To address those issues, we present EagleDepth, an efficient framework for high-resolution monocular depth estimation that combines the geometric priors of latent diffusion with fine-grained pixel-space generation. Our key idea is to retain depth-aware latent representations as guidance while generating the final depth map directly in pixel space. We train the latent and pixel components sequentially: first, we fine-tune a pretrained latent diffusion model using paired RGB--depth supervision; then, we adapt a pretrained pixel diffusion decoder, PiD, to predict depth conditioned on the learned features. Training of the pixel component starts at 1024 resolution and continues across multiple resolutions up to 4K. The latent branch processes resized, lower-resolution RGB images, while the pixel branch generates depth at the target resolution, bypassing the original VAE decoder. This design preserves learned geometric knowledge without requiring the latent backbone to operate at the output resolution. On five commonly used depth estimation datasets and the high-resolution Synth4K dataset, our framework achieves state-of-the-art depth estimation performance, with faster inference and better preservation of fine structures and object boundaries.

</details>


### [61] [Frozen in a Frame: The Velocity Blind Spot in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04585)
*Tinghe Zhang,Chunyu Liu,Yu Leon Liu,Zerui Zhao,Jiaheng Chen,Yucheng Xiao,Jiaxing Li,Yunlong Wang,Alex Lamb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出JEPA式世界模型以单帧渲染图为预测目标时存在结构性盲点：单帧嵌入不含速度信息，导致线性速度探针仅达随机水平；作者提出RateIdent三阶段诊断协议与TI-JEPA（将潜变量拆分为姿态码与显式有限差分运动码）作为轻量修复，在多个物理环境中显著提升停止于目标的规划表现，且无特权监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA世界模型从单帧渲染图训练编码器，让预测器把当前嵌入与动作映射到下一帧嵌入。作者认为这存在结构性盲点：无运动模糊的渲染器仅由构型决定画面，因此任何编码器（包括官方发布的LeWM权重）的单帧嵌入都不携带速度信息，需要验证并修复。

Method: 提出RateIdent三阶段诊断协议用于检测单帧嵌入的速度盲点；提出TI-JEPA，将潜空间拆分为姿态码与显式有限差分运动码并联合预测，无特权监督。在官方ViT-Tiny加AdaLN-transformer规模复现，并推广到真实dm_control Reacher照片从零训练，同时与同规模循环RSSM式预测器对比。

Result: 在四个真实基准（PushT、Reacher、Cube、TwoRoom）的官方检查点上，所有线性速度探针处于或低于随机水平，而位置探针R^2约0.95。在三个物理环境中的停止于目标规划任务上，TI-JEPA相对匹配记忆基线取得显著且跨种子稳健的提升：Pendulum最终距离降低55%（p=3.2x10^-10），CartPole降低64%（p=5.1x10^-15）。在真实Reacher照片上其分支分离度超过有记忆基线约38倍，为全文最大差距。对同规模RSSM式预测器，在三个环境中两个的rollout精度持平或更优，姿态与运动可分别探测，并在耦合最强的环境上取胜。

Conclusion: 可检验的形式化论证与六个评估环境表明：当速度重要时，单帧目标是错误的预测对象；一个小的、可解释的结构性改动即可修复，且无需特权监督。代码、检查点与项目页均已提供。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) for world modeling train an encoder so a predictor maps a current embedding and action to the next frame's embedding, always from a single rendered frame. This has a structural blind spot: a renderer without motion blur draws a scene from configuration alone, so a single-frame embedding carries no velocity information, for any encoder, including the official released LeWM weights. We confirm this on official checkpoints across four real benchmarks (PushT, Reacher, Cube, TwoRoom): every linear velocity probe sits at or below chance while position probes reach R^2 about 0.95. We introduce RateIdent, a three-stage diagnostic protocol, and TI-JEPA, a lightweight fix splitting the latent into a pose code and an explicit finite-difference motion code, predicted jointly. Across three physically grounded environments, TI-JEPA gives a significant, seed-robust gain on a stop-at-goal planning task over a matched-memory baseline, e.g. 55% lower final distance on Pendulum (p=3.2x10^-10) and 64% on CartPole (p=5.1x10^-15). We reproduce this at official ViT-Tiny plus AdaLN-transformer scale, then push the same recipe onto real dm_control Reacher photographs trained from scratch, where TI-JEPA's branch separation exceeds the memory-having baseline's by roughly 38x, the paper's largest margin. Against a same-footprint recurrent RSSM-style predictor, TI-JEPA matches or beats its rollout accuracy on two of three environments, stays separately probeable for pose and motion, and wins outright on the most coupled one. A checkable formal argument and six evaluated environments show single-frame targets are the wrong object to predict when velocity matters, and a small, interpretable structural change fixes it with no privileged supervision. Code, checkpoints, and the project page are linked below the title.

</details>


### [62] [WASP: Weakly Aligned Spatiotemporal Pairs for Fetal Brain MRI-Ultrasound Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04601)
*Francesco Correnti,Gabriele Magrini,Marco Mistretta,Niccolò Biondi,Pietro Pala,Alessandro Ramalli,Simona Fiori,Andrew D. Bagdanov,Matteo Lenge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WASP 框架将胎儿 MRI 与超声的跨模态对应建模为熵最优传输问题，仅用临床元数据（孕周与诊断切面）构造弱对齐的时空配对，无需配对数据集即可把 MRI 表征映射进 US 潜空间，显著提升 US 预训练模型的孕周估计与切面分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿 MRI 成像质量优但成本高、难以规模化，超声廉价安全且已有大规模预训练生态，但两者之间存在数据规模不对称。核心障碍是公开数据中不存在受试者级别的成对 MRI-US 数据，导致无法按常规方式训练跨模态基础模型。

Method: 提出 WASP（Weakly Aligned Spatiotemporal Pairs）：以孕周（GA）和诊断切面等临床元数据作为驱动，将跨模态对应关系形式化为熵正则化的最优传输问题，构造弱对齐的时空配对，仅训练一个轻量对齐模块把 MRI 表征提升到 US 潜空间，不微调主干网络。

Result: 在 MRI 未参与预训练的 USFM 主干上增益最大：GA 估计误差由 21.9 天降至 17.4 天，标准切面分类准确率由 61.9% 升至 69.0%；在双模态预训练主干上提供较小的、依赖主干的改进，如 BioMedParse GA 误差 6.5→6.0 天、SAM-Med2d 切面准确率 83.3%→88.1%。

Conclusion: WASP 表明无需成对跨模态数据，仅凭临床元数据驱动的弱对齐即可让纯 US 预训练模型理解胎儿 MRI，且增益在主干未见 MRI 时最大，为跨模态医学基础模型的低成本扩展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is widely regarded as the optimal sensor for fetal brain analysis due to its superior soft-tissue contrast and anatomical detail. However, its high cost and operational burden make it invasive and difficult to obtain at scale. Ultrasound (US), in contrast, is cheap, safe, and routinely acquired, and as a result it has produced substantially larger datasets and a growing ecosystem of pretrained models. This asymmetry raises a natural question: Can we teach a US-only model to understand fetal MRI from only a limited set of examples? The standard recipe, training a foundation model on subject-to-subject paired MRI-US scans, is not viable since no such paired fetal dataset is publicly available. In this paper we address this gap with Weakly Aligned Spatiotemporal Pairs (WASP), a framework that formulates cross-modal correspondence as an entropic Optimal Transport problem driven by clinical metadata, in particular Gestational Age (GA) and diagnostic planes, enabling the fitting of a lightweight alignment module that lifts MRI representations into the US latent space, without fine-tuning the backbone. Empirically, WASP yields its largest gains when MRI is unseen by the model during pretraining (on USFM, GA estimation error drops from 21.9 to 17.4 days and standard plane classification accuracy climbs from 61.9% to 69.0%), while providing smaller, backbone-dependent refinements for backbones pretrained on both modalities (e.g., BioMedParse GA estimation error from 6.5 to 6.0 days and SAM-Med2d plane accuracy from 83.3% to 88.1%). Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [63] [Organize Primitives into Semantic Parts: Reinforcement Reasoning for 3D Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04602)
*Xiaoming Gong,Ruoyu Wu,Zhenhong Sun,Chunlin Chen,Daoyi Dong,Huadong Mo,Zhi Wang,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出RePart框架，将图元（超二次曲面）到语义部件的组织建模为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程，用轨迹级强化学习学习merge-and-stop策略，再通过边界感知表面标注将部件标签映射回原网格，在PartNet与3DCoMPaT++上取得领先的部件分割结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图元的三维分割比稠密表面预测更紧凑、显式，天然支持结构抽象和边界定位。但几何分解本身无法决定图元如何被组织成语义部件：单个部件可能跨多个图元，而几何相似或相邻的图元也可能属于不同部件。因此需要一种面向语义组织的推理机制。

Method: 将图元到部件的组织形式化为有限时域MDP；构建由细粒度超二次曲面组成的可组合图元工作区（Composable Primitive Workspace）；采用merge-and-stop策略，以最终划分的下游效果（而非局部图元兼容性）作为优化目标，通过轨迹级强化推理学习策略；最后用边界感知表面标注（Boundary-Aware Surface Labeling）将推断出的部件身份映射回原始网格，以保留超出图元近似精度的高精度表面边界。

Result: 在PartNet上，RePart在全部四项聚合划分指标中均取得最优结果；在3DCoMPaT++上，无需在目标数据集上微调即获得最高的RI与SC。

Conclusion: 实验表明强化推理是一种将几何图元组织为语义部件的有效机制，同时能够保持稠密分割精度，说明"几何分解+轨迹级语义组织+边界感知回投影"的范式在三维部件分割上具有可行性与竞争力。

Abstract: Primitive-based 3D segmentation offers a compact and explicit alternative to dense surface prediction, naturally supporting structural abstraction and boundary localization. However, geometric decomposition alone does not determine how primitives should be organized into semantic parts: a single part may span multiple primitives, while geometrically similar or touching primitives may belong to different parts. We therefore introduce RePart (Reinforcement Part Reasoning), which formulates primitive-to-part organization as a finite-horizon Markov decision process and learns semantic organization through trajectory-level reinforcement reasoning. RePart constructs a Composable Primitive Workspace from fine-grained superquadrics and applies a merge-and-stop policy whose decisions are optimized by their downstream effects on the resulting partition rather than local primitive compatibility. The inferred part identities are then mapped back to the original mesh through Boundary-Aware Surface Labeling, preserving accurate surface boundaries beyond the primitive approximation. On PartNet, RePart achieves the strongest results across all four aggregate partition metrics; on 3DCoMPaT++, it obtains the highest RI and SC without target-dataset fine-tuning. These results demonstrate that reinforcement reasoning provides an effective mechanism for organizing geometric primitives into semantic parts while retaining dense segmentation accuracy. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [64] [ConEx: Human-Interpretable Saliency Maps via Concept-Aware Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04605)
*Yehonatan Elisha,Oren Barkan,Ziv Weiss Haddad,Noam Koenigstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ConEx框架，通过架构特定的掩码机制自动发现类别特定概念并用CAV表示，将显著性可视化与概念推理桥接，实现忠实且可解释的视觉解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉解释方法仅突出像素重要性，难以将低层线索与语义概念关联，限制了可解释性与可信度。

Method: ConEx自动发现类别特定概念，以概念激活向量(CAV)表示，无需人工监督，采用架构特定的掩码机制降低分割掩码噪声以提升概念纯度；生成忠实显著性图展示各概念位置及对预测的贡献。提出VCM与CCM两个度量评估概念对齐并与现有方法对比。

Result: 在多种设置下，ConEx在忠实性、分割和概念质量基准上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: ConEx推动了视觉模型朝向真正可解释、概念驱动的解释方向。

Abstract: Many visual explanation methods in computer vision highlight pixel importance but struggle to link these low-level cues to semantically meaningful concepts, limiting their interpretability and trustworthiness. We introduce Concept-based Explanations (ConEx), a novel framework that bridges saliency visualization with concept-based reasoning to provide both faithfulness and interpretability. ConEx automatically discovers class-specific concepts and represents them through concept activation vectors (CAVs), learned without manual supervision using an architecture-specific masking mechanism that reduces noise introduced by the segmentation masks to enhance concept purity. ConEx generates faithful saliency maps that reveal where each concept appears in the image and how it contributes to the prediction. To evaluate the reliability of these learned concepts, we propose two complementary metrics, Vector-Concept Match (VCM) and Concept-Class Match (CCM), that quantify concept alignment and enable direct comparison with existing methods. Extensive experiments across diverse settings demonstrate that ConEx achieves state-of-the-art performance on faithfulness, segmentation, and concept-quality benchmarks. Overall, ConEx advances the field toward truly interpretable and concept-grounded explanations in vision models.

</details>


### [65] [Sparse-View 4D Gaussian Splatting via Spatiotemporal Priors and Generative Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04606)
*Shengqi Wang,Zhengxian Yang,Kaiwen Tian,Yang Liu,Bowen Liu,Hua Du,Taicheng Huang,Jiamin Wu,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 面向 SIGGRAPH Asia 2026 体积视频挑战赛稀疏视角赛道，提出 4D 高斯泼溅框架，融合区域自适应空间先验、运动一致时间先验与扩散生成伪监督，在六路宽基线相机下实现鲁棒动态重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角（仅 6 相机、宽基线）下动态场景重建病态性强，高斯初始化与稠密化易失衡，前景动态与静态背景难以统一处理，且中间帧缺乏监督，导致渲染质量与几何稳定性不足。

Method: 三部分：(1) 区域自适应空间先验——用前景掩码引导高斯初始化，掩码投票分别控制前景/背景稠密化，背景几何用尺度对齐的单目深度正则；(2) 运动一致时间先验——帧插值提供中间时刻监督，用估计光流约束投影高斯运动；(3) 生成式辅助——在最大角度间隙放置虚拟相机，用位姿条件扩散模型修复渲染图，作为伪监督迭代加入训练。

Result: 验证集全帧 PSNR 从基线 25.60 dB 提升至 29.75 dB；官方测试基准全帧 PSNR 30.04 dB、前景 PSNR 27.88 dB，稀疏视角赛道总排名第一。

Conclusion: 结合空间、时间先验与生成式伪监督可有效缓解稀疏视角 4D 重建的病态性，在挑战赛基准上取得领先结果，但属竞赛场景级优化方案，跨场景泛化性与实时性尚未验证。

Abstract: We present a 4D Gaussian Splatting framework for the Sparse-View Track of the SIGGRAPH Asia 2026 Volumetric Video Challenge, which requires dynamic scene reconstruction from only six cameras with wide baselines. To achieve robust dynamic reconstruction under such sparse views, our framework integrates three components. (1) Region-adaptive spatial priors: We use foreground masks to guide Gaussian initialization and mask voting to control densification separately for the dynamic foreground and static background. Background geometry is regularized using monocular depth aligned to metric scale. (2) Motion-consistent temporal priors: We provide supervision at intermediate times through frame interpolation and constrain projected Gaussian motion with estimated optical flow. (3) Generative assistance: We place virtual cameras in the widest angular gaps and restore their rendered images using a diffusion-based model conditioned on camera poses. The restored images are iteratively incorporated into training as pseudo-supervision. On the validation set, our framework improves full-frame PSNR from 25.60 dB for the baseline to 29.75 dB. On the official test benchmark, it achieves 30.04 dB full-frame PSNR and 27.88 dB foreground PSNR, ranking first overall in the Sparse-View Track.

</details>


### [66] [Task-Sensitive Geometry of Representation Transfer for Object Detection under Image Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04627)
*Van Vung Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究干净图像监督如何影响退化图像（高斯退化）下的目标检测表征，发现知识蒸馏虽整体提升精度但对象级迁移高度异质；通过正交桥接分离出与直接教师对齐互补的表征分量，在受控干预下可挽救13.22%丢失目标（随机扰动仅4.30%）；提出"任务敏感几何"（基于归一化检测损失梯度的通道空间几何），发现映射互补方向与局部干预响应呈正相关（ρ=0.242），并在独立数据及RT-DETR-L上复现，但据此设计的路由规则与专用蒸馏目标未带来统计可靠增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作表明干净图像监督（如知识蒸馏）能提升退化图像检测的整体精度，但"整体增益"并不代表迁移过来的表征变化在局部、对象层面都一致有用。作者希望探究：干净任务知识如何改变退化图像的表征？局部表征响应能否用几何方式（结构化方向）刻画？目标是理解表征迁移的可解释结构，并检验其能否转化为训练收益。

Method: 1) 数据：成对的干净与高斯退化BDD100K图像；2) 用干净教师蒸馏到退化图像学生模型，观测对象级迁移异质性；3) 通过正交桥（orthogonal bridge）分离出与直接干净教师对齐互补的表征分量，映射到蒸馏学生空间；4) 受控干预对比：真实映射互补方向 vs 范数匹配的随机扰动；5) 提出"任务敏感几何"——由归一化检测损失梯度构造的通道空间几何；6) 在保留队列上计算映射互补方向在该冻结几何中的朝向与局部干预响应幅度的偏Spearman相关，并做幅度控制、独立数据集与RT-DETR-L重复、残差族对照、干预幅度分层分析；7) 尝试将信号用于路由规则与专用蒸馏目标。

Result: 受控干预沿映射互补方向可挽救蒸馏表征下丢失目标中的13.22%，而范数匹配随机扰动仅4.30%，且对已保留目标伤害极低。在保留队列上，映射互补方向在冻结任务敏感几何中的朝向与局部干预响应幅度正相关（偏Spearman ρ=0.242，95% CI [0.108, 0.359]）；该关系在独立数据（ρ=0.180）及RT-DETR-L（ρ=0.227）上复现，但对直接干净教师残差族不成立，且在大幅干预时减弱。基于该信号的路由规则与专用蒸馏目标未能在CLEANKD基础上取得统计可靠增益。

Conclusion: 结果为"局部、方向族依赖的任务敏感几何"提供了证据：蒸馏表征中存在可与直接教师对齐区分的互补方向，其几何朝向能预测局部干预效果。但将该结构转化为全局训练改进仍是开放问题——所提路由与专用蒸馏目标未带来可靠增益。

Abstract: Object detection under image degradation can benefit from clean-image supervision, but aggregate gains do not imply that transferred representation changes are uniformly useful. We study how clean task knowledge affects degraded-image representations and whether local responses to structured representation directions can be characterized geometrically. Using paired clean and Gaussian-degraded BDD100K images, we show that clean-teacher distillation improves observed detection accuracy while producing heterogeneous object-level transfer. We isolate a representation component complementary to direct clean-teacher alignment and map it into the distilled student space through an orthogonal bridge. Controlled interventions rescue 13.22% of objects lost under the distilled representation, versus 4.30% under norm-matched random perturbations, with very low harm on preserved objects. We introduce task-sensitive geometry, a gradient-derived channel-space geometry constructed from normalized detection-loss gradients. On a reserved cohort, mapped-complement orientation within this frozen geometry is positively associated with local intervention-response magnitude after controlling for intervention magnitude (partial Spearman $\rho$ = 0.242, 95% CI [0.108, 0.359]).
