However, handling noisy poses, preserving accurate priors, and recovering physical scale require more than simply accepting these inputs. We introduce \emph{Vision-Prior Geometry Grounded Transformer} (VPGGT), a VGGT-based framework that extends OmniVGGT for prior-aware embodied perception. We formulate sensor-motivated pose corruptions from ground-truth trajectories for training and introduce a parameter-free \emph{prior residual connection} (PRC) to mitigate \emph{prior dilution}, where predictions are less accurate than their supplied pose priors. Our noise formulation targets camera poses; supplied intrinsics and depth receive no additional corruption. We further introduce \emph{Metric Global Attention}, which conditions a global scale token on available pose and depth scales and predicts a shared metric scaling factor for the geometric outputs. Experiments across four datasets show that \emph{PRC} improves translation-direction accuracy and joint pose AUC over a matched training baseline when camera priors are provided for all views, under both exact and corrupted poses. These results support explicit prior access during refinement as a useful addition to feature-level conditioning.

</details>


### [135] [Monocular markerless biomechanics for clinically interpretable gait assessment in spinal cord injury](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05552)
*Shreyasvi Natraj,Mathieu Ruepp,Yanke Li,Robert Riener,Inge Eriks-Hoogland,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发布 SCAI SCI Gait 数据集（239 名脊髓损伤成人，同步视频/动捕/测力台），用单目视频拟合参数化人体网格驱动 OpenSim 模型，实现无标记下肢运动学与地面反作用力预测，并用于临床表型聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维步态分析对脊髓损伤康复有指导价值，但依赖标记点动捕与测力台，临床普及受限；无标记单目方法仅在健康成人队列验证过，神经损伤队列尚属空白。

Method: 构建含同步单目视频、光学动捕与测力台数据的 SCI 步态数据集；对单侧矢状面视频拟合参数化人体网格，以虚拟标记驱动按人体测量学缩放的 OpenSim 模型；进一步用条件依赖图与 Markov blanket 分析波形成分与功能独立性的关联，并做速度分层聚类。

Result: 无标记下肢运动学与动捕一致性达 r=0.68–0.90 (p<0.001)、RMSE 4.18–6.49°；基于运动学预测的地面反作用力与测力台测量吻合，r=0.85–0.87 (p<0.001)、RMSE 2.13–2.19 N/kg；波形成分与功能独立性存在条件关联，同速个体呈现不同机械策略。

Conclusion: 单目视频可作为可扩展的临床生物力学评估与数据驱表型分型手段，适用于脊髓损伤患者。

Abstract: Three-dimensional gait analysis guides rehabilitation after spinal cord injury but depends on marker-based motion capture and force plates, which few clinics have. Monocular markerless pipelines have been established in fewer healthy adult cohorts but not in neurological cohorts. We present the SCAI SCI Gait dataset, comprising 239 adult individuals with spinal cord injury with synchronized video, motion capture, and force-plate measurements, we fitted a parametric body mesh to a single sagittal-view video, driving an anthropometrically scaled OpenSim model via virtual markers. Markerless lower-body kinematics showed state-of-the-art agreement with motion-capture measurements (r = 0.68-0.90, p < 0.001, and RMSE = 4.18-6.49 degrees), and accurate kinematics-based predicted ground-reaction forces closely matched those measured by force plates (r = 0.85-0.87, p < 0.001, and RMSE = 2.13-2.19 Newton per kg). Furthermore, conditional-dependence graph analysis with Markov blankets revealed that waveform components were conditionally associated with functional independence, and speed-stratified clustering revealed distinct mechanical strategies among individuals walking at similar speeds. These findings establish the use of monocular video as a scalable approach for clinically meaningful biomechanical assessment and data-driven phenotyping in patients with spinal cord injury. Github: this https URL

</details>


### [136] [SteadySplats: Resampling of Low-Variance Gaussians for High-Fidelity Stochastic Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05576)
*Felix Windisch,Thomas Köhler,Lukas Radl,Chris Wyman,Georgios Kopanas,Bernhard Kerb,Markus Steinberger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一套针对随机顺序无关透明渲染(如3DGS)去噪的原则性方法,在表征与图像合成两个层面抑制高频噪声:渲染时用基于历史的空域重采样加速收敛、时序重要性重采样保证相机运动一致性,训练时加入颜色正则项降低视线方向上的方差;Vulkan渲染器在1spp下PSNR比先前随机方法提升约13dB,并快速收敛到排序3DGS水平(L1<1e-4)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机顺序无关透明(如随机splatting)渲染3DGS高效优雅,但输出存在固有可见噪声而难以实用,需要系统性地消除噪声源。

Method: 在随机渲染阶段采用history-based空间重采样加速图像收敛,并用时序重要性重采样在相机运动下保持时序一致性;在训练阶段引入颜色正则化器,隐式降低3DGS沿视光线的方差;基于Vulkan实现优化渲染器。

Result: 在低/高采样数下均有效抑制噪声:1spp时相对先前随机方法PSNR提升约13dB,并快速收敛至排序3DGS质量,平均L1误差低于1e-4。

Conclusion: 将去噪问题分解到表征与合成两个层面,使随机顺序无关透明渲染在实用质量下可行,兼顾效率与低噪声输出。

Abstract: Stochastic order-independent transparency enables efficient and elegant rendering of primitive-based radiance fields like 3D Gaussian Splatting models, but remains impractical due to the inherent visible noise in the output. We propose a principled approach to minimize high-frequency noise, addressing its sources at the representation and image synthesis level. During stochastic rendering, our history-based spatial resampling scheme drastically accelerates image convergence, while temporal importance resampling ensures coherence under camera movement. During training, a color regularizer implicitly reduces the variance along view rays in the 3DGS models. With these properties, our optimized, Vulkan-based renderer effectively mitigates output noise at low and high sample counts, achieving a substantial 13~dB PSNR increase in quality over previous stochastic methods at 1 sample per pixel and quickly converging to sorted 3DGS with an average L1 error of less than $10^{-4}$.

</details>


### [137] [Generating the Wild: Individual-Consistent Image-to-Video Generation for Wildlife](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05587)
*Yuzhuo Li,Di Zhao,Xinyu Zhang,Daniel Wilson,Yun Sing Koh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WildIcon：频域引导的图生视频框架，通过提取参考图高频个体特征注入cross-attention，实现野生动物个体一致性生成，并构建WildlifeVid数据集，下游ReID验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野生动物个体识别面临数据稀缺：同一动物在不同姿态、视角、运动下的观测样本稀少。现有I2V模型侧重全局布局、语义与运动，难以保留区分个体的细粒度局部外观线索（毛皮纹理、条纹边界、斑点配置、轮廓过渡），而这些身份关键线索与高频信息密切相关。

Method: 提出WildIcon高频引导I2V框架：1）频率感知身份编码分支，从参考图提取个体特异高频线索；2）结合孤立前景信息生成身份token，作为身份条件注入cross-attention块；3）基于冻结骨干+轻量身份适配，在保持基础I2V模型运动可控性与语义保真度的同时保留细粒度身份线索；4）构建WildlifeVid数据集（时间连贯高质量片段+个体级身份标签）。

Result: I2V生成与下游动物ReID实验显示，WildIcon的个体一致性优于现有基线；其过滤后的输出可作为下游ReID的有效候选训练增强数据。

Conclusion: 高频引导的身份条件注入是提升个体级生成一致性的有效手段，且生成数据可用于增强下游识别任务。

Abstract: Individual-level wildlife identification often suffers from data scarcity, as varying observations of the same animal under diverse poses, viewpoints, and motions are rarely available. Image-to-video (I2V) generation offers a promising way to mitigate this limitation by synthesizing additional observations from a single reference image. However, existing I2V models mainly emphasize global layout, semantics, and motion, and therefore often fail to preserve fine-grained local appearance cues that distinguish one wildlife individual from another, such as fur texture, stripe boundaries, spot configurations, and contour transitions. We observe that these identity-critical cues are closely related to high-frequency information. To address this challenge, we propose WildIcon, a high-frequency-guided I2V framework for wildlife individual consistency. Specifically, WildIcon introduces a frequency-aware identity encoding branch that extracts individual-specific high-frequency cues from the reference image. Combined with isolated foreground information, the resulting identity tokens are then injected into cross-attention blocks as identity conditioning. Building on a frozen backbone with lightweight identity adaptation, WildIcon preserves fine-grained identity cues visible in the reference image while retaining the motion controllability and semantic fidelity of the base I2V model. In addition, to support the training and evaluation of wildlife individual-consistent I2V, we construct WildlifeVid, a wildlife-centric video dataset with high-quality, temporally coherent clips and individual-level identity labels. Experiments on I2V generation and downstream animal re-identification (ReID) show that WildIcon achieves stronger individual consistency than existing baselines, and that its filtered outputs can serve as useful candidate training augmentations for downstream ReID.

</details>


### [138] [EchoDino: A pediatric foundation model for transferable echocardiographic analysis across the lifespan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05603)
*Sheng Cheng,Donnchadh M. O'Sullivan,Daniel J. Penny,Craig G. Rusin,Minh B. Nguyen,Devika Subramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoDino：基于DINOv3自监督的心脏超声基础模型，用170万段未标注儿科超声视频的370万帧训练，冻结编码器即可提取全局、局部与稠密空间表征，并提出MEMS采样选择视频最具信息量帧；在9个儿科与成人数据集上全面超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏超声解读需整合全局解剖、局部结构与动态运动信息，现有机器学习模型多为单一任务、依赖昂贵标注数据；儿科场景标注尤为稀缺，且解剖结构随年龄变化，亟需无标注可迁移的基础模型。

Method: 将DINOv3自监督框架适配至370万帧儿科超声视频帧，训练EchoDino编码器；提出Motion-biased Entropy Maximization Sampling（MEMS）以运动偏置的熵最大化挑选视频级分析中最具信息量的帧；下游任务采用冻结编码器表征。

Result: 9个儿科与成人数据集上优于强基线：视图分类准确率0.609→0.889，结构性心脏病检测AUC 0.811→0.872，年龄估计误差3.857→1.389年，分割精度最佳，射血分数误差降低。

Conclusion: 由无标注儿科数据泛化至成人超声，EchoDino为全生命周期心脏图像分析提供了通用基础模型范式。

Abstract: Echocardiography is the most widely used cardiac imaging modality, yet interpretation demands integrating visual evidence across global anatomy, localized structures and dynamic cardiac motion. Machine-learning models have automated individual tasks, but they are typically built for a single purpose and depend on expensively labeled datasets - a barrier particularly acute in pediatric care, where data are scarce and anatomy changes with age. Here we present EchoDino, a self-supervised foundation model for echocardiography, created by adapting the DINOv3 framework to 3.7 million frames from 1.7 million unlabeled pediatric echocardiography videos. With its encoder frozen, EchoDino produces representations that capture global context, local anatomy, and dense spatial detail. We introduce Motion-biased Entropy Maximization Sampling (MEMS) to select the most informative frames for video-level analysis. Across nine pediatric and adult datasets, EchoDino outperformed strong baseline models, raising view-classification accuracy from 0.609 to 0.889 and the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve for structural-heart-disease detection from 0.811 to 0.872, while also cutting age-estimation error from 3.857 to 1.389 years, achieving the best segmentation accuracy and lowering ejection-fraction errors. By generalizing from label-free pediatric data to adult echocardiography, EchoDino offers a versatile foundation for cardiac image analysis across the lifespan.

</details>


### [139] [Kandinsky 6.0 Video: Foundation Models for Synchronized Video and Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05608)
*Team Kandinsky,Julia Agafonova,Bulat Akhmatov,Mikhail Aksyutin,Grigorii Alekseenko,Anastasia Aliaskina,Olga Androsova,Vladimir Arkhipkin,Anna Averchenkova,Alexander Belykh,Serafima Bocharova,Sofiya Bogakovskaya,Anton Bukashkin,Mark Bulygin,Kirill Buzygin,Irina Cheremnykh,Kirill Chernyshev,Mikhail Chernyshov,Vladimir Chernyy,David Chikovani,Georgy Daniltsev,Denis Dimitrov,Anna Dmitrienko,Vladimir Dokholyan,Sergey Emelyanov,Dmitry Ermilov,Georgii Fedorov,Polina Gavrilova,Nikolai Gerasimenko,Aleksandr Gordeev,Andrey Inozemtsev,Andrei Ivaniuta,Alexander Ivanov,Mikhail Karaev,Anastasiia Kargapoltseva,Ivan Kirillov,Nikita Kiselev,Valeria Kobenko,Yury Kolabushin,Denis Koposov,Anatoly Korobov,Vladimir Korviakov,Kirill Kozlov,Denis Krzhivokolskiy,Konstantin Kuklev,Alexander Kunitsyn,Sergey Kuzin,Vladislav Lakhtionov,Alexey Letunovskiy,Maxim Litvinov,Alexander Lyulkov,Georgy Makarov,Kirill Malakhov,Egor Malykh,Mikhail Mamaev,Dmitrii Mikhailov,Polina Mikhailova,Ivan Mikheev,Elizaveta Muromtseva,Nikolai Nazarkin,Tatiana Nikulina,Lev Novitskiy,Stanislav Onuchin,Nikita Osterov,Denis Parkhomenko,Anatoliy Parpara,Vladimir Polovnikov,Konstantin Reznikov,Azat Saginbaev,Nikita Samsonov,Alexander Sentsov,Nikita Shaimov,Artem Sherstyuk,Andrey Shutkin,Egor Silvestrov,Bulat Suleimanov,Matvey Suprunov,Sergey Taranov,Irina Tolstykh,Tatiana Trofimuk,Ilya Trushkin,Aleksandra Tsybina,Olga Varlashina,Viacheslav Vasilev,Ilya Vasiliev,Eugeny Vilisov,Sergey Yakubson,Konstantin Zakharov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Kandinsky 6.0 Video 提出 3B 与 29B 两个基础扩散模型，通过双流 CrossDiT 架构与双向交叉注意力实现文本/图像到音视频的同步生成，含 44 kHz 音频、口型同步与 Full-HD 超分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型多只处理视觉模态，音视频联合生成中音频与视频的时序、语义对齐仍困难，且大规模音视频配对数据下如何兼顾单模态保真度与跨模态同步尚未很好解决。作者希望在继承 Kandinsky 5.0 视频能力的基础上，构建可同步生成高质量音频与视频的基础模型。

Method: 采用双流 CrossDiT 架构：预训练视频流与从零训练的音频流通过双向交叉注意力进行时序与语义对齐；训练流程为连续预训练（先在音频语料上单独训练音频流，再用配对音视频数据联合训练以保持单模态保真度），随后进行监督微调、基于强化学习的后训练与蒸馏；内置超分模型将分辨率提升至 1920×1080，支持 T2AV 与 I2AV 两种模式，生成 5 秒 44 kHz 音视频。

Result: 在人工并排评测中，29B 的 Kandinsky 6.0 Video Pro 明显优于前代 Kandinsky 5.0 Video Pro，并与领先的音视频生成模型保持竞争力，尤其在语音质量上表现突出；以 MIT 许可证开源代码、模型权重与 diffusers 集成。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了双流交叉注意力与连续预训练策略在同步音视频生成上的有效性，并以开源方式推动多媒体生成研究与部署。但其定位为通用的音视频生成基础模型，未涉及机器人、具身交互或物理仿真验证，与物理 AI/VLA/世界模型等核心方向的直接关联有限。

Abstract: We present Kandinsky 6.0 Video, a family of foundation diffusion models for synchronized text-to-audio-video generation, comprising Kandinsky 6.0 Video Lite (3B parameters) and Kandinsky 6.0 Video Pro (29B parameters). Both models generate 5-second video clips with synchronized 44 kHz audio, including lip-sync, in text-to-audio-video (T2AV) and image-to-audio-video (I2AV) modes; a built-in super-resolution model raises the output resolution to Full-HD (1920$\times$1080). Building on the video generation capabilities of Kandinsky 5.0, Kandinsky 6.0 Video employs a dual-stream CrossDiT architecture that connects a pretrained video stream and a newly trained audio stream through bidirectional cross-attention for temporal and semantic alignment. Our continuous pretraining strategy first trains the audio stream from scratch on large-scale audio corpora and then trains both streams jointly on paired audio-video data while preserving unimodal fidelity; pretraining is followed by supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement-learning-based post-training, and distillation. In side-by-side human evaluation, Kandinsky 6.0 Video Pro clearly outperforms its predecessor, Kandinsky 5.0 Video Pro, and remains competitive with leading audio-video generation models, particularly in speech quality. To accelerate open research and deployment in multimedia generation, we release the code, model checkpoints, and diffusers integration under the MIT license.

</details>


### [140] [StageVLN: Spatial and Trajectory Auxiliary Guidance for Efficient Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05664)
*Anh Dao,Quan-Dung Pham,Le Danh Vinh,Anh Nguyen,Nguyen Viet Tri Pham,Yiyu Chen,Pham Tuyen Le,Van-Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StageVLN 是一个训练期框架，通过冻结几何基础模型提供的多层空间引导以及相对朝向与专家路线进度目标，塑造 VLN 导航表示，所有辅助模块在推理时移除，在 R2R-CE 与 RxR-CE 上以 4B 骨干取得 56.3% SR / 51.4% SPL 与 54.3% SR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN 策略受益于 VLM 的语义先验，但标准动作监督不显式要求中间表示保留场景几何、相对朝向或全局回合进度；而在推理阶段引入深度估计器、显式地图、点云或几何基础模型会带来额外计算、内存开销与架构依赖。

Method: 提出 StageVLN 训练框架：用冻结的几何基础模型为分层导航器状态提供多层级空间引导，并用相对朝向目标与专家路线进度目标提供互补的轨迹状态监督；所有辅助组件仅在训练时使用，部署时移除，保持原始推理通路不变。

Result: 在 R2R-CE validation-unseen 上以 4B 参数骨干达到 56.3% SR 和 51.4% SPL，推理时无需额外几何编码器；在 RxR-CE 上达到 54.3% SR，且未使用额外导航训练数据或推理期几何编码器。

Conclusion: 将特权几何与轨迹监督仅用于训练表征塑造、推理保持原生通路，是一种无需增加部署开销即可提升 VLN 几何与进度感知的有效范式，适用于连续环境导航任务。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) policies increasingly benefit from strong semantic priors provided by large vision-language models (VLMs). However, standard action supervision does not explicitly encourage intermediate representations to preserve scene geometry, relative orientation, or global episode progress. Incorporating depth estimators, explicit maps, point clouds, or geometry foundation models at inference can provide such structure but introduces additional computation, memory overhead, and architectural dependence during deployment. We introduce StageVLN, a training framework that shapes navigation representations through privileged spatial and trajectory guidance while preserving the original inference pathway. A frozen geometry foundation model provides multi-level spatial guidance to hierarchical navigator states, while relative-heading and expert-route progress objectives provide complementary trajectory-state supervision. All auxiliary components are used only during training and removed at deployment. On R2R-CE validation-unseen, StageVLN achieves 56.3\% SR and 51.4\% SPL with a 4B-parameter backbone, without an additional geometry encoder at inference. On RxR-CE, it achieves 54.3\% SR without additional navigation training data or a geometry encoder at inference.

</details>


### [141] [Difference Feature Map Distillation: Transferring Inter-Sample Relational Knowledge Towards Efficient Transformer-Based Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05707)
*Zhicheng Ding,Xinyu Chu,Qing Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DFM-KD关系型知识蒸馏框架，通过蒸馏样本间特征差异而非逐点绝对激活来迁移transformer跟踪器的特征空间结构，提升学生模型鲁棒性与跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向自动驾驶感知的视觉目标跟踪需满足严苛的时延与功耗约束，而transformer跟踪器虽精度SOTA但计算与内存开销大，难以在资源受限平台实时部署。传统特征蒸馏用MSE等逐点对齐强制绝对激活相似，未能传递特征空间的关系结构。

Method: 提出Difference Feature Map Knowledge Distillation（DFM-KD）：以批内跨样本特征差异（inter-sample feature differences）作为蒸馏信号，对齐教师与学生在特征空间中的关系结构，使学生学习教师如何建模外观变化与一致性，而非拟合绝对激活值。

Result: 大量实验表明，DFM-KD在跟踪精度（precision）与成功率（success rate）上均一致优于常规特征级蒸馏方法，蒸馏后模型特征鲁棒性增强。

Conclusion: 关系型差异蒸馏是一种面向transformer视觉跟踪的高效压缩–加速路径，可在保持精度优势的同时降低部署成本。

Abstract: In autonomous driving perception, visual object tracking systems must satisfy stringent latency and power constraints while remaining robust in complex and dynamic environments. Although transformer-based trackers achieve state-of-the-art accuracy, their substantial computational and memory overheads hinder deployment on real-time, resource-constrained platforms. To move toward this goal, we propose Difference Feature Map Knowledge Distillation (DFM-KD), a novel relational distillation framework tailored for transformer-based visual object tracking. Unlike conventional feature distillation methods that minimize point-wise discrepancies (e.g., mean squared error) between teacher and student feature representations, DFM-KD transfers knowledge through inter-sample feature differences, explicitly aligning the relational structure of the feature space. By distilling how the teacher models appearance variation and consistency across samples, rather than enforcing similarity in absolute activations, DFM-KD enables the student to better capture the structural dynamics of visual changes within a batch. As a result, the distilled model exhibits enhanced feature robustness and improved tracking performance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DFM-KD consistently outperforms conventional feature-level distillation methods in both tracking precision and success rates.

</details>


### [142] [Rotated, but How Far? Diagnosing and Improving Object-Rotation Reasoning in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05715)
*Zhaochen Wang,Yujun Cai,Huangbo Zou,Hower Yang,Naipeng Dong,Miao Xu,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OR-Bench细粒度物体旋转推理基准，揭示VLM存在"规范角坍缩"失败模式，并提出轻量解码器RotationCue将冻结表示中的粗粒度旋转信息回灌模型，在3个VLM上提升7.9-12.6个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM能检测物体在跨视角下发生旋转，但无法可靠估计旋转幅度。作者想量化这一能力缺陷并诊断其根源（信息缺失 vs. 信息未被利用）。

Method: 构建OR-Bench，含8类任务（旋转检测、幅度估计、多视角旋转推理）；评测12个VLM并做表征探针分析；提出RotationCue——从VLM冻结表示中线性解码粗粒度旋转，作为中间文本上下文反馈给模型。

Result: 最强模型检测近100%，但粗粒度角度估计接近随机；精确角度下91.8-100%预测集中在0°/90°/180°；无视觉输入时坍缩仍存在；线性探针优于模型生成答案。RotationCue在3个VLM上使所有模型-任务组合提升7.9-12.6点宏平均精度，且保持通用能力。

Conclusion: VLM的旋转推理失败并非单纯信息缺失，而是编码信息未被充分利用；用轻量探针召回并回灌表示是低成本有效的补救方式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can detect that an object has rotated across views, but cannot reliably tell by how much. We introduce OR-Bench, a fine-grained benchmark for object-rotation reasoning with eight tasks covering rotation detection, rotation magnitude estimation, and multi-view rotation reasoning. Across 12 VLMs, the gap is stark: the strongest models approach 100% accuracy on detection, yet even coarse magnitude estimation is near chance. When asked for exact angles, models place 91.8--100% of their predictions on just $0^\circ$, $90^\circ$, and $180^\circ$, a failure we term canonical-angle collapse. This collapse persists even without visual input. Representation probing shows that missing information is only part of the explanation. Although rotation information becomes less recoverable at finer granularity, substantial coarse-grained information remains, and a simple linear probe outperforms the models' generated answers. This suggests that VLMs underuse rotation information they already encode. We therefore propose RotationCue, a lightweight decoder that recovers coarse rotation information from the VLM's own frozen representations and feeds it back to the model as intermediate textual context. Across three VLMs, RotationCue improves every model--task combination on OR-Bench, raising macro-average accuracy by 7.9--12.6 points while preserving general capabilities.

</details>


### [143] [T-JEPA: A Temporal Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for Learning Better Remote Sensing Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05731)
*Bowen Peng,Li Liu,Yongxiang Liu,Weijie Li,Jie Zhou,Zhen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 T-JEPA，一种时间联合嵌入预测架构（temporal JEPA），将 EO 序列中稀疏、非均匀的时间采样本身作为监督信号，学习"时间间隔条件化"的潜空间状态转移，在静态与时序迁移任务上取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感基础模型对时序观测的利用方式有限：或依赖预定义的成对关系，或只做整体重建，未能把地球观测数据天然存在的稀疏、非均匀时间采样结构转化为监督信号。

Method: 共享单帧编码器处理每个观测；时间预测器以被掩码的源潜表示为输入，并结合真实流逝时间，预测完整的目标潜表示场。通过跨多个时间间隔的预测约束，将观测状态组织为结构化的潜轨迹；采用非对称元数据注入缓解捷径学习；多时间尺度直接监督优于递归展开中间状态；并行引入掩码像素重建以保留空间细节。

Result: 在预训练数据与吞吐量匹配的条件下，在静态与时序任务上均取得领先的迁移性能；分析表明所学表示具备依赖时间间隔的转移可预测性与连贯的潜动态，同时保持跨周期一致性、表示多样性与语义可分性。

Conclusion: 以时间间隔为条件的潜空间预测是一种有效利用 EO 时序稀疏采样结构的自监督范式，可在不增加预训练成本的前提下提升遥感基础模型的迁移能力。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) data provide rich temporal supervision, yet existing remote sensing foundation models mainly exploit sequential observations through imposing predefined pairwise relations or aggregating holistic reconstruction context. We seek to further exploit the sparse and nonuniform temporal sampling inherent in EO sequences as supervisory signals. To this end, we propose T-JEPA, a temporal joint-embedding predictive architecture that learns time-gap-conditioned latent transitions. A shared single-frame encoder processes each observation, while a temporal predictor estimates the complete target latent field from a masked source latent representation and the actual elapsed time. Across multiple temporal intervals, these predictive constraints organize observed states into structured latent trajectories. Asymmetric metadata injection mitigates shortcut learning, and direct supervision across multiple temporal scales proves more effective than recursively rolling out intermediate states. In parallel, masked pixel reconstruction provides complementary supervision for preserving spatial details. Under matched pre-training data and throughput, T-JEPA achieves leading transfer performance on both static and temporal tasks. Analyses further reveal that T-JEPA learns representations with time-gap-dependent transition predictability and coherent latent dynamics, while maintaining strong cross-period consistency, representation diversity, and semantic discriminability.

</details>


### [144] [HLA-WM: Hybrid Linear Attention for Long-Horizon Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05739)
*Zhuokun Chen,Feng Chen,Xi Lin,Xiyu Wu,Jiahao He,Jianfei Cai,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HLA-WM：一种免训练混合线性注意力框架，通过相机几何引导的粗粒度检索 + 分块递归状态的细粒度重组，缓解 Gated DeltaNet 的远程遗忘，提升长时视频世界模型的重访一致性，并降低历史状态显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程视频世界模型需要持久记忆来维持场景一致性。Softmax 注意力靠不断增长的 KV cache 保留完整历史，显存代价高；递归线性注意力（如 Gated DeltaNet, GDN）把历史压进固定尺寸状态，显存低但存在严重的远程遗忘——远处但相关的场景信息会被后续状态更新逐步衰减。

Method: HLA-WM 为免训练框架，融合两种粒度的记忆：1）利用 GDN 的仿射结构缓存紧凑的分块（chunk-wise）转移摘要；2）用相机几何检索与当前场景相关的历史块（粗粒度几何引导检索）；3）将检索到的历史块重组为查询特定的递归状态（细粒度递归线性状态计算）。不额外训练，直接作用于自回归基线生成器。

Result: 在 60 秒 SANA-WM-Bench 上，六项聚合重访一致性与相机控制指标全部提升，PSNR +0.74 dB，旋转误差降低 28.5%；下游 refine 后提升仍保持。在 MBench-A 的 547 个样本、4 个子集、所有推理模式上，三项重访一致性指标一致改善。60 秒上下文下，历史状态显存较全 KV cache 降低 12 倍，推理吞吐损失 ≤1.6%。

Conclusion: 可选择性寻址的递归记忆能在保留 GDN 效率优势的同时显著改善长程场景召回，表明"检索增强的线性状态记忆"是长时视频世界模型记忆机制的有效方向。

Abstract: Long-horizon video world models require persistent memory to preserve scene consistency over extended rollouts. Softmax attention retains the full generation history through a growing KV cache, whereas recurrent linear attention compresses history into fixed-size states with substantially lower memory cost. However, we identify severe long-range forgetting in Gated DeltaNet (GDN), where information from distant but relevant scenes is progressively attenuated by subsequent state updates. To address this limitation, we propose HLA-WM, a training-free hybrid linear-attention framework that combines coarse-grained geometry-guided retrieval with fine-grained recurrent linear-state computation. HLA-WM exploits the affine structure of GDN to cache compact chunk-wise transition summaries, retrieve scene-relevant historical chunks using camera geometry, and recompose them into query-specific recurrent states. On the $60$-second SANA-WM-Bench, HLA-WM improves all six aggregate revisit-consistency and camera-control metrics of the base autoregressive generator without additional training, including a $0.74$ dB PSNR gain and a $28.5\%$ reduction in rotation error. The improvements persist after downstream refinement and generalize to MBench-A, where HLA-WM consistently improves all three revisit-consistency metrics across all four subsets and all evaluated inference modes over $547$ samples. At a $60$-second context, HLA-WM reduces historical-state memory by $12\times$ relative to full KV caching while incurring at most a $1.6\%$ reduction in inference throughput. These results demonstrate that selectively addressable recurrent memory can improve long-range scene recall while preserving the efficiency advantages of GDN. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [145] [Robust Local Optimization Done Right](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05743)
*James Pritts,Kevin Köser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统剖析RANSAC评分与局部优化(LO)的鲁棒性差异：提出profile-marginal评分边际化内点尺度并据此设定LO损失宽度，发现优化器需从种子当前拒绝的对应关系获取梯度，联合半二次优化在E/F/H估计中最一致；据此构建的RANSAC在PhotoTourism上将本质矩阵中位位姿误差从2.23°降至1.58°，尺度严重错配时从38°降至6.2°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RANSAC评分与局部优化承担不同鲁棒性职责，但损失形状、内点尺度错配与优化策略三者各自的影响未被系统隔离，实践中LO常因尺度设定不当或优化器盆地特性而失效，且"评分与精化目标应一致"的惯例缺乏验证。

Method: 在本质矩阵、基础矩阵、单应三类估计上做受控消融：(1)profile-marginal评分对冗余内点尺度做边际化并选出内点划分，再由该划分估计尺度以设定LO损失宽度；(2)对比从当前残差重加权的优化器与宽盆地优化器，考察其对强扰动种子与准确种子的表现；(3)采用联合半二次(joint half-quadratic)优化平衡二者；(4)另构建与profile-marginal评分匹配、单调不降的优化器以检验目标一致性假设。

Result: 尺度设得过大时LO仍鲁棒，设得过小则连选择本身都会退化；重加权型优化器被钉死在种子上，宽盆地方法能恢复强扰动种子却损害评分已选出的准确假设，故盆地大小不足以评价RANSAC LO；联合半二次优化最稳定。与评分匹配的优化器精度并非最优。最终RANSAC将PhotoTourism本质矩阵中位位姿误差由2.23°降至1.58°(尺度正确)、由38°降至6.2°(尺度放大128倍)。

Conclusion: 评分与精化的鲁棒性需求应被分离处理：边际化内点尺度可解耦选择与精化对尺度的敏感性，优化器需具备从种子拒绝样本中获取梯度的能力，联合半二次优化是跨模型类别最一致的策略，"评分目标与精化目标须匹配"的既有处方被证伪。

Abstract: RANSAC scoring and local optimization (LO) impose different robustness requirements, motivating the separation of hypothesis selection from refinement. We systematically isolate the effects of robust-loss shape, incorrectly specified inlier scales, and optimization strategy on essential matrix, fundamental matrix, and homography estimation. A profile-marginal score marginalizes the nuisance inlier scale and selects an inlier partition, from which we estimate the scale that sets the LO loss width; this makes LO robust to an inlier scale specified too large, whereas one specified too small degrades selection itself. Refinement needs gradient from correspondences the seed currently rejects: optimizers that reweight from current residuals stay pinned to their seed, whereas methods with broad basins recover strongly perturbed seeds yet degrade accurate score-selected hypotheses, so basin size alone is insufficient to assess RANSAC LO. Joint half-quadratic optimization balances the two and is the most consistent strategy across model classes. An optimizer matched to the profile-marginal score, which never decreases it, does not reach the best accuracy, challenging the prescription that scoring and refinement objectives should match. Composed from these findings, our RANSAC reduces the median essential-matrix pose error of a state-of-the-art RANSAC on PhotoTourism from 2.23 degrees to 1.58 degrees with a correctly specified inlier scale and from 38 degrees to 6.2 degrees when it is grossly misspecified (128x too large).

</details>


### [146] [A new design of a fall detection system integrating landmark identification and deep learning techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05749)
*Tri Nhut Do,Thi Thuy Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种融合 MediaPipe 关键点识别与深度学习预测的跌倒检测系统，通过关键点特征区分日常动作与真实跌倒，训练约40轮后精度稳定，实时测试8–50岁参与者跌倒检出率达95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人跌倒若不能及时发现会带来严重安全风险，而现有检测方案误报率偏高、可靠性不足，因此需要提升跌倒识别的准确性与响应及时性，以支持老年照护与智能健康监测场景。

Method: 用 MediaPipe 进行空间关键点识别，将人体关键点序列作为特征输入深度学习预测算法，通过关键点与深度模型的集成来区分常规动作与真实跌倒，从而抑制误报；采用迭代训练（约40个周期收敛）并进行覆盖多场景的实时实验验证。

Result: 训练准确率随周期稳步上升并在第40个周期趋于稳定，误差率在初期周期显著下降；涵盖8–50岁男女参与者的实时实验取得95%的跌倒检测率，并在多种场景下表现出适应性。

Conclusion: 关键点识别与深度学习的结合可有效提升跌倒检测的准确性与可靠性，降低误报并保证及时响应，在老年护理与智能健康监测中具有应用前景。

Abstract: This article introduces an innovative system that integrates landmark identification with deep learning to enhance fall detection accuracy and reliability. By utilizing advanced computer vision techniques, such as Media Pipe for spatial recognition, the system effectively differentiates between routine movements and actual falls. The integration of landmarks with a deep learning prediction algorithm minimizes false alarms, ensuring timely responses to genuine falls. Comprehensive experimentation underscores the system's versatility across various scenarios, emphasizing its potential to improve safety and independence for older adults. The training process demonstrates a steady increase in accuracy, stabilizing by the 40th cycle, while error rates decline significantly during the initial cycles. Real-time experiments, involving both male and female participants aged 8 to 50, recorded a remarkable 95% detection rate of falls, demonstrating the system's effectiveness and promising future applications in elder care and smart health monitoring environments.

</details>


### [147] [Vision-enabled detection of safety helmet compliance in construction zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05756)
*Tri Nhut Do*,Ba Loc Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 基于YOLOv8的施工场地安全帽佩戴实时检测系统，mAP@0.5精度超97%，在光照与运动变化下表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施工管理中工人头部安全是首要问题，人工巡检难以实时覆盖；作者希望借助视觉系统自动监测安全帽合规情况，以降低头部伤害风险并推动职业安全管理技术化。

Method: 部署于战略位置的摄像头采集高分辨率视频流，采用YOLO框架下的深度学习目标检测算法（YOLOv8）识别未佩戴安全帽的人员，通过训练损失下降与指标提升迭代优化识别性能。

Result: 在佩戴与未佩戴安全帽两类目标上，mAP@0.5精度超过97%；在不同光照条件和多样工人运动姿态下均具有较高检测准确率，训练过程中损失持续下降、指标稳定提升。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了YOLOv8在安全帽合规检测中的有效性，其意义超出合规监管本身，为职业安全管理提供了新应用方向，并为智能建造环境中的安全技术发展奠定基础。

Abstract: In the rapidly evolving field of construction management, worker safety remains a top priority. This paper introduces an innovative vision-based system for real-time detection of helmet compliance, specifically designed for construction sites, utilizing advanced computer vision techniques and machine learning algorithms within the YOLO (you only look once) framework. Our system leverages high-resolution video feeds from strategically positioned cameras to monitor adherence to safety regulations regarding helmet usage. By employing deep learning methodologies, the system effectively identifies individuals not wearing helmets, thereby significantly mitigating the risk of head injuries among workers. Our training and validation results revealed an impressive precision exceeding 97% at mAP@0.5 for both helmeted and non-helmeted individuals. Furthermore, our experiments demonstrate exceptional detection accuracy, demonstrating the system's resilience under varying lighting conditions and diverse worker movements. The consistent decrease in loss and improvement in metrics throughout training validates the effectiveness of the YOLOv8 model in enhancing recognition performance. The implications of this research extend beyond mere regulatory compliance, opening avenues for innovative applications in occupational safety management. This study highlights the critical role of technology in protecting lives and lays the groundwork for future advancements in smart construction environments.

</details>


### [148] [A Three-Dimensional Reverse-Projection Method for Sparse Point Cloud Completion and Its Application to High-Speed Train Nose Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05758)
*Xiaozhen Ma,Zhao Tang,Hanbin Lai,Ruiqi Chen,Jin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一套面向高速列车车头扫描的点云获取-滤波-补全-表面重建流水线：FAST-LIVO2 提供初始点云，MLS 平滑，采用三轴投影细分插值与反向孔洞边界识别补全玻璃窗缺失区域，最后比较贪心投影三角化、泊松重建与 Marching Cubes 三种网格化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃窗在 LiDAR 扫描中不被回波，导致点云孔洞，是含玻璃环境三维重建中尚未解决的问题；同时扫描存在稀疏采样。作者需要一套无需接触手段的数字化建模流程，用于高速列车车头这一含大面积玻璃的结构。

Method: FAST-LIVO2 多传感器里程计与建图获得初始点云；MLS 对观测做平滑；提出三轴投影细分与插值，并结合反向孔洞边界识别（three-axis reverse completion），从孔洞周围的观测插值缺失区域；对补全后的点云分别用贪心投影三角化、泊松表面重建以及基于 Marching Cubes 的流程生成网格。

Result: 在比例缩放的列车车头展示模型上，所提方法可填补玻璃窗周围缺失点云区域；在其设定的评价条件下，贪心投影三角化的几何距离误差低于另外两种重建流程。

Conclusion: 该流水线支持列车车头几何的非接触数字化建模，为含玻璃物体重建提供了可行方案；但方法在特定缩比模型上验证，未涉及真实运行场景或大范围泛化。

Abstract: Holes in LiDAR scans of environments with glass windows remain an unresolved problem in three-dimensional reconstruction. This study presents a pipeline for point cloud acquisition, filtering, completion, and surface reconstruction to address sparse sampling and missing window regions in scans of a high-speed train nose. FAST-LIVO2 provides the initial point cloud through multisensor odometry and mapping, and moving least squares (MLS) smooths the observations. We then introduce three-axis projection-based subdivision and interpolation with reverse hole boundary identification, referred to as three-axis reverse completion. The method interpolates missing regions from observations around each hole. Greedy projection triangulation, Poisson surface reconstruction, and a Marching Cubes-based pipeline generate meshes from the completed point cloud. Experiments on a proportionally scaled display model of a high-speed train nose show that the proposed method fills missing point cloud regions around the glass windows. Under the evaluation setting used in this study, greedy projection triangulation yields lower geometric distance errors than the other two reconstruction pipelines. The pipeline supports non-contact digital modeling of train nose geometry and provides a practical approach to reconstructing objects with glass windows.

</details>


### [149] [Controllable Road Marking Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05771)
*Zhiyu,Yufan Zhang,Ruichen Tan,Zengxiang Lei,Satish Ukkusuri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可控道路标线生成任务与条件BEV生成框架：由可行驶区域掩码、外环标线与文本提示合成中心区域标线布局，结合拓扑感知损失的rectified-flow DiT、高斯模糊监督目标与免训练SGR后处理，在Argoverse 2上Buffered F1 80.8、clDice 50.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车道与道路标线是车辆导航与多智能体协同的关键先验，但大规模标注仍依赖人工流程，限制定量分析与场景测试；因此需要可从可行驶区域与文本描述自动生成标线布局的方法，以支持仿真场景变体、地图补全与早期基础设施设计探索。

Method: 1) 任务与基准：Controllable Road Marking Generation，由可行驶区域掩码+可选外环标线+文本描述生成缺失中心区域标线，输出车道分隔线、道路分隔线、人行横道三通道，提示由元数据确定性派生。2) 条件BEV管线：文本条件潜空间rectified-flow DiT，配拓扑感知辅助损失；对细、稀疏标线采用高斯模糊训练目标稳定学习；3) 免训练后处理SGR（Structured Gaussian Render）：提取折线、拟合三次Bézier曲线并以各向异性超高斯基元重渲染，恢复清晰分隔线几何。

Result: Argoverse 2的4,597张测试瓦片：Buffered F1 80.8、clDice 50.2，对比适配的SOTA掩码细化基线38.8/24.6；Waymo数据集上88.0 Buffered F1、66.2 clDice。消融显示拓扑感知监督与SGR在连通性上互补增益；文本编辑实验显示更强引导提升编辑成功率，但也会增加对非目标结构的改动。

Conclusion: 该框架为面向仿真的标线变体生成、自动化地图补全与早期基础设施设计探索提供了可行路径，量化指标显著优于掩码细化基线。

Abstract: Lane and road markings provide critical guidance for vehicle navigation and multi-agent coordination, yet authoring them at scale remains a manual workflow that limits quantitative analysis and scenario testing. We introduce Controllable Road Marking Generation, which synthesizes a missing center-region marking layout from a drivable-area mask, optional outer-ring markings, and a textual description. Our benchmark uses deterministic, metadata-derived prompts and three output channels: lane dividers, road dividers, and pedestrian crossings. We develop a conditional bird's-eye-view (BEV) pipeline that combines (i) a text-conditioned latent rectified-flow DiT trained with a topology-aware auxiliary loss, (ii) Gaussian-blurred training targets that stabilize learning of thin, sparse markings, and (iii) Structured Gaussian Render (SGR), a training-free post-process that recovers crisp divider geometry by extracting polylines, fitting cubic Bézier curves, and re-rendering them as anisotropic super-Gaussian primitives. On 4,597 Argoverse~2 test tiles, our system achieves Buffered F1 of 80.8 and clDice of 50.2, compared with 38.8 and 24.6 for an adapted state-of-the-art mask-refinement baseline. On Waymo dataset, it yields 88.0 Buffered F1 and 66.2 clDice. Component ablations show complementary connectivity gains from topology-aware supervision and SGR. Text-editing experiments reveal that stronger guidance improves edit success but also increases changes to non-target structures. We see this framework as a step toward simulation-ready road-marking variation, automated map completion, and early-stage infrastructure design exploration.

</details>


### [150] [InteractionBench: A Real-Time Interaction Benchmark for Streaming Video Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05775)
*Enxin Song,Suhao Yu,Yifei Xu,Barbara Su,Weili Xu,Wenhao Chai,Yao Tang,Jie Deng,Haiyang Xu,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 InteractionBench 基准，用于评估视频助手的"何时发言/何时沉默"决策，覆盖查询响应、事件触发与持续更新三类任务，在 812 个视频上的 1060 个交互中评测内容准确率、时机准确率与沉默合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频助手需要在指令要求响应时发言、否则保持沉默，但现有评测缺乏对完整系统（模型+记忆+响应控制器）这一决策能力的衡量，且离线分数无法预测在线行为。

Method: 构建 InteractionBench：812 个视频、1060 个交互，含 69 个负样本流（无应答需求）与 53 个对照套件（计数事件与相似"几乎命中"情形配对）；在视频时间轴上以内容准确率、时机准确率、沉默合规性三项指标评分；评测轮询式 Qwen3-VL-8B 与原生实时交互系统等方案。

Result: 轮询式 Qwen3-VL-8B 时机准确率 77.8% 但沉默合规仅 10.9%；原生实时系统沉默合规 29.2%、时机 66.8%，在 89.9% 的负样本流上仍发言；无开源权重系统通过三分之一近失套件；随机删减回复只会在时机与沉默之间权衡；离线分数无法预测在线失败；原生系统控制器自身增益有限，智能体方案每页约需 30 秒。

Conclusion: 及时发言与保持沉默存在权衡，现有系统在沉默合规与近失判别上普遍失败，说明"克制"能力代价高昂且尚无成熟解法；该基准揭示了离线评测的盲区，为实时交互系统的响应控制研究提供了诊断工具。

Abstract: A video assistant must speak when its instruction warrants a response and stay silent otherwise. We introduce a benchmark that evaluates this decision for the complete system of model, memory, and response controller. InteractionBench covers query responses, event triggers, and ongoing updates in 1,060 interactions over 812 videos, with 69 negative streams and 53 suites that pair counted events with look-alike near misses. It scores content accuracy, timing accuracy, and silence compliance on the video clock. Timely speech costs silence across systems. Polled Qwen3-VL-8B reaches 77.8 timing accuracy but 10.9 silence compliance. A native real-time interaction system reaches 29.2 silence compliance at 66.8 timing accuracy, yet emits on 89.9% of negative streams. No open-weight system clears a third of the near-miss suites. Fewer replies help only when chosen, as random deletion merely trades timing for silence. Offline scores miss these failures and mispredict online behavior. Adding restraint is costly, as the native system's controller adds little by itself and agentic systems add it only at about 30 s per this http URL page: this https URL Code: this https URL Data: this https URL

</details>


### [151] [A Spatiotemporal Semantic Importance-Guided Unified Compression and Editing Framework for AI-Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05779)
*Xihua Sheng,Dong Liu,Chang Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向AI生成视频的统一压缩与编辑框架，以冻结视频生成器作为可复用生成先验，并设计三种时空语义重要性引导技术，分别解决“传什么、传多少、如何用”侧信息的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成视频在体量、时长与分辨率上快速增长，带来存储与传输压力。与自然视频不同，AI生成视频是生成分布中的采样，语义结构决定内容一致性，而大量局部纹理与随机细节可被合理再生，因此压缩应保留语义重要的时空信息而非逐像素重建；同时用户还需要基于提示的编辑并保持原时空语义，故需统一压缩与编辑框架。

Method: 以冻结视频生成器作为可复用生成先验，构建压缩与编辑统一框架，包含三项时空语义重要性引导技术：1）创新选择方法，利用时空语义重要性投影潜变量差异，使所选创新优先编码语义不变量而非可替换的生成变化；2）帧自适应码率分配方法，估计潜帧的非均匀语义需求，将更多创新分配给需要更强语义保持的帧；3）统一重建与编辑方法，连续调节所传侧信息的影响强度，使同一压缩表示既能强引导忠实重建，又可作为结构保持的提示驱动编辑的灵活语义锚点。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅描述框架与三项技术组件；其效果诉求为在保持语义不变量的前提下提升压缩效率，并支持同一表示下的忠实重建与提示驱动编辑。

Conclusion: 该工作将AI生成视频的压缩与编辑统一到生成先验框架下，以语义重要性替代逐像素保真作为设计准则，为生成式视频的高效存储、传输与可控编辑提供新思路。

Abstract: AI-generated videos are rapidly increasing in volume, duration, and resolution, creating growing demands for efficient storage and transmission. Unlike natural videos captured from the physical world, AI-generated videos are samples from a learned generative distribution, where semantic structures are critical to content consistency, while many local textures and stochastic details can be plausibly regenerated. This distinction suggests that compression should preserve semantically important spatiotemporal information rather than reconstruct every pixel of a particular generative sample. Beyond reconstruction, AI-generated videos also create a practical need for prompt-based editing, where users expect to modify generated content while preserving its original spatiotemporal semantics. Motivated by these observations, we propose a unified compression and editing framework for AI-generated videos that incorporates a frozen video generator as a reusable generative prior. Within this framework, we design three spatiotemporal semantic importance-guided techniques that respectively address what to transmit, how much to transmit, and how to use the transmitted side information. First, an innovation selection method projects the latent discrepancy using spatiotemporal semantic importance, so that the selected innovations prioritize semantic invariants over replaceable generative variations. Second, a frame-adaptive bit allocation method estimates the nonuniform semantic demands of latent frames and allocates more innovations to frames requiring stronger semantic preservation. Third, a unified reconstruction and editing method continuously adjusts the influence of the transmitted side information, enabling the same compressed representation to provide strong guidance for faithful reconstruction or serve as a flexible semantic anchor for structure-preserving prompt-driven editing.

</details>


### [152] [FairRSFM: A Biome-Aware Benchmark and Debiasing Framework for Remote Sensing Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05790)
*Md Aminur Hossain,Omkumar Vaghasiya,Rajeev Ranjan Dwivedi,Vinod Kurmi,Biplab Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FairRSFM，一个按生物群系分组的遥感基础模型鲁棒性评测基准，揭示聚合指标掩盖的生态区域性能差异，并评估三种缓解基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感基础模型（RSFM）普遍使用聚合指标评测，会掩盖不同生态区域上的系统性性能差距；缺乏统一、可复用的生态组鲁棒性诊断协议。

Method: 将14类陆地生物群系的地理参考样本映射为6个生态宏组；在统一的冻结骨干评测协议下，覆盖 m-EuroSAT、m-BigEarthNet、m-SA-Crop-Type、MMEarth20K（Dynamic World标签）四个下游数据集；使用 Prithvi-EO-2.0、SatMAE、DOFA 三个模型、三个随机种子；评估 BOLP（生物群系正交线性探测）、DBR（动态生物群系重加权）、GroupDRO 三种缓解基线。

Result: 聚合性能一致地掩盖生物群系相关差异：Prithvi-EO-2.0 在 m-EuroSAT 上整体 macro-F1 为 90.98%，但平均最差组仅 83.72%；m-SA-Crop-Type 从整体 27.30% mIoU 降至 Xeric & Mineralogical 组的 18.47%。BOLP 可在不更新骨干的情况下将 m-BigEarthNet 上 Prithvi-EO-2.0 的最差组 F1@opt 从 46.12% 提升至 50.27%。缓解方法效果依赖模型与任务。

Conclusion: FairRSFM 提供了诊断与缓解遥感基础模型生态鲁棒性差距的可复用协议，代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Remote sensing foundation models (RSFMs) are commonly evaluated using aggregate metrics, which can hide systematic performance disparities across ecological regions. We introduce FairRSFM, a biome-aware benchmark for evaluating ecological group robustness in RSFMs. FairRSFM maps georeferenced samples from 14 terrestrial biome classes into six ecologically meaningful macro-groups and evaluates models under a unified frozen-backbone evaluation protocol. The benchmark covers four downstream datasets: m-EuroSAT, m-BigEarthNet, m-SA-Crop-Type, and MMEarth20K with Dynamic World label maps. Using Prithvi-EO-2.0, SatMAE, and DOFA across three random seeds, we show that aggregate performance consistently masks biome-dependent disparities across architectures and tasks. For example, Prithvi-EO-2.0 reaches 90.98% overall macro-F1 on m-EuroSAT but a mean worst-group score of only 83.72%, while m-SA-Crop-Type drops from 27.30% overall mIoU to 18.47% in the Xeric and Mineralogical group. We further evaluate Biome-Orthogonal Linear Probing (BOLP), Dynamic Biome Reweighting (DBR), and GroupDRO as complementary mitigation baselines. Their effectiveness is model- and task-dependent; for example, BOLP improves Prithvi-EO-2.0 worst-group F1@opt on m-BigEarthNet from 46.12% to 50.27% without updating the RSFM backbone. FairRSFM provides a reusable protocol for diagnosing and mitigating ecological robustness gaps in remote sensing foundation models. Code and datasets are available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [153] [Gauss-Map Variation for Image Denoising: Geometric Analysis and an Anderson--Accelerated Majorization--Minimization Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05801)
*Haibin Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于缩放图像图切平面投影子空间空间变化的Gauss映射变分（GMV）正则模型，用微分几何工具分析其边界保持性质，并设计Anderson加速的MM算法求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像去噪中边缘与角点的对比度保持是经典难题，需要构建能刻画边界几何结构、并在连续层面可分析的正则项。

Method: 1) 以缩放图像图的切平面投影子的空间变化量构建GMV正则项，并给出其在Gauss映射下的等价表示；2) 借助管状坐标、Frenet标架等微分几何工具，分析C^2及分段C^2边界下的正则项行为；3) 引入单位法向场与标量幅值场的双线性分解，将法向场子问题化为逐点显式MM更新，外层采用Anderson加速；4) 对L^1与L^2保真项分别证明迭代充分下降、有界性与收敛到临界点。

Result: 理论上得到边缘与角点对比度保持的估计，并证明L^1/L^2两种数据保真下迭代序列的充分下降、有界与收敛性；合成与自然图像实验表明模型具有边界保持能力，在去除高斯噪声与脉冲噪声上性能有竞争力。

Conclusion: GMV为图像去噪提供了一个几何可解释、理论收敛性完备的变分框架，边界保持效果良好，但与具身智能等方向无直接关联。

Abstract: We propose a Gauss-map variation (GMV) model for image denoising that measures the spatial variation of the tangent-plane projectors of the scaled image graph. We establish an equivalent representation of the regularizer in terms of the corresponding Gauss map and, using differential geometric tools including tubular coordinates and the Frenet frame, analyze its behavior across general $C^2$ and piecewise $C^2$ boundaries. The resulting estimates provide edge- and corner-contrast preservation properties. To solve the proposed model, we introduce a bilinear decomposition involving a unit normal field and a scalar magnitude field and develop an Anderson-accelerated majorization--minimization algorithm. The normal field subproblem admits an explicit pointwise majorization--minimization update, which is combined with an Anderson acceleration. For both $L^1$ and $L^2$ data fidelity terms, we establish sufficient decrease and boundedness of the iterates and prove that the generated sequence converges to a critical point of the penalized model. Numerical experiments on synthetic and natural images demonstrate the boundary preserving capability of the proposed model and its competitive performance in removing Gaussian and impulsive noise.

</details>


### [154] [Level-of-Token Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05816)
*Kiyohiro Nakayama,Brian Chao,Jan Ackermann,Hansheng Chen,Federico Tombari,Leonidas Guibas,Lior Yariv,Gordon Wetzstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoT Diffusion 提出一种多分辨率 token 布局（Level-of-Token），通过非对称 flow 参数化与多分辨率嵌入微调预训练 DiT，在保持全分辨率 flow 预测的同时减少 token 序列长度，实现图像与视频生成的质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型对图像/视频所有区域分配同等计算量，但细节的空间分布往往在生成前即可预知（如语义掩码、深度、纹理方差），因此存在按需分配计算的冗余，可借此提升推理效率。

Method: 构建显式多分辨率 token 布局，token 为不同大小与形状的矩形 patch，细节区用细 token、其他区用粗 token；将预训练 DiT 适配到该布局，采用逐 patch 非对称 flow 参数化与多分辨率 token 嵌入，使每步去噪仍可进行全分辨率 flow 预测但只处理缩减后的 token 序列，从而保持预训练生成先验。布局可来自语义掩码、包围盒、纹理方差、景深线索及 agentic 规划。

Result: 在图像与视频生成任务上取得较好的质量-效率折中，加速幅度由布局的 token 预算决定；支持由多种线索导出的布局实现自适应生成，并保留预训练生成先验。

Conclusion: LoT Diffusion 通过显式多分辨率 token 布局将预知的细节分布转化为计算分配，验证了布局自适应生成的可行性与效率优势，为扩散模型的高效推理提供了一种通用且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Image and video diffusion models allocate equal computation to every region, even when the intended scene calls for varying levels of detail. The spatial distribution of detail can often be anticipated before generation, indicating where computation can be reduced. We introduce Level-of-Token (LoT) Diffusion, a framework that turns this knowledge into an explicit multiresolution token layout (Level-of-Token layout) for adaptive and efficient generation. Tokens represent rectangular patches of varying sizes and shapes, allocating finer tokens where detail is needed and coarser tokens elsewhere. We adapt pretrained diffusion transformers to LoT layouts through a patch-wise asymmetric flow parametrization and embeddings for multiresolution tokens, preserving full-resolution flow prediction at every denoising step while processing only a reduced token sequence. LoT Diffusion enables layout-adaptive generation while preserving pretrained generative priors. We demonstrate LoT with layouts derived from semantic masks, bounding boxes, texture variance, and depth-of-field cues, as well as agentic plans. Across image and video generation, LoT offers favorable quality-efficiency tradeoffs, with significant speedups determined by the layout's token budget. Our project website is at this https URL .

</details>


### [155] [Imagine to Act: High-Fidelity Data Synthesis via Image Editing World Model for Scalable GUI Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05861)
*Yongxin Ning,Runliang Niu,Qianli Xing,Zhiyi Duan,Qingzu He,Pan Wang,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Infinite-Dreamer 提出一种基于像素级图像编辑世界模型的免仿真数据合成方法：用 VLM 将 GUI 动作引起的界面变化描述为结构化 delta-text，微调图像编辑骨干可控生成真实截图转场，从而合成单帧鲁棒性数据与多步想象轨迹；仅用合成数据微调 Qwen3-VL 得到 Infinite-Actor，在 AndroidWorld、MobileWorld、AndroidControl-Curated 上一致超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI 智能体的训练依赖大规模高保真视觉-动作轨迹，但这类数据极难获取：人工演示无法扩展，而现有 GUI 世界模型依赖文本描述或 HTML 渲染，丢失了图标、布局风格等关键像素级视觉细节，导致生成数据保真度不足、泛化性受限。

Method: 将 GUI 状态转移建模为图像编辑任务；用 VLM 把动作引发的 UI 变化表示为结构化 delta-text；据此微调图像编辑骨干网络，实现可控的截图转场合成。基于该模型生成两类数据：单帧视觉鲁棒性数据与多步想象轨迹。仅使用合成数据微调 Qwen3-VL 基线，得到 Infinite-Actor。

Result: 在 AndroidWorld、MobileWorld、AndroidControl-Curated 三个基准上，Infinite-Actor 在各规模下均稳定优于 Qwen3-VL 基线：8B 版本 AndroidWorld Pass@1 提升 +4.45，MobileWorld Pass@3 成功率接近翻倍；2B 版本 Pass@1 提升 +9.05。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 以像素级图像编辑世界模型替代仿真器与 HTML 渲染进行数据合成，可在无真实环境交互的情况下有效提升 GUI 智能体的能力与泛化性，且小模型收益更显著，具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for automating complex digital workflows across diverse applications. However, training highly capable and generalizable agents fundamentally relies on massive, high-fidelity visual-action trajectories, which are notoriously difficult to acquire. While human demonstrations are unscalable, existing GUI world models rely on text descriptions or HTML rendering, discarding crucial pixel-level visual details like icons and layout styles. To address this issue, we introduce Infinite-Dreamer, a simulation-free data synthesis method powered by a pixel-level Image Editing World Model. By conceptualizing GUI transitions as image editing tasks, we leverage Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to describe action-induced UI changes as structured delta-text. We then fine-tune an image editing backbone to controllably synthesize realistic screenshot transitions. We utilize this model to generate both single-frame visual robustness data and multi-step imaginary trajectories. To validate the effectiveness of our approach, we fine-tune the Qwen3-VL baseline solely on the synthesized data to obtain Infinite-Actor, and evaluate it on AndroidWorld, MobileWorld, and AndroidControl-Curated benchmarks. Infinite-Actor consistently outperforms the Qwen3-VL baselines across scales: Infinite-Actor-8B improves AndroidWorld Pass@1 by +4.45 and nearly doubles the MobileWorld Pass@3 success rate, while Infinite-Actor-2B improves Pass@1 by +9.05. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [156] [Weave Mamba Fusion: Global Cross-Scale Interaction for Lightweight Face Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05865)
*Dohun Kim,Jinmyung Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Weave Mamba Fusion (WMF)，将相邻金字塔尺度逐列交织以支持跨尺度双向SS2D扫描，构建WeaveBiFPN与WeaveFace人脸检测器，在WIDER FACE上以0.34M参数、1.16 GFLOPs达到91.41% mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征金字塔（FPN、BiFPN）依赖局部融合，难以建模无约束人脸检测（小尺度、遮挡、极端姿态）所需的全局跨尺度依赖。Mamba类状态空间模型以线性复杂度提供全局上下文，但扫描需把两个金字塔尺度合并为单一特征图：求和会在扫描前塌缩尺度结构，拼接则保留结构但代价过高。

Method: Weave Mamba Fusion（WMF）：将相邻两个金字塔尺度按列交错（weave），使水平双向SS2D扫描的每一步在两个尺度间切换，从而在扫描中保留跨尺度结构；配合部分通道处理与无参数de-weaving降低开销。将WMF嵌入每个融合节点构成WeaveBiFPN，作为WeaveFace检测器的neck。

Result: 在WIDER FACE上，WeaveFace以0.34M参数、1.16 GFLOPs取得91.41%平均AP，在0.5M参数以下检测器中领先；Hard子集增益最大，达87.14% AP。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 列级交织式Mamba融合在保持跨尺度特征结构的同时实现高效全局交互，为轻量人脸检测提供了优于求和/拼接融合的有效范式。

Abstract: Feature pyramid methods, from FPN to BiFPN, have achieved strong performance in face detection by fusing multi-scale features. However, detecting faces under unconstrained conditions, such as small scale, occlusion, and extreme pose, remains difficult, as it requires global cross-scale dependencies that local fusion cannot model. State space models such as Mamba provide global context with linear complexity by scanning features as a sequence, and therefore offer a promising direction for this problem. Nevertheless, such a scan needs the two pyramid scales combined into a single feature map, and the way they are combined determines whether cross-scale structure is preserved. Summation collapses the two scales before the scan, so the scan has no cross-scale structure to exploit, while concatenation keeps both scales but at far higher cost. To address this, we propose \textbf{Weave Mamba Fusion (WMF)}, which interleaves two adjacent pyramid scales column by column so that each step of a horizontal bidirectional SS2D scan moves from one scale to the other. With partial-channel processing and parameter-free de-weaving, WMF enables efficient cross-scale interaction while preserving feature structure. Integrating WMF into every fusion node yields \textbf{WeaveBiFPN}, the neck of our \textbf{WeaveFace} detector. On WIDER FACE, WeaveFace achieves 91.41\% mean AP with only 0.34M parameters and 1.16 GFLOPs, outperforming prior detectors under 0.5M parameters. Its largest gains are on the Hard subset, where it reaches 87.14\% AP. The code is publicly available at \url{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [157] [Certification of Real Images through Calibrated Content Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05870)
*Sarim Hashmi,Abdelrahman Elsayed,Mohammed Talha Alam,Samuele Poppi,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估20个深度伪造检测器对4年内10个生成器的检测能力，发现准确率从99.5%降至76%，且对抗扰动可将其降至2%以下；作者提出"合理可否认性"检测范式，要求生成内容能被同一生成器忠实重建，并给出可校准的认证式检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型合成的伪造多媒体已被大规模滥用，但检测器的可靠性随时间下降并易被对抗扰动攻破。作者认为根本原因在于内容本身无法揭示真实来源：生成器可通过记忆精确复现真实内容，故仅凭内容判定真伪在原理上存在歧义，需要新的检测范式。

Method: 首先对20个深度伪造检测器与10个近年生成器（覆盖4年）做跨代基准评测，并施加有界对抗扰动；随后提出"合理可否认性"范式：若任一已知生成器能对该内容给出忠实重建，则其合成来源可信、真实性可合理否认；据此构建输出校准预测的检测器，用校准界限控制已知生成器内容被误判的比例，并在攻击样本上采用更严格的安全阈值。

Result: 基准检测器准确率随生成器迭代由99.5%降至76%，对抗扰动使所有基线降至2%以下（标签被完全反转）。所提检测器可校准到至多1%的生成内容被错误认证，而该工作点上多数基线召回率接近零（最强基线准确率93%）；对攻击样本采用更严格阈值可在评估的有界扰动攻击空间内维持该界限并抵御自适应攻击者，但不覆盖任意对抗变换。此外后验可验证性正在退化：3000张Reddit图像中1116张无法被2022年生成器复现，而2024年生成器仅55–79张无法复现。

Conclusion: 内容本身不足以判定来源，检测应从"真伪二分类"转向"真实性是否可合理否认"的可校准证明式判定；该范式在强对抗与误认证控制上显著优于现有基线，但仅在有界扰动假设下成立，且随着生成器能力增强，后验可验证性持续削弱，检测问题将愈发困难。

Abstract: Generative models can synthesize high-quality inauthentic multimedia content that is already being misused at scale. We evaluate twenty deepfake detectors against ten generators released in the last four years and find accuracy decreasing over time, from near-perfect 99.5% to 76%. Adversarial perturbations further reduce every baseline detector to below 2% accuracy, effectively inverting the detector's assigned label. We argue that this unreliability reflects a fundamental ambiguity: generators can reproduce authentic content exactly (e.g., through memorization), so content alone cannot reveal the true provenance this http URL this reason, content produced by a generator must admit a faithful reconstruction by that same generator, and finding such a reconstruction makes synthetic provenance plausible and authenticity plausibly this http URL therefore propose and evaluate a detection paradigm that outputs a calibrated prediction of whether authenticity is plausibly deniable: a faithful reconstruction by any known generator establishes plausible deniability, while calibration bounds how often content from known generators fails to be reproduced. Our evaluation shows that (i) our detector can be calibrated so that at most 1% of generated content is wrongly certified, an operating point at which most baseline detectors reach near-zero recall, including the strongest with 93% accuracy; (ii) calibrating a stricter security threshold on attacked samples preserves this bound against adaptive adversaries within the evaluated bounded-perturbation attack space, whose perturbations break every baseline, but does not cover arbitrary adversarial transformations; and (iii) post-hoc verifiability is eroding, as 1,116 of 3,000 Reddit images resist reproduction by a 2022 generator, but only 55 to 79 resist reproduction by 2024 generators.

</details>


### [158] [fMRI-TAMCL: Text-Anchored Supervised Multimodal Contrastive Learning for fMRI-Based Brain Disorder Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05880)
*Juliana Mantebea Danso,Enoch Opanin Gyamfi,Mylene C.Q. Farias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 fMRI-TAMCL：以生成式文本为锚，将 rs-fMRI 图像、稀疏功能连接与受试者特定文本三模态统一编码为聚类图，投影到共享单位超球面并用文本锚定的监督对比学习对齐，实现跨 5 个数据集的脑疾病分类泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: rs-fMRI 分类面临高度多模态与多站点异质性困难；rs-fMRI 数据集普遍缺少文本模态，现有方法用表型或 BOLD 激活生成文本，但依赖对受试者、站点、设备、协议的固定假设，跨数据集泛化差。

Method: 构建文本锚定的多模态对比学习框架：（1）Subject-Adaptive Threshold Derivation 模块生成 BOLD 激活文本；（2）Feature-Value Serialization 模块生成表型文本；三模态（fMRI 图像、稀疏 FC、生成文本）均编码为聚类图，映射到共享单位超球面空间，通过成对、文本锚定的监督对比学习对齐，并以注意力机制融合后用于下游分类。

Result: 在五个数据集上验证泛化能力，优于 29 个基线方法，下游分类准确率 78.6%–86.4%。

Conclusion: 以生成文本为语义锚点进行多模态图对比对齐，可缓解固定假设带来的站点/协议依赖，提升 rs-fMRI 脑疾病分类的跨数据集泛化性。

Abstract: Resting-state fMRI is important in the classification of brain disorders, but highly multimodal and exhibits strong multisite heterogeneity. Existing methods fuse images, BOLD-based functional connectivity, and phenotypic data modalities. Unlike other medical imaging datasets, rs-fMRI datasets rarely include a text modality, so they are generated from phenotypic data or BOLD activations. These text generation methods rely on fixed assumptions for subjects, sites, devices, and protocols, leading to poor generalization across datasets. We propose fMRI-TAMCL, a text-anchored multimodal contrastive learning framework that integrates fMRI images, sparse FC, and generated subject-specific text. Its Subject-Adaptive Threshold Derivation module generates BOLD activation text, while Feature-Value Serialization module generates phenotypic text. All three modalities are encoded as clustered graphs, projected onto a shared unit hypersphere space, aligned using pairwise, text-anchored supervised contrastive learning, and fused with attention. fMRI-TAMCL proves its generalization capability across five datasets outperforming 29 baselines with 78.6%-86.4% accuracy in downstream classification.

</details>


### [159] [Spatial Supervision Without Attribution Optimization: Improving Post-Hoc Class Activation Maps via Box-Guided Evidence Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05891)
*Wenhao Liang,Liangwei Nathan Zheng,Lin Yue,Wei Emma Zhang,Mingyu Guo,Weitong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Box-Guided Evidence Routing (BGER) 通过在特征图上加轻量门控并在框/掩码监督下训练，使分类器自身的 Grad-CAM 定位更准，而无需优化归因图本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规训练不约束 CAM 的空间合理性，作者探究能否用低成本的空间监督（边界框/掩码）提升分类器预测类 Grad-CAM 的质量，同时不把归因图放入训练目标。

Method: 在最终特征图上训练轻量门控，用框或掩码监督（BCE 路由损失），分类经由门控特征；Grad-CAM 单独在门控前的表示上计算，因此被评估的图从不进入训练损失。并设置对照实验：分类绕开门控、门控处梯度截断、直接监督 CAM。

Result: CUB-200-2011 上 MaxBoxAccV2 从 0.584 升至 0.715，Stanford Dogs 上从 0.757 升至 0.832，精度基本持平。对照组显示 ResNet-50 的增益主要来自空间监督重塑骨干而非路由本身（绕开门控仍保留大部分提升，梯度截断几乎无影响）；DenseNet-121 胸部 X 光两种设置下截断保留大部分增益，但 Swin-T 上增益消失；直接监督 CAM 定位更强但精度代价更大。

Conclusion: 空间监督可改善独立评估的 post-hoc CAM，但收益机制与幅度依赖具体架构和评测场景。

Abstract: Post-hoc class activation maps (CAMs) are a standard tool for inspecting the evidence behind an image classifier's predictions, yet nothing in ordinary training encourages these maps to be spatially appropriate. We study whether inexpensive spatial supervision can improve a classifier's own predicted-class Grad-CAM without ever optimizing an attribution map. Box-Guided Evidence Routing (BGER) trains a lightweight gate on the final feature map under box or mask supervision and routes classification through the gated features, while Grad-CAM is computed separately at the pre-gate representation, so the evaluated map never enters the training objective. With a BCE routing loss, BGER raises MaxBoxAccV2 from $0.584$ to $0.715$ on CUB-200-2011 and from $0.757$ to $0.832$ on Stanford Dogs at comparable accuracy. Matched controls attribute most of the ResNet-50 gain to the spatial supervision reshaping the backbone rather than to routing itself: when classification bypasses the gate, most of the improvement remains, and detaching gradients through the gate leaves the ResNet-50 result nearly unchanged. The same detachment preserves most of the gain in two DenseNet-121 chest X-ray settings but removes the apparent gain on Swin-T, and directly supervising the CAM reaches stronger localization at a larger accuracy cost. Overall, spatial supervision can improve separately evaluated post-hoc CAMs, but both the mechanism and the size of the benefit depend on the architecture and the evaluation setting.

</details>


### [160] [TasteRoute: Personalized Routing for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05896)
*Zhi Rui Tam,Chao-Chung Wu,Sin-Han Yang,Peyton Ku,Brendan Kuang,Tzu-Ting Hsieh,Min-Fang Hsu,Fang-Ling Tsai,Yun-Nung Chen,Wei-Chiu Ma,Chieh-Yen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出个性化视频生成路由器 TasteRoute，联合输入请求、用户偏好与生成预算选择模型，并发布 TasteRoute-3k 人类标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型在能力与成本上差异巨大，单一模型难以兼顾质量与开销；同时作者发现即使以其他标注者的共识作为 oracle，其与各标注者自身偏好的一致率也仅 34-55%，说明"偏好"高度个性化，无法用统一最优解替代。

Method: 将路由建模为基于三要素（输入请求、用户画像/偏好信号、可用生成预算）的联合选择问题；在文本到视频与图像到视频两种设定下评估，并与强基线做偏好路由与成本对比；构建含多模型视频对比、质量判断、偏好排序和用户画像信号的 TasteRoute-3k 标注数据集。

Result: 在 T2V 与 I2V 场景下，TasteRoute 在偏好路由指标上与强简单基线相当，同时降低平均生成成本；预算上限越高，成本节省越明显。

Conclusion: 个性化且成本感知的模型路由是可行且有效的，作者开源数据集以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Rapid progress in video generation has led to a plethora of models that differ substantially in capability and generation cost. This raises a natural question: can each request be efficiently routed to an appropriate model? We find that even when the consensus of the other annotators is used as an oracle, it agrees with each annotator's own favorite only 34-55% of the time. Motivated by this observation, we introduce TasteRoute, a personalized video-generation router that selects a generator jointly based on the input request, user preferences, and available generation budget. Across text-to-video and image-to-video settings, TasteRoute is competitive with strong simple baselines on preference routing while reducing average generation cost. The cost saving increases under higher budget caps. Finally, we release TasteRoute-3k, a human-annotated dataset containing multi-model video comparisons, quality judgments, preference rankings, and user-profile signals to facilitate future research on personalized and cost-aware video routing.

</details>


### [161] [Fitting Vision Adapters at Frontier Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05897)
*Jaehoon Lee,Harry Partridge,Mudith Jayasekara,Charles O'Neill,Max Kirkby,Michael Psenka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究在冻结的视觉编码器与语言模型之间训练小型投影器（50M）为纯语言模型注入视觉能力的规模化规律，发现部分视觉能力可迁移，部分存在瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型参数规模急剧扩大，但传统"冻结视觉编码器+投影器"范式能注入哪些视觉能力、随语言侧规模如何缩放，尚缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 将 Kimi K2.6 视觉编码器经 50M 参数投影器接入无原生视觉能力的 GLM 5.2/5.3，保持预训练权重冻结，并给出可复现的大规模适配器训练配方。在 MMMU-Pro 与 BLINK 上评测整体及单项视觉任务表现。

Result: 实验表明视觉能力随语言模型侧规模呈一定缩放趋势，但不同视觉任务可迁移性差异明显，部分能力仍受限。

Conclusion: 投影器式多模态适配可在规模化语言模型上补充部分视觉能力，但并非全部视觉能力均可被有效注入，需针对任务差异设计策略。

Abstract: Training a small projector between a frozen vision encoder and language model is an established approach to multimodal learning. As the parameter count of language models scales dramatically, we revisit which vision capabilities this approach can add while keeping their pretrained weights fixed. Here we train a 50M parameter projector from the vision encoder of Kimi K2.6 to GLM 5.2 and 5.3, both models without native vision capabilities, and further present a reproducible recipe for training these adapters at scale. We study the following: (a) how vision capabilities of multimodal models scale as purely the language model side scales, and (b) what specific vision capabilities are able to be imbued into a pure language model at scale, and which ones remain limited. We evaluate on MMMU-Pro and BLINK, examining both overall performance and results on individual visual tasks.

</details>


### [162] [LoDEOT: Low-Dimensional and Efficient Offset Tokens for Building Footprint Extraction from Off-Nadir Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05899)
*Kai Li,Zigan Zhou,Zhenyang Li,Hui Shan,Zhe Chen,Yupeng Deng,Zhihao Xi,Yu Meng,Yifan Peng,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoDEOT 用五维偏移 token 替代高维实例 token 预测屋顶到足迹的二维偏移量，在五个建筑数据集上提升足迹提取精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从离轴（off-nadir）遥感影像提取建筑足迹时，实例级的屋顶-足迹偏移（RFO）预测是关键。现有 query-based 方法用高维实例 token 回归二维有符号偏移，缺乏结构先验，冗余且监督信号分散。

Method: 在局部针孔投影与垂直拉伸假设下，推导出理想 RFO 图存在五参数充分描述子（本征形状、复合幅值、相对几何），据此设计五维 offset token：检测与分割仍用高维实例 token，但将实例 token、浓度门控屋顶证据、box-mask 证据映射到五维 offset token，再由独立二维读出层回归偏移；并利用去噪 query 的目标索引使各监督解码层估计对齐同一干净实例 RFO。

Result: 在 BONAI 上取得最优屋顶检测 bAP 与 bAP50，五项偏移校正足迹指标均居评估的端到端方法之首，FAP50 为 54.58、mEPE 为 5.23 像素，FAP50 较基线高 7.56–16.85 个百分点。

Conclusion: 紧凑的五维 offset token 足以支持精确 RFO 预测，结构化低维先验优于高维实例 token 回归，可有效提升离轴影像的建筑足迹提取。

Abstract: Instance-level roof-to-footprint offset (RFO) prediction is central to extracting building footprints from off-nadir imagery. Query-based pipelines commonly use high-dimensional instance tokens to predict signed two-dimensional RFOs. We investigate whether RFO prediction can instead use a compact offset token. Under local pinhole projection and vertical-extrusion assumptions, the idealized RFO map admits a five-parameter sufficient descriptor comprising intrinsic shape, composite amplitude, and relative geometry. This factorization provides a structural prior for a five-dimensional offset token, whose channels learn task-relevant latent representations through end-to-end training. Based on this design, we propose LoDEOT, which retains high-dimensional instance tokens for detection and segmentation but maps instance-token, concentration-gated roof, and box-mask evidence to a five-dimensional offset token followed by an independent two-dimensional readout. Known denoising-query target indices further align each supervised decoder-layer estimate with the same clean instance RFO, organizing successive predictions as target-aligned recovery under perturbed query conditions. Experiments on five real-world building datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of LoDEOT for building footprint extraction. Experiments on real-world building datasets demonstrate that a five-dimensional offset token can support accurate RFO prediction. On BONAI, LoDEOT achieves the best roof-detection bAP and bAP50 and leads all five offset-corrected footprint metrics among the evaluated end-to-end methods, with FAP50 of 54.58 and mEPE of 5.23 pixels. Its FAP50 exceeds those of the evaluated end-to-end baselines by 7.56-16.85 percentage points.

</details>


### [163] [End-to-End Autonomous Recursive Arborescence Deformable Flow and Non-Linear Hemodynamics for Patient-Specific Coronary Centerline Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05900)
*Zeyu Jia,Xin Ming*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出端到端框架，将连续血管树几何生成与非线性血流动力学解耦：三维开口定位头、可变形步进流架构、自顶向下递归树状态机与Picard非线性Kirchhoff求解器，实现单连通无环血管树与机器精度质量守恒，并揭示线性Poiseuille流对狭窄缺血的严重误判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体数据中提取患者特异性血管树是计算血管造影与无创血流动力学评估的基础。现有体素分割易断裂细微分叉，启发式欧氏最小生成树引入非解剖学捷径，且线性Poiseuille流忽略狭窄处二次动能耗散，导致缺血被低估。

Method: 四项模块化设计：(1) 自主3D开口标志定位头，采用双窦查询通道与球面门控细化，摆脱中心线种子依赖；(2) 空间锚定可变形步进流架构，通过三线性采样查询连续3D特征金字塔，逐序生成轨迹并施加锚点边界约束X(0)=P_start；(3) 自顶向下递归树状态机，以Tree-NMS检测分叉峰并参数化前驱父指针(p_k<k)，保证单连通无环拓扑(beta_0=1, beta_1=0)与可微步终止；(4) 迭代Picard非线性Kirchhoff求解器，结合Young-Tsai/Gould二次耗散项。

Result: 开口定位队列平均误差7.63 mm（LCA 7.43 mm，RCA 7.83 mm，71.4%≤8.0 mm）。非线性求解器质量守恒残差5.82e-11 mL/s。14例开发患者在标准化仿真狭窄应力测试(Q_0=4.0 mL/s)下：线性Poiseuille流将75%直径病变误判为非缺血(FFR>0.80)，14/14例均误判；非线性求解器捕捉功能性缺血(FFR=0.5864，病变压差32.89 mmHg，p=6.10e-5)，侧支分流3.66倍。测试集保持防火墙隔离。

Conclusion: 将几何树生成与非线性血流动力学解耦的端到端框架，可在保证拓扑合法性与质量守恒的同时显著提升狭窄缺血检出能力，表明线性流动假设在功能性评估中不可靠。

Abstract: Extracting patient-specific vascular trees from volumetric medical images is fundamental to computational angiography and non-invasive hemodynamic assessment. Conventional voxel segmentation models often sever delicate bifurcations, while heuristic Euclidean Minimum Spanning Trees introduce non-anatomical shortcuts. Moreover, linear Poiseuille flow neglects quadratic kinetic dissipation across arterial narrowings, underestimating ischemia. We formulate an end-to-end framework decoupling continuous geometric arborescence generation from non-linear hemodynamics. First, an autonomous 3D Ostium Landmark Localization Head with dual-sinus query channels and spherical-gated refinement eliminates centerline seeding dependency, achieving cohort mean localization error of 7.63 mm (7.43 mm LCA, 7.83 mm RCA; 71.4% <= 8.0 mm) from raw contrast context. Second, a Spatially-Grounded Deformable Step Flow Architecture queries continuous 3D feature pyramids via trilinear sampling, sequentially generating trajectories with anchor boundary enforcement (X(0) = P_start). Third, a Top-Down Recursive Arborescence State Machine detects bifurcation peaks via Tree-NMS and parameterizes predecessor parent pointers (p_k < k), guaranteeing single connected acyclic tree topology (beta_0 = 1, beta_1 = 0) with differentiable step termination. Fourth, an iterative Picard non-linear Kirchhoff solver with Young-Tsai / Gould quadratic dissipation enforces machine-precision mass conservation (residual 5.82e-11 mL/s). Across 14 development patients under standardized in-silico stenosis stress testing (Q_0 = 4.0 mL/s), linear Poiseuille flow misclassifies 75% diameter lesions as non-ischemic (FFR > 0.80) in 14/14 cases, whereas our non-linear solver captures functional ischemia (FFR = 0.5864, lesion disparity 32.89 mmHg, p = 6.10e-5) with 3.66x collateral shunting. Test set firewall isolation was maintained.

</details>


### [164] [Safe Image Generation via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05908)
*Eungyeol Han,Jong-Seok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种"生成中"安全框架，通过监测扩散去噪轨迹中的中间表征检测NSFW信号，并用强化学习实现对不安全轨迹的可控引导，从而在保持图像质量与提示保真度的同时生成安全图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I安全机制主要局限于生成前过滤（提示词文本分类器）或生成后审核（合成完成后的图像审查），而对抗攻击可作用于更广泛的生成空间。这种"防护窄、攻击宽"的不对称暴露了在生成过程中介入的安全需求。

Method: 1）在扩散模型去噪轨迹上做过程监控，从中间表征中检测新显现的NSFW信号；2）将生成中检测与可控引导（controllable steering）耦合，用强化学习从NSFW提示生成安全图像，使在生成已开始后出现的NSFW信号也能被纠正。

Result: 在标准与对抗评测集上一致优于现有安全生成方法，同时保持感知质量与提示保真度。代码将在录用后开源。

Conclusion: 安全防护应从事前/事后审核转向生成过程内的检测与干预，轨迹级监测加RL引导是兼顾安全性与生成质量的有效范式。

Abstract: Recent Text-to-Image (T2I) models achieve remarkable visual image generation performance, but they can still generate NSFW (Not-Safe-For-Work) contents, including violent or explicit images. Existing safety checker mechanisms are largely confined to pre-generation filtering (e.g. prompt-level text classifiers) or post-hoc moderation applied after an image is completely synthesized. However, adversarial attack methods operate over a much broader space. This imbalance highlights the need for a safety mechanism that intervenes during the generation process. We propose an in-generation safety framework that monitors the denoising trajectory and detects emerging NSFW signals from intermediate representations. Rather than merely detecting NSFW generations, our method applies reinforcement learning to generate safe images from NSFW prompts. By coupling in-generation detection with controllable steering, our approach mitigates unsafe trajectories even when NSFW signals emerge after generation has already begun. Experiments results show that our method consistently outperforms existing safe image generation methods across both standard and adversarial evaluation sets, while preserving perceptual quality and prompt fidelity. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [165] [AstraSR: Real-World Thermal Super-Resolution with GPT-6 Astra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05910)
*Mengyuan Li,Changhong Fu,Jun Zhang,Ziyu Lu,Yuhang Zhang,Haobo Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AstraSR：用GPT-6生成HR参考，对真实LR热红外图像做直接生成式监督，实现真实场景热成像超分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实热红外超分受限于传感器分辨率低，且难以获得配对的高分辨率观测用于监督。传统方法把带退化的实拍图当作HR参考并施加预定义退化合成LR，既引入合成LR与实拍LR之间的域差，又把采集退化带入监督信号。

Method: 以实拍LR热图像为条件，驱动GPT-6 Astra多模态生成模型产出HR参考，构建图像对数据集；采用直接生成式监督策略，用像素损失、梯度损失与感知损失联合约束，从生成参考中迁移强度分布、结构边界与视觉细节。

Result: 与7种现有真实世界SR方法定性比较，热成像场景中呈现连续目标轮廓、清晰结构边界和平滑强度过渡，在热成像清晰度与结构一致性上优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 用生成式多模态大模型替代传统退化建模来提供HR监督，可缓解域差与退化监督问题，为真实热红外超分提供可行路线。

Abstract: Real-world thermal super-resolution (SR) is constrained by limited sensor resolution and the difficulty of obtaining corresponding high-resolution (HR) observations for direct model supervision. Conventional SR methods typically construct training pairs by treating captured thermal images with real-world degradations as HR references and applying predefined degradation to generate synthetic low-resolution (LR) inputs. Such a construction not only introduces a domain gap between synthetic and captured LR observations but also retains acquisition degradations in the supervision. To address this issue, we propose AstraSR, a real-world thermal SR method guided by GPT-6 Astra, a frontier multimodal generative model endowed with emergent and transformative visual capabilities. Specifically, we construct a dataset of image pairs by using captured LR thermal images to condition GPT-based HR reference. We develop a direct generative supervision strategy that learns from captured thermal inputs paired with GPT-generated HR references. Pixel, gradient, and perceptual losses jointly supervise the transfer of intensity patterns, structural boundaries, and visual details from the generated references. Qualitative comparisons with seven existing state-of-the-art real-world SR methods show continuous object contours, distinct structural boundaries, and smooth intensity transitions in the thermal scenes. These results demonstrate that AstraSR outperforms existing real-world SR methods in both thermal clarity and structural coherence.

</details>


### [166] [Every View Counts: View-Consistent Panoptic Quality for Multi-view Panoptic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05911)
*Youngmin Lee,Byungha Ko,Guhnoo Yun,Dong Hwan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VC-PQ 指标，将全景质量从单图像扩展到多视图集合，按实例可见视图数等权计分并惩罚视图不一致的预测，并给出损失分解，主张与 PQ^scene 并列报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈多视图全景分割模型沿用逐场景优化方法中的 PQ^scene，该指标把所有视图拼成一张大图，漏检或 ID 变化只按面积比例扣分，无法体现"视图一致性"这一多视图任务的核心属性，导致评估与任务目标错位。

Method: 提出 View-Consistent Panoptic Quality（VC-PQ）：从单图像 PQ 扩展到输入视图集合，对每个实例可见的视图等权计数，并惩罚与真值可见视图集合不一致的预测。对 VC-PQ 进行分解，将失分归因于掩码精度、视图一致性、匹配阈值；引入一个额外参数以退化为 tiling 的面积加权。在 ScanNet++ 与 ScanNetv2 上采用固定协议对比前馈方法与 PQ^scene，并对真值做受控扰动分析。

Result: 分解显示各前馈方法的失分来源不同（掩码精度 / 视图一致性 / 匹配阈值）。受控扰动实验表明：VC-PQ 对实例被漏检或 ID 改变的视图数量敏感，而 PQ^scene 只对其面积敏感。

Conclusion: VC-PQ 使视图一致性成为多视图全景分割评估的一部分，建议在报告 PQ^scene 的同时一并报告 VC-PQ；本质是评估协议贡献，不改变模型本身。

Abstract: Multi-view panoptic segmentation assigns a semantic class and a scene-level instance ID to every pixel of an unordered set of images, and recent feed-forward 3D models predict these labels for the input views in a single forward pass. Their predictions, however, have been evaluated with the scene-level PQ (PQ^scene) borrowed from per-scene optimization methods, typically on rendered held-out views. PQ^scene tiles all views of a scene into a single image, so that a missed appearance or a change of ID lowers the score of the matched pair only in proportion to its area. We propose View-Consistent Panoptic Quality (VC-PQ), which extends PQ from a single image to a set of input views, counts equally every view in which an instance is visible, and penalizes a prediction that is not visible in the same views as its ground truth. A decomposition of VC-PQ attributes the score a method loses to mask accuracy, view consistency, and the matching threshold. A single additional parameter recovers the area weighting of tiling for comparison. Under a fixed evaluation protocol on ScanNet++ and ScanNetv2, recent feed-forward methods are evaluated with VC-PQ and PQ^scene, and the decomposition shows where each of them loses its score. Controlled perturbations of the ground truth show that VC-PQ responds to the number of views in which an instance is missed or changes ID, whereas PQ^scene responds to their area. The aim of this work is to make view consistency part of the evaluation of multi-view panoptic segmentation, with VC-PQ reported alongside PQ^scene.

</details>


### [167] [Prompt and Refinement: Asymmetric Mutual Learning for Infrared Small Target Detection with Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05918)
*Yimin Fu,Songbo Wang,Lizhuo Liu,Baicheng Pan,Zhunga Liu,Michael K. Ng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PAR 范式：用预训练 SAM 与从零训练的 ISTD 检测器构成非对称互学习框架，通过互为修正线索的监督掩码、局部对比度正则与证据不确定性估计，实现抗标签噪声的红外小目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标像素级标注成本高、依赖专家知识且目标可分性弱，真实场景中训练标签必然含噪，噪声标签会把目标感知学习引向虚假模式，因此需要一种对标签噪声鲁棒的 ISTD 学习范式。

Method: PAR 由预训练 SAM 与从零训练的 ISTD 专用检测器组成，通过 peer-teaching 协同学习；结合局部对比度正则，两非对称模型的预测互为对方监督掩码的修正线索，避免单一模型自确认循环；检测器预测作为纠正性掩码提示，促进视觉基础模型的任务适配；并在优化中引入证据不确定性估计以缓解噪声标签影响。

Result: 在三个 ISTD 数据集、多种噪声标签场景下的实验表明，PAR 持续取得 state-of-the-art 性能。

Conclusion: 非对称互学习与互补归纳偏置的交互可有效抑制标签修正的自确认退化，使标注向目标内在特征渐进精化，为弱标注红外小目标检测提供了抗噪方案。

Abstract: Existing data-driven infrared small target detection (ISTD) methods typically require large-scale datasets with accurate pixel-level annotations for model training. However, such labor-intensive requirements are difficult to satisfy in real-world applications due to the heavy reliance on expert knowledge and the inherently weak distinctiveness of infrared small targets. Consequently, the presence of noisy labels during model training is inevitable, which can severely mislead the learning of target perception toward spurious patterns. To address this challenge, we propose Prompt and Refinement (PAR), a label-noise-robust asymmetric mutual learning paradigm for ISTD. Specifically, PAR comprises a pretrained Segment Anything Model (SAM) and an ISTD-specific detector trained from scratch, which learn collaboratively through a peer-teaching scheme. Coupled with local contrast regularity, the predictions of the two asymmetric peer models are mutually exploited as rectification cues for the supervisory masks of their counterparts. The interaction between complementary inductive biases effectively prevents the label correction process from degenerating into the self-confirmation loop of a single model, enabling progressive refinement of the annotations toward intrinsic target characteristics. In addition, the detector predictions are utilized as corrective mask prompts to facilitate task-specific adaptation of the vision foundation model.
