Motivation: 早产儿自发运动分析日益依赖无标记姿态估计（PE）获取临床运动生物标志物，而训练准确的婴儿PE模型需大量人工标注关键点，标注本身易出错；错位关键点会作为伪影传播到重建的关节轨迹中，对临床分析危害显著。因此亟需自动检测噪声关键点标注的方法。

Method: 借鉴噪声标签学习中的小损失假设与基于训练动态的样本选择，训练一个混合卷积-注意力模型，从关键点的空间坐标与局部视觉特征预测其解剖类别；由交叉熵训练动态导出逐关键点描述子，再用无监督聚类划分为干净与噪声子集。在自采的65名住院早产儿数据集NeoPose上，于随机位置扰动与左右交换两种噪声场景、多个噪声水平下验证，并扩展到COCO基准。

Result: 噪声关键点检测F1最高达91.9%；在COCO上，从训练集中滤除CE检测出的噪声关键点后，在中高噪声水平下下游姿态估计精度提升最高达7.4 AP。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效识别并过滤噪声关键点标注，既适用于特定临床婴儿姿态估计场景，也可泛化到通用基准，说明训练动态驱动的样本选择在关键点标注清洗中具有实用价值，可提升下游PE精度。

Abstract: Spontaneous movement analysis in preterm infants relies increasingly on markerless pose estimation (PE) to derive clinically relevant motion biomarkers directly from video recordings. Training accurate infant PE models requires large sets of manually annotated keypoints, and human annotation is inherently prone to error. Noisy keypoints (i.e., keypoints mislocalized with respect to their true anatomical position) are especially problematic in this clinical setting, since they can propagate as artificial artifacts into the reconstructed joint trajectories. Building on the small-loss hypothesis and training-dynamics-based sample selection established in the noisy-label learning literature, we propose a novel framework for detecting noisy keypoint annotations. A hybrid convolutional-attention model is trained to predict the anatomical category of each keypoint from its spatial coordinates and local visual features; the resulting cross-entropy training dynamics are then used to derive per-keypoint descriptors, which are partitioned into clean and noisy subsets via unsupervised clustering. We validate the approach on NeoPose, a newly collected dataset of 65 hospitalized preterm infants, under two realistic noise scenarios (random positional perturbation and left-right swapping) across multiple noise levels. Results show that the proposed approach achieves an F1-score of up to 91.9% in noisy-keypoint detection. The framework further generalizes to the heterogeneous COCO benchmark, where filtering CE-detected noisy keypoints from the training set also yields measurable improvements (up to 7.4 AP points) in downstream pose estimation accuracy at moderate-to-high noise levels.

</details>


### [204] [MaRO-GS: Mask-Robust Object-Centric Gaussian Splatting from Inconsistent Multi-view Masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06472)
*Eunji Kim,Gahyeon Kim,Gianella Cravioto,Dong-hun Lee,Chaewon Moon,Chae-yeong Song,Sang-hyo Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaRO-GS：面向多视图图像的目标物体级3DGS重建框架，通过掩码可靠性视图过滤、物体支持的稠密化控制与轮廓对齐物体损失，实现抗监督不一致的直接物体高斯优化，提升PSNR、分割精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体级3DGS方法重建整个场景而非直接优化目标物体，计算开销大；且依赖2D分割掩码关联高斯与物体，而掩码在跨视图间常不一致，导致错误的监督信号污染高斯优化，降低物体重建保真度。

Method: 提出MaRO-GS框架，直接在物体掩码的多视图图像上优化目标物体高斯。三个关键组件：(1) 掩码可靠性视图过滤，剔除不可靠视图以提供可靠监督；(2) 物体支持的高斯稠密化控制，抑制与目标物体无关的高斯并防止背景过度稠密化；(3) 轮廓对齐物体损失（Silhouette-aligned Object Loss），维持以物体为中心的优化。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，MaRO-GS在PSNR、分割精度和计算效率上均有提升，在小型物体数据集LERF-Mask上取得最大PSNR增益2.05 dB。

Conclusion: MaRO-GS通过视图过滤、稠密化控制与轮廓对齐损失，使物体级3DGS对不一致监督具备鲁棒性，实现更精确高效的物体重建，适用于多视图物体重建任务。

Abstract: We address the challenge of accurate 3D object reconstruction from multi-view images in Gaussian Splatting. Existing object-level 3DGS methods reconstruct the entire scene rather than directly optimizing the target object, even when only the target object is needed, which incurs substantial computational overhead. They also rely on 2D segmentation masks to associate Gaussians with objects, but these masks are often inconsistent across views. Such inconsistencies corrupt Gaussian optimization and produce incorrectly supervised Gaussians that degrade object reconstruction fidelity. To overcome these limitations, we propose MaRO-GS, a 3DGS framework that directly optimizes target-object Gaussians from object-masked multi-view images and remains robust to inconsistent supervision. For reliable supervision, mask-reliability view filtering excludes unreliable views. Object-supported Gaussian density control suppresses Gaussians irrelevant to the target object and prevents background densification, while Silhouette-aligned Object Loss maintains object-focused optimization. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate that MaRO-GS improves PSNR, segmentation accuracy, and computational efficiency, with the largest PSNR gain of 2.05 dB on the small-object LERF-Mask dataset.

</details>


### [205] [Topology-Informed Prompt-Conditioned Universal Segmentation of Uterine Structures from Ultrasound and MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06494)
*Yongheng Sun,Yuexi Gu,Jingwen Sun,Maureen Kohi,Mingxia Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出拓扑感知提示条件通用分割框架TPUS，用图神经网络骨干+任务提示在超声和MRI上联合分割多种子宫结构，Dice达0.898与0.693。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子宫多结构分割对妇科疾病计算机辅助诊疗重要，超声与MRI信息互补；但跨模态统一建模面临图像外观、解剖上下文、空间分辨率、标签空间差异大，且各数据集分割目标不一致，联合学习易负迁移。

Method: TPUS包含：基于图的多数据集骨干（模态专属stem + 模态共享图编解码器）实现模态敏感输入适配与结构特征推理；任务感知类别提示对分割过程进行条件化；动态卷积适配模块生成任务特定输出；拓扑感知损失约束解剖一致性。

Result: 在子宫超声数据集与T2加权子宫肌瘤MRI数据集上，两个留出测试集Dice分别为0.898和0.693，优于多种通用与universal分割基线。

Conclusion: TPUS验证了跨模态、跨任务异构标签空间下统一子宫分割的可行性，拓扑约束与提示条件化是关键增益来源。

Abstract: Multi-structure segmentation of the uterus is important for computer-assisted screening, diagnosis, and treatment planning of uterine diseases, where ultrasound and MRI provide complementary clinical information. However, developing a unified model across these modalities is challenging due to their substantially different image appearances, anatomical contexts, spatial resolutions, and label spaces. Moreover, existing datasets often define different segmentation targets, making joint learning challenging and potentially leading to negative transfer across heterogeneous tasks. To this end, we propose a Topology-informed Prompt-conditioned Universal Segmentation (TPUS) framework for segmenting multiple uterine structures across ultrasound and MRI. TPUS introduces a graph-based multi-dataset backbone comprising modality-specific stems and a modality-shared graph-based encoder-decoder to support modality-sensitive input adaptation, structural feature reasoning, and joint representation learning across heterogeneous uterine segmentation tasks. In addition, TPUS uses task-aware class prompts to condition the segmentation process for different datasets and label spaces, a dynamic convolutional adaptation module to generate task-specific output responses, and a topology-informed loss to encourage anatomically consistent predictions. Experiments on a uterine ultrasound dataset and a T2-weighted uterine myoma MRI dataset demonstrate that TPUS achieves Dice scores of 0.898 and 0.693 on the two held-out test sets, respectively, outperforming several generic and universal segmentation baselines. Source code can be accessed at this https URL .

</details>


### [206] [NeuroCBIR: A Fast and Accurate Image Retrieval System for Whole-Brain and Region-Specific MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06502)
*Felix Nieto-del-Amor,Jingru Fu,J.-Sebastian Muehlboeck,Eric Westman,Daniel Ferreira,Rodrigo Moreno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeuroCBIR：基于VAE+对比学习的脑MRI检索框架，支持全脑与103个区域级3D T1w查询，跨数据集重识别mAP@5≥98.4%，CPU提取嵌入约18.7s，检索<0.01s，公开26,000+预计算嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经影像CBIR方法受限于小数据集、单一脑区或粗粒度类别标签，临床实用性与泛化性不足；作者希望实现快速、灵活、可用于个性化诊断支持的检索系统。

Method: 提取103个皮层与皮层下区域，构建全脑与区域级查询；用变分自编码器（VAE）的隐表征结合对比学习，生成刻画解剖模式的扫描特异性嵌入（embedding）。

Result: 被试重识别在全脑与区域层面均表现优异（mAP@5≥98.4%），跨数据集与采集条件泛化稳健；零样本年龄预测与多类病理分层显示嵌入包含下游可用信息；4核CPU上单扫描嵌入提取约18.7s，相似度检索<0.01s；已公开26,000+预计算T1w嵌入。

Conclusion: NeuroCBIR是面向脑MRI的可复现、区域灵活、具临床个性化诊断潜力的检索工具，代码与预计算嵌入已开源。

Abstract: Content-based image retrieval (CBIR) in neuroimaging enables the identification of structurally similar brain scans, supporting diagnosis, prognosis, and treatment planning; however, existing methods are often limited to small datasets, single brain regions, or coarse class labels, thereby restricting their clinical utility and generalizability. Here, we present NeuroCBIR, a framework for fast and flexible retrieval of both whole-brain and region-specific 3D T1w MRI scans. A total of 103 cortical and subcortical regions are extracted to enable both whole-brain and region-level queries. NeuroCBIR leverages latent representations learned by a variational autoencoder (VAE) combined with contrastive learning, producing scan-specific embeddings that capture anatomical patterns. These embeddings were evaluated for subject re-identification, zero-shot age prediction, and zero-shot multi-class pathology stratification. Re-identification performance was high across both whole-brain and brain-region levels (mean average precision across the top-5 retrieved images (mAP@5) >= 98.4%), with robust generalization across datasets and acquisition conditions. While NeuroCBIR is not trained for age prediction or pathology stratification, zero-shot evaluations for these two tasks demonstrate that the embeddings encode meaningful information for downstream tasks. Embedding extraction on a 4-core CPU required approximately 18.7 s per scan, whereas similarity search was effectively instantaneous (less than 0.01 s). NeuroCBIR is publicly available for brain MRI with more than 26,000 precomputed T1w MRI embeddings. It supports reproducible research, region-specific flexibility, and clinically meaningful personalized diagnostic support. The software is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [207] [Harmful Content Generation in Text-to-Image Models: Capabilities and Moderation Limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06503)
*Paschalis Giakoumoglou,Manos Schinas,Symeon Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对五个开源文生图模型进行系统性有害内容生成评测：用自动化流水线将新闻标题改写为五类有害提示，人工评估1500张生成图，揭示高有害生成率、社区微调变体的脆弱性、审核系统检测缺口以及合成图像检测器的语义分布偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图模型能生成高度逼真图像，带来被滥用的风险；虽有安全机制，但缺乏针对真实攻击场景的对其有效性的系统评估。

Method: 构建自动化评测流水线，将合法新闻标题转化为针对五类有害内容（色情、暴力/血腥、有害刻板印象、自残、仇恨言论）的提示词；在五个开源文生图模型上对比评测内置安全机制的常规模型与绕过限制的社区微调变体；对1500张生成图进行人工评估；进一步评测自动化审核系统与合成图像检测器。

Result: 有害内容生成率很高：血腥类89.2%、色情47.6%、刻板印象43.6%、仇恨言论46.0%、自残34.5%（以图像化暴力为主）；社区微调变体对色情提示尤为脆弱；有害提示下图像质量基本保持，FLUX.1-dev在30.9%的情况下生成明显逼真的有害图像；审核系统存在显著漏检；仅用良性数据训练的合成图像检测器在露骨内容上表现更差，训练数据多样化可提升检测。

Conclusion: 当前生成安全防护、审核系统与合成图像检测均存在明显局限，凸显了需要更强的规模化防滥用手段。

Abstract: Text-to-image generative models can produce highly realistic imagery but also raise concerns about harmful misuse. While safety mechanisms exist, systematic evaluations of their effectiveness against realistic attacks remain limited. We present a systematic evaluation of harmful content generation across five open text-to-image models using an automated pipeline that transforms legitimate news captions into unsafe prompts targeting sexually explicit content, violence/gore, harmful stereotypes, self-harm, and hate speech. We evaluate both standard models with built-in safety mechanisms and community fine-tuned variants that bypass content restrictions. A human evaluation of 1,500 generated images shows high harmful-content generation rates: 89.2% for gore-related prompts, 47.6% for sexually explicit content, 43.6% for harmful stereotypes, 46.0% for hate speech, and 34.5% for self-harm, predominantly through graphic violence. Models show substantial capability for generating violent and stereotypical content, while community fine-tuned variants are particularly vulnerable to sexually explicit prompts. Generation quality is largely preserved under harmful prompting, producing imagery of sufficient fidelity to pose risks for disinformation and abuse; FLUX.1-dev produces clearly realistic harmful images in 30.9% of cases. We further evaluate automated moderation systems and find substantial detection gaps that allow unsafe images to evade filtering. Finally, we assess synthetic image detectors and show that models trained only on benign datasets perform worse on explicit content, while more diverse training data improves detection, highlighting semantic distribution gaps in current approaches. These findings expose limitations in current generation safeguards, moderation systems, and synthetic image detection, highlighting the need for stronger defenses against misuse at scale.

</details>


### [208] [A General Pipeline for Dense Illuminant Estimation via Physically Based Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06508)
*Luca Cogo,Gianmarco Corti,Simone Bianco,Raimondo Schettini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种从基于物理的3D渲染场景中导出稠密光照色度图的通用可复用流水线，生成74,321张合成图像用于光照估计模型预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光照估计是计算摄影的基础问题，但基于学习的方法受限于缺乏大规模且具有准确光照真值的数据集。作者希望降低数据获取门槛。

Method: 复用现有3D场景集合，在可控光照条件下系统生成像素级光照标注，得到74,321张合成图像，用于单光照与多光照估计模型的预训练。

Result: 在多种SOTA架构上，合成预训练持续提升性能，单光照估计最高提升28%，多光照估计最高提升57%，在数据稀缺场景尤为明显。

Conclusion: 合成数据生成流水线为光照估计方法的预训练提供了有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Illuminant estimation is a fundamental problem in computational photography, as it enables the correction of color shifts induced by varying lighting conditions. While learning-based methods have demonstrated strong performance, their progress is hindered by the limited availability of large-scale datasets with accurate illuminant ground-truth. In this work, we propose a general and reusable pipeline to derive dense illuminant chromaticity maps from physically based 3D-rendered scenes. By repurposing an existing 3D scene collection, our approach enables the systematic generation of pixel-wise illuminant annotations under controlled lighting conditions, effectively lowering the barrier to data acquisition for learning-based illuminant estimation. Using this pipeline, we generate a large-scale synthetic set of 74,321 images, which we employ for pre-training both single- and multi-illuminant estimation models. Extensive experiments with state-of-the-art architectures show that synthetic pre-training consistently improves performance, with gains of up to 28% for single-illuminant estimation and up to 57% for multi-illuminant estimation, particularly in data-scarce regimes. These findings demonstrate that synthetic data generation pipelines offer an effective and scalable solution for the pre-training of illuminant estimation methods.

</details>


### [209] [BrainTRACE: Tracing Longitudinal, Multimodal, and Volumetric Evidence in Brain MRI Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06571)
*Qizhen Lan,Mengchen Fan,Hang Zhang,Jingwei Duan,Moule Lin,Jialin Chen,Baocheng Geng,Xiaoqian Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BrainTRACE，一个以报告为锚定、面向纵向脑MRI解读的VQA基准，含7273个计分实例、1778名纵向患者、约29k配准3D序列体数据，按五级临床推理与四类证据需求组织，评测20个VLM配置显示模型难以将孤立视觉线索组合为有据的纵向解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VQA与3D影像基准多以孤立图像、静态体数据或无证据锚定的报告式答案评测脑MRI，掩盖了支撑临床有效性证据链上的失败，无法衡量模型是否具备纵向比较、多序列整合与体积空间定位能力。

Method: 构建BrainTRACE基准：从1778名纵向患者、7299次MRI检查、约29k共配准3D序列体积中派生7273个计分VQA实例；按从采集识别到病例级综合的五级临床推理分层，并按纵向比较、报告锚定引用、多序列整合、体积空间证据四类证据需求划分；提供兼容标准VLM接口的渲染输入、3D证据条件，以及审计六步纵向证据链的分解式病例推理轨道。

Result: 对20个VLM配置的评测表明，当前系统能识别孤立视觉线索，但很少将其组合为有据的纵向解读。

Conclusion: BrainTRACE揭示了现有VLM在脑MRI纵向证据链推理上的系统性缺口，并公开基准规范、评测列表、评分实现、评分细则与审计记录格式，以支持可复现的脑MRI VLM评测进展。

Abstract: Brain MRI interpretation is a longitudinal clinical reasoning problem: radiologists compare serial studies, integrate information across MRI sequences, localize findings within volumetric anatomy, and translate this evidence into report-grounded assessments. Existing medical VQA and 3D imaging benchmarks capture important parts of this workflow, but often evaluate brain MRI through isolated images, static volumes, or ungrounded report-style answers, thereby obscuring failures in the evidence chain that support clinical validity. We introduce BrainTRACE, a report-grounded benchmark for evaluating whether vision-language models can trace the evidence structure required for longitudinal brain MRI interpretation. BrainTRACE contains 7,273 scored VQA instances derived from 1,778 longitudinal patients, 7,299 MRI studies, and approximately 29k co-registered 3D MRI sequence volumes. The benchmark is organized by five levels of clinical reasoning, from acquisition recognition to case-level synthesis, and by evidence demands covering longitudinal comparison, report-grounded references, multi-sequence integration, and volumetric spatial evidence. BrainTRACE supports rendered inputs compatible with standard VLM interfaces, a 3D-evidence condition, and a decomposed case-reasoning track that audits six steps in a longitudinal evidence chain. Evaluation of 20 VLM configurations shows that current systems can identify isolated visual cues but rarely compose them into grounded longitudinal interpretations. We release the benchmark specification, evaluation lists, scoring implementation, scoring rubrics, and audit-record format to support reproducible progress in brain MRI VLM evaluation.

</details>


### [210] [FrontVeg V2: A Training-Free Software Framework for Foreground-Aware Zero-Shot Plant Trait Segmentation in High-Resolution Images of Trellised Crops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06575)
*Abdoul Djalil Ousseini Hamza,Herearii Metuarea,Corentin Lothod{é},Morgane Roth,Jacem Ben Hamden,Eric Duch{ê}ne,Lionel Ley,David Alletru,David Rousseau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FrontVeg V2 是一个开源、免训练的前景感知零样本植物性状分割框架，结合单目深度估计、谷值感知深度阈值分割、分块零样本分割、图基掩码组装与几何感知融合，用于高分辨率棚架作物图像，并提供 CLII 与 Napari 两种使用方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 棚架作物高分辨率图像中，相邻植被行会造成大量误检，且不同作物与性状的标注成本高，难以针对每个任务重训练模型，因此需要一个免训练、前景感知、可复用的分割框架。

Method: 流水线包含四个核心组件：单目深度估计、基于谷值感知的深度阈值自动前景提取（Valley-Aware Depth Thresholding）、分块零样本分割（tiled zero-shot segmentation）、图基掩码组装（Graph-Based Mask Assembly），最后进行几何感知融合；当前实现集成 Depth Anything V2 与 SAM3，支持命令行批处理与 Napari 图形界面。

Result: 可在减少邻行植被误检的同时分割植物器官与病害症状；支持多作物、多性状数字表型分析而无需任务特定模型重训练。

Conclusion: FrontVeg V2 为农业数字表型提供了一个免训练、可复用的零样本分割框架，具备工程集成与交互式使用能力。

Abstract: FrontVeg V2 is an open-source, training-free software framework for foregroundaware zero-shot segmentation of plant traits in high-resolution images of trellised crops. The pipeline combines monocular depth estimation, automatic foreground extraction using Valley-Aware Depth Thresholding, tiled zero-shot segmentation, Graph-Based Mask Assembly, and geometry-aware fusion. This design enables plant organs and disease symptoms to be segmented while reducing detections arising from neighboring vegetation rows. The current implementation integrates Depth Anything V2 (DAV2) and SAM3 and can be used through both command-line batch processing and a Napari graphical interface. FrontVeg V2 provides a reusable framework for multi-crop, multi-trait digital phenotyping without task-specific model retraining.

</details>


### [211] [Keepsake: Selective Spatial Memory for Long-Horizon Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06588)
*Abdul Mohaimen Al Radi,Kunyang Li,Yuzhang Shang,Mubarak Shah,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Keepsake：免训练、在线、固定容量的空间记忆控制器，用位姿-外观图评估观测的"可替代性"动态决定保留/驱逐，使长时程相机可控视频生成在不改生成器的前提下大幅降低存储并提升一致性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程相机可控视频生成需要持久记忆保持场景一致性，但存在两难：全历史方法存储与检索开销无界；选择性构建方法一次性决策且从不复核，而观测价值随记忆库演化而改变。核心问题是：随生成历史演化，哪些已存观测仍应留在记忆中。作者的关键洞见是观测价值不是固定的，而是关系性的——取决于记忆库中当前可用的替代项。

Method: Keepsake 为免训练在线控制器，作用于固定容量空间记忆。每次更新时，在保留观测与新生成观测上构建位姿-外观图，融合相机位姿邻近度与视觉相似度；定义保留优先级，同时刻画强替代项数量与最近替代项的相似度，从而持续重估记忆价值：保留缺乏替代支持的观测，在固定预算下驱逐高度可替代者。仅修改持久记忆更新环节，宿主生成器、去噪调度与检索规则均不变。

Result: 在 MemCam 与 WorldMem 上，固定记忆预算下 FVD 与 LPIPS 均有提升；在 180 秒 MemCam 轨迹上仅保留 5,397 帧中的 32 帧，同时将 FVD 降低 35.1%。

Conclusion: 以"可替代性"为依据的在线记忆重估机制，可在固定预算下兼顾长时程一致性与存储效率，且与宿主生成器解耦、即插即用。

Abstract: Long-horizon camera-controlled video generation relies on persistent memory to maintain scene consistency. Existing systems follow two strategies to achieve this consistency. Full-history approaches retain all generated observations, causing unbounded storage and retrieval costs. Selective-construction approaches reduce redundancy, but make one-time retention decisions that are never revisited, even as an observation's value changes with the evolving memory bank. Both strategies leave a shared question unresolved: as the generated history evolves, which stored observations should still remain in memory? Our key insight is that the value of a stored observation is not fixed, but relational: it depends on the alternatives currently available in the memory bank. A view supported by many geometrically and visually similar substitutes can be relinquished with little loss of coverage, whereas an observation with few viable alternatives should remain regardless of age. We introduce Keepsake, an online, training-free controller for fixed-capacity spatial memory. At each update, Keepsake constructs a pose-appearance graph over retained and newly generated observations, combining camera-pose proximity with visual similarity. A retention priority jointly captures the number of strong substitutes and the similarity of the closest alternative, allowing Keepsake to continually reassess memory value, preserve observations with little alternative support, and evict highly replaceable ones under a fixed budget. The controller modifies only the persistent-memory update; the host generator, denoising schedule, and retrieval rule remain unchanged. Across MemCam and WorldMem, Keepsake improves FVD and LPIPS under a fixed memory budget. On 180-second MemCam trajectories, it retains only 32 of 5,397 frames while reducing FVD by 35.1%.

</details>


### [212] [VGGT-Bridge: Beyond Sequential Pose Graphs via Coarse-Stride Skip Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06594)
*Sungjae Choi,Hanna Bae,Sunghyun Baek,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGGT-Bridge 在免训练前提下为 chunk-and-align 长序列重建引入长程 skip edges、尺度漂移修正与 loop-aware 策略，显著降低位姿漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT 等前馈视觉几何 Transformer 注意力复杂度为二次，难以扩展到数千帧长序列；现有 chunk-and-align 方法只用顺序相邻块连边，逐帧小误差沿链累积成大尺度漂移。

Method: 提出 VGGT-Bridge：在重叠分块基础上加入长程 skip edges，直接约束非相邻块，无需重训练。对稀疏采样的 coarse chunks 运行 VGGT，使单个 coarse chunk 将远处 fine chunks 桥接为一条直接约束；将 VGGT 首帧尺度偏置转化为漂移修正（对选定 coarse chunk 逆序输入），并用 loop-aware 策略保证该逆序与已有回环闭合兼容。

Result: 相比 SwiftVGGT baseline，ATE 在 KITTI Odometry 降低 28.3%，Virtual KITTI 降低 18.8%，Waymo Open 降低 10.0%，在所有 chunk-and-align 方法中性能最佳。

Conclusion: 通过打破纯顺序连边拓扑并复用模型自身的尺度偏置，可在不重训练、不改变基座模型的条件下有效抑制长序列位姿漂移，为前馈 3D 重建的可扩展性提供新思路。

Abstract: Feed-forward visual geometry transformers such as VGGT reconstruct dense 3D structure from images in a single forward pass, simplifying multi-view 3D reconstruction. However, their quadratic attention complexity makes them difficult to scale to long sequences with thousands of frames. Chunk-and-align frameworks address this by splitting a long sequence into overlapping chunks and stitching their local reconstructions into a pose graph. Yet existing methods connect only sequentially adjacent chunks, so small per-frame errors accumulate along the chain into large-scale drift. To move beyond sequential edges, we propose VGGT-Bridge, which adds long-range skip edges that directly constrain non-adjacent chunks without retraining. By running VGGT on sparsely sampled coarse chunks, each coarse chunk bridges distant fine chunks into a single direct constraint. We further turn VGGT's first-frame scale bias into a drift correction by feeding selected coarse chunks in reverse, and a loop-aware policy keeps this reversal compatible with existing loop closures. VGGT-Bridge reduces ATE by 28.3% on KITTI Odometry, 18.8% on Virtual KITTI, and 10.0% on Waymo Open over the SwiftVGGT baseline, achieving the best performance among all chunk-and-align methods.

</details>


### [213] [Analysis of SWIR Imaging Detection Performance Under Adverse Environmental Conditions for Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06596)
*Rohan Mehra,Alexandre Riffard,Yannis Loumouamou,Mathieu Labussière*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在RASMD数据集上系统对比RGB与短波红外(SWIR)目标检测，发现RGB多数场景更优或相当，并提出"传感器优势挖掘"框架定位SWIR在四种安全关键场景中的优势，将SWIR定位为互补模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWIR成像被视为自动驾驶的潜力模态，但其相对RGB的实际收益在多样条件下缺乏系统性刻画。作者希望明确回答：SWIR在何时、何种条件下真正优于RGB。

Method: 基于RASMD配对RGB-SWIR数据集，覆盖四种天气、两种实时检测架构(RF-DETR等)并做多种微调，统一真值下对比评估；进一步提出"传感器优势挖掘"框架，结合多模型一致性与针对性人工检查，在基本无标注的配对数据上识别某一模态检测更可靠的场景。

Result: 整体上RGB在多数场景表现相当或更优，RF-DETR跨条件鲁棒性更强；SWIR在四类安全关键场景具明确优势：挡风玻璃眩光、玻璃水滴、低对比度目标可见性、远距离车辆检测。

Conclusion: SWIR应作为互补模态，用于增强罕见但困难条件下的感知能力；数据集可申请获取，代码与训练权重公开。

Abstract: Short-wave infrared (SWIR) imaging has emerged as a promising modality for autonomous driving, yet its practical benefits over RGB remain poorly characterized across diverse conditions. This paper presents a systematic comparative study of paired RGB and SWIR object detection on the RASMD dataset, covering four weather conditions and two real-time detection architectures, with various fine-tunings evaluated against a unified ground truth. Overall, RGB demonstrates comparable or superior performance in most scenarios, while RF-DETR exhibits greater robustness across varying conditions. Beyond aggregate metrics, we propose a sensor-dominance mining framework that combines multi-model agreement with targeted manual inspection to identify scenarios where one sensing modality provides more reliable detections using largely unannotated paired data. This analysis reveals that SWIR offers clear advantages in four safety-critical situations, including windshield glare, water droplets on the windshield, low-contrast object visibility, and long-range vehicle detection. The findings suggest that SWIR should be viewed as a complementary modality that enhances perception in rare but challenging conditions. The datasets will be available upon request, and all code and trained model weights are publicly released at this https URL .

</details>


### [214] [Multitask Conditional Generative Adversarial Network Enables Automatic Whole Knee Cartilage and Menisci Segmentation and Reliable T1\r{ho} and T2 Quantification Without High-Resolution Morphological Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06602)
*Ahmed Tahseen Minhaz,Richard Lartey,Zhiyuan Zhang,Jeehun Kim,Kunio Nakamura,Mingrui Yang,Jiasen Zhang,Weihong Guo,Naveen Subhas,Carl S. Winalski,Xiaojuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多任务条件生成对抗网络（MT-cGAN），直接从qMRI回波图像合成类DESS图像并分割膝关节软骨与半月板，避免额外3D高分辨率DESS扫描，Dice达0.84且$T_{1\rho}$/$T_2$定量CV低于1.85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期骨关节炎的定量MRI（qMRI）分析依赖准确的软骨和半月板分割，而分割通常需要耗时、昂贵的3D高分辨率DESS扫描；作者希望仅用qMRI回波图像同时完成图像合成与组织分割，从而缩短扫描时间、推动qMRI临床转化。

Method: 回顾性纳入3个队列、361名受试者共508个膝关节MRI体数据。以DESS图像经预训练模型加人工校正生成分割金标准，由MAPSS回波图像计算$T_{1\rho}$和$T_2$图。构建多任务条件GAN（MT-cGAN），从回波图像联合学习合成类DESS图像与分割软骨、半月板。以Dice评价分割、以变异系数（CV）评价$T_{1\rho}$和$T_2$定量一致性，并与带迁移学习的最先进条件GAN对比。

Result: MT-cGAN在所有软骨与半月板分区取得最高分割性能，平均Dice 0.84（0.80–0.86），显著优于迁移学习条件GAN（平均Dice 0.82，$p<0.001$，Wilcoxon符号秩检验）；在relaxometry定量上与参考DESS协议一致性最高，CV最低（$T_{1\rho}$ 1.84%，$T_2$ 1.81%）。

Conclusion: MT-cGAN能直接从回波图像准确分割软骨与半月板并提供可靠的$T_{1\rho}$、$T_2$定量，省去单独的形态学DESS扫描，缩短扫描时间，有利于qMRI的临床转化。

Abstract: Early osteoarthritis detection through quantitative MRI (qMRI) requires accurate cartilage and meniscus segmentation, traditionally necessitating time-consuming, costly 3D high-resolution Double Echo Steady-State (DESS) MRI scans. This study developed a multi-task conditional generative adversarial network (MT-cGAN) to simultaneously synthesize DESS-like images and segment tissues directly from qMRI echo images. This retrospective study evaluated 508 knee MRI volumes from 361 subjects (mean age: $40.4 \pm 12.2$ years; 179 female) across three cohorts. Ground truth segmentation masks were generated from DESS images using a pretrained model with manual correction, and $T_{1\rho}$ and $T_2$ maps were computed from magnetization-prepared angle-modulated partitioned $k$-space spoiled gradient echo snapshots (MAPSS) echo images. MT-cGAN was trained to jointly synthesize DESS-like images and segment cartilage and meniscus directly from echo images. Model performance was evaluated using Dice score for segmentation accuracy and coefficient of variation (CV) for $T_{1\rho}$ and $T_2$ quantification. MT-cGAN achieved the highest segmentation performance, mean Dice score 0.84 (range: 0.80--0.86) across all cartilage and meniscus compartments and significantly outperformed the state-of-the-art conditional GAN model with transfer learning (mean Dice, 0.82; $p < 0.001$, Wilcoxon signed-rank test). For relaxometry quantification, MT-cGAN demonstrated the highest consistency with the reference DESS protocol, yielding the lowest CV ($T_{1\rho}$: 1.84%, $T_2$: 1.81%). The proposed MT-cGAN accurately segmented cartilage and menisci while providing reliable $T_{1\rho}$ and $T_2$ quantification directly from echo images. By eliminating the need for separate morphological DESS scans, this workflow reduces required scan times to facilitate the clinical translation of qMRI.

</details>


### [215] [Lens3D: Target-Conditioned Visual Foveation for Fine-Grained 3D Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06611)
*Junming Huang,Zini Chen,Shuaiying Hou,Chi Wang,Qiang Dai,Weiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lens3D 通过外部 2D VLM 辅助与知识蒸馏，将细粒度物体理解能力迁移到原生 3D LLM，并构建 LensBench 评测集验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 大语言模型即使视频中已含相关证据，仍忽略细粒度属性与视觉显著性较低的小物体及其部件，导致细粒度理解能力不足。

Method: 提出 Lens3D 框架：LensUnd 利用 3D 定位选取信息互补的视角，调用外部 2D 视觉语言模型完成细粒度物体描述、小目标定位与细粒度问答；LensDistill 通过详细描述监督将外部细粒度知识蒸馏进 3D LLM，使其无需外部 VLM 调用即可从原生输入做描述。

Result: 构建 LensBench，含 2,068 个物体、每物体 3 条银标准参考描述；在 Video-3D LLM 与 3DRS 上，LensDistill 显著提升细粒度物体描述，同时保持既有 grounding 与场景级 QA 性能。

Conclusion: 验证了将外部获取的细粒度知识迁移进原生 3D LLM 的可行性。

Abstract: Existing 3D large language models often overlook fine-grained attributes and less visually salient objects and parts, even when relevant evidence is present in scene videos. We introduce Lens3D to improve fine-grained object understanding through external visual assistance and knowledge transfer. Its LensUnd pipeline adopts 3D localization to select informative, complementary views for an external 2D vision-language model, supporting fine-grained object captioning, small-object grounding, and fine-grained object question answering. LensDistill transfers the resulting fine-grained knowledge to 3D LLMs through detailed caption supervision, enabling captioning from native inputs without external VLM calls. We also construct LensBench, a held-out evaluation set of 2,068 objects with three silver-standard reference descriptions per object. Experiments with Video-3D LLM and 3DRS demonstrate that LensDistill substantially improves fine-grained object captioning while preserving existing grounding and scene-level QA performance. These results establish the feasibility of transferring externally acquired fine-grained knowledge into native 3D LLMs.

</details>


### [216] [Video Encoders Built on Image Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06616)
*Jusheng Zhang,Wenhao Wang,Longqi Cai,Liangzhe Yuan,Yuxiao Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将视频编码解耦为逐帧表示、跨帧token分配与时间交互三阶段，在冻结图像编码器之上以约28%-35%的视觉token达到全图像路径的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生视频编码器在视觉编码阶段就耦合相邻帧，而全图像路径虽保持帧独立表示却带来巨大的视觉token开销，因此作者探究能否用紧凑的视频编码器构建在图像表示之上。

Method: 将常被耦合的三种操作分离：冻结图像编码器先产生逐帧候选；问题感知选择器依据相关性、多样性和跨帧对应关系在帧间分配固定token预算；轻量学习式refiner读取邻帧上下文，仅对保留的anchor写残差更新，从而保持源位置且视觉输出维持固定预算。

Result: 在13个基准和3种视觉-语言骨干上，性能匹配全图像聚合路径，仅用约28%-35%视觉token。Qwen3-VL-8B上13基准宏平均62.75（1535 token），对比全Image路径62.58（4424 token）与原生Conv3D 59.49（2212 token）；Qwen3-VL-32B上宏平均66.28（对比Image的66.09），端到端加速2.16倍。

Conclusion: 紧凑视频编码不必然需要早期时间混合：可先保留帧特异证据，再进行联合分配与选择后的时间上下文化。

Abstract: The design of a video encoder determines when frames begin to interact and which frame-specific visual evidence remains accessible to the language model. Native video pathways couple neighboring frames during visual encoding, whereas image pathways preserve independently computed frame representations but incur a much larger visual-token cost when all image tokens are forwarded. We ask a basic question: whether a compact video encoder can instead be built on image representations. To answer this question, we separate three operations that are often coupled: per-frame representation, cross-frame token allocation, and temporal interaction. A frozen image encoder first produces frame-specific candidates. A question-aware selector then allocates a fixed token budget across frames using relevance, diversity, and cross-frame correspondence, after which a lightweight learned refiner reads neighboring-frame context and writes residual updates only to the retained anchors. This preserves source positions and keeps the visual output at the fixed budget. Across 13 benchmarks and three vision-language backbones, the resulting pathway matches full-image aggregate performance while using only about 28%-35% of its visual tokens. Specifically, on Qwen3-VL-8B, it achieves a 13-benchmark macro-average of 62.75 with 1,535 visual tokens, compared with 62.58 for the full Image pathway at 4,424 tokens and 59.49 for native Conv3D at 2,212 tokens. On Qwen3-VL-32B, it reaches a 13-benchmark macro-average of 66.28, compared with 66.09 for Image, while providing a 2.16x end-to-end speedup. These results show that compact video encoding does not require early temporal mixing: frame-specific evidence can be preserved first, allocated jointly, and temporally contextualized after selection.

</details>


### [217] [RealtimeWAM: One-Step Asynchronous World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06617)
*Chengtao Lv,Jinyang Du,Shuyi Feng,Yang Yong,Shiqiao Gu,Shunzi Yang,Ruihao Gong,Shen Ren,Tianwei Zhang,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RealtimeWAM通过一步动作生成与异步推理显著加速世界动作模型：提出教师锚定一致性蒸馏（TACD）缓解局部-全局误差差距，以及跨专家波前流水线（CEWP）消除专家级等待，在LIBERO等基准上近乎无损（<1%下降）实现约25倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效世界动作模型（WAM）采用Mixture-of-Transformers架构，视频表示只计算一次供动作专家复用，但仍存在两个效率瓶颈：专家内迭代（多步动作去噪）与专家间等待（视频与动作专家串行执行），限制了推理效率。

Method: 1) TACD：针对"低局部一致性误差不保证最终动作准确"的局部-全局误差差距，在局部一致性监督之外引入冻结教师多步展开终点的显式监督，实现准确的一步动作生成；2) CEWP：通过块级共享视频KV缓存，使两专家重叠执行，仅在对应动作注意力消费前同步，消除不必要的专家级等待。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin等多个基准及Fast-WAM、Faster-WAM等模型变体上验证优越性；保持近乎无损性能（<1%下降），同时实现显著端到端加速（H100上约25倍）。

Conclusion: RealtimeWAM以一步动作生成与跨专家异步流水线同时解决专家内迭代与专家间等待瓶颈，兼顾精度与效率，为高效世界动作模型提供了可行方案，代码与权重已开源。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) incorporate visual representations from video generation backbones to guide action prediction. Recent efficient WAMs adopt Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architectures and compute video representations once for reuse by the action expert. However, intra-expert iteration (\ie, multi-step action denoising) and inter-expert waiting (\ie, sequential execution of the video and action experts) still limit inference efficiency. To this end, we present RealtimeWAM, an extremely efficient WAM variant with one-step action generation and asynchronous inference, addressing these two bottlenecks. To reduce intra-expert iteration, we propose Teacher-Anchored Consistency Distillation (TACD) to address a local-global error gap: low local consistency error alone does not guarantee accurate final actions. TACD supplements local consistency with explicit supervision from the frozen teacher's multi-step rollout endpoint, enabling accurate one-step action generation. Additionally, we propose Cross-Expert Wavefront Pipelining (CEWP) to eliminate unnecessary expert-level waiting. It overlaps the two experts through block-wise sharing of the video KV cache, synchronizing only immediately before the corresponding action attention consumes it. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks (\eg, LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin) and model variants (\eg, Fast-WAM and Faster-WAM) demonstrate the superiority of RealtimeWAM. Notably, RealtimeWAM maintains near-lossless performance (\ie, $<1\%$ drop) across these benchmarks while delivering significant end-to-end speedup (\eg, $\sim25\times$ on H100). Our code and checkpoints are available via this \href{ this https URL }{link}.

</details>


### [218] [Talk Like You: Imitating How You Speak in Real-Time Talking Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06658)
*Baiqin Wang,Zhixing Ding,Jijie Li,Jiankuo Zhao,Zhen Lei,Xiangyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TalkLikeYou：基于运动空间建模与Flow Matching单步采样的说话人头生成框架，通过两阶段模仿学习捕捉个体化唇部说话习惯，并提出PLAD指标评估模仿精度与多样性，实现实时生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有说话人头生成方法虽在视觉保真度和唇形同步上表现优异，但普遍忽略用户个性化定制，尤其是体现个人说话习惯的细粒度运动模式；这些习惯差异极细且个体间高度相似，难以建模与捕获，导致生成的面部运动过于统一，缺乏多样化的人物特定发音模式。

Method: 提出TalkLikeYou框架：在运动空间（motion-space）中对说话习惯建模；采用Flow Matching并仅用一次采样步实现实时推理；设计两阶段模仿学习策略以捕捉习惯间的细微差异；支持用户通过数据集预设风格或参考视频指定目标习惯；提出PLAD指标，将口部运动投影到代表性发音轴上以评估模仿准确性与生成多样性。

Result: 大量实验表明，TalkLikeYou能实时生成高质量说话人头，并在说话习惯模仿方面显著优于先前方法；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 个体化说话习惯可作为可学习、可迁移的运动模式被有效建模与模仿；结合高效生成范式（Flow Matching单步采样）与两阶段模仿学习，能够在保证实时性与生成质量的同时实现人物特定的口型习惯定制，为个性化人脸动画生成提供了新思路与评估工具（PLAD）。

Abstract: In daily life, each person exhibits unique speaking habits, leading to subtle yet consistent lip-shape variations even when pronouncing the same word. Although recent talking head generation methods have achieved impressive visual fidelity and lip synchronization, they largely overlook user-specific customization, especially the motion patterns that characterize individual speaking habits. These habits are difficult to model and capture, as their motion patterns are highly fine-grained and often similar across individuals. As a result, many approaches produce overly uniform facial motions and fail to capture diverse, person-specific articulation patterns. To address this, we propose TalkLikeYou, an efficient framework that imitates how a target person speaks in talking head generation. Our method models habit in motion-space and achieves real-time performance through Flow Matching with only one sampling step during inference. We further adopt a two-stage imitation learning strategy to capture subtle distinctions between habits, allowing users to specify a target habit through either a preset style from the dataset or a reference video. In addition, we introduce a new metric PLAD that projects mouth motions onto representative articulation axes to evaluate imitation accuracy and generation diversity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TalkLikeYou generates high-quality talking heads in real-time and significantly improves speaking habit imitation compared with prior methods. The code is available at: this https URL

</details>


### [219] [Cross-dataset harmonization for robust endoscopic image analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06663)
*Romil Imtiaz,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于参考图像的颜色直方图统计映射方法，在CIE-Lab空间做通道级统计变换，将不同内窥镜数据集的外观对齐，从而缩小域偏移，提升跨数据集息肉检测性能最高30.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜机器学习模型在来自不同内窥镜的图像上性能显著下降，主因是传感器与光源导致的颜色分布差异；该跨设备域偏移此前虽被指出，但未被专门解决。

Method: 参考式图像协调：从选定参考数据集在CIE-Lab色彩空间提取全局颜色统计（均值/方差等），对每张目标图像施加通道级统计变换，将其颜色分布映射到参考域，无需重训练即可作为预处理使用。

Result: 在柔性结肠镜与胶囊内镜息肉检测数据集上采用数据集级交叉验证：跨数据集性能一致提升，最高达30.7%，优于相关基线与当前最优方法。

Conclusion: 跨数据集泛化差距的相当一部分由低层外观差异驱动，可通过简单、无需重训练的统计颜色协调有效缓解，说明域偏移的可控性。

Abstract: A significant problem in endoscopic image analysis is that the machine learning (ML) models used for this purpose usually underperform when applied on images acquired from endoscopes that are different from those used to acquire the images of their training set. The main difference of the images originating from different endoscopes is their color distributions, which depend both on the image sensors and the light sources used. Although previous studies have highlighted this challenge, to the best of our knowledge it has not been previously explicitly tackled. This study focuses on this problem and proposes very simple but impactful method. It implements a reference-based image harmonization that reduces global appearance differences between endoscopic datasets. Specifically, it extracts global color statistics from a chosen reference dataset in the CIE-Lab color space and applies a statistical channel-wise transformation to map each target image toward the appearance of the images of the reference dataset. The method is evaluated in the context of polyp detection in both flexible colonoscopy and capsule endoscopy datasets using a dataset-level cross validation protocol. The results indicate that the proposed harmonization consistently improves cross-dataset performance up to 30.7%, outperforming relevant baseline and state-of-the-art methods. The results indicate that a substantial part of the generalization gap is driven by low-level appearance variation that can be mitigated without retraining.

</details>


### [220] [VideoTapestry: Query-Adaptive Memory Refinement for Multi-Agent Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06672)
*Yucheng Liu,Yufei Yin,Mingxiao Feng,Jiajun Deng,Wengang Zhou,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoTapestry：免训练多智能体框架，通过由粗到细的查询驱动精化，自适应重构预构建的三层层次化视频记忆，用于长视频问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解对记忆需求高，答案常分散在长时间跨度中。现有两类范式各有缺陷：查询驱动探索对定位误差敏感；查询无关的记忆构建则可能遗漏问题特定细节。因此需要兼顾全局上下文与问题相关细粒度证据的机制。

Method: 提出VideoTapestry，一种免训练（training-free）多智能体框架。预构建记忆分三层：全局叙事上下文、事件级时间结构、细粒度关系证据。每层配置专门智能体，将检索与精化限制在特定尺度的上下文中；在查询引导下重新访问相关视频区域，用针对性多模态观测逐层丰富记忆；随后按原有层次结构组装成查询自适应复合记忆，以紧凑形式保留全局上下文，并在与查询相关的分支上保留细粒度证据供最终推理。

Result: 相比直接使用GPT-5.5推理，在LVBench、LongVideoBench (Long)、Video-MME (Long)、EgoSchema上分别取得17.2%、14.9%、9.8%、7.0%的绝对准确率提升，并在所有对比方法中达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 层次化、多尺度、查询驱动的记忆精化可在无需训练的情况下显著提升长视频理解性能，为长时序记忆组织提供了有效范式，但其评测局限于视频问答基准，未涉及具身或机器人场景。

Abstract: Long-video understanding places substantial demands on memory, as answering questions often requires retrieving information distributed across extended temporal spans. Existing approaches broadly follow two paradigms: query-driven exploration, which is sensitive to localization errors, and query-independent memory construction, which may omit question-specific details. We introduce VideoTapestry, a training-free multi-agent framework that adapts a preconstructed hierarchical video memory through coarse-to-fine, query-driven refinement. The preconstructed memory organizes video content into three levels, capturing global narrative context, event-level temporal structure, and fine-grained relational evidence, respectively. To support coarse-to-fine localization and observation, we assign a specialized agent to each level, keeping retrieval and refinement within a scale-specific context. Guided by the query, these agents revisit relevant video regions and enrich layer-wise memories with targeted multimodal observations. Their refinements are assembled according to the original hierarchy into a composite query-adaptive memory, preserving global context in a compact form while retaining fine-grained evidence along query-relevant branches for final reasoning. Compared with direct GPT-5.5 inference, VideoTapestry achieves absolute accuracy gains of 17.2%, 14.9%, 9.8%, and 7.0% on LVBench, LongVideoBench (Long), Video-MME (Long), and EgoSchema, respectively, achieving the state-of-the-art results among all competitors.

</details>


### [221] [ChronoWorld: Camera-Controlled Consistent 4D World Generation via Spatiotemporal Cues and Geometric Reflections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06687)
*Xiaoyu Zhou,Dingwei Xian,Zhenyu Wang,Yajiao Xiong,Yongtao Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ChronoWorld 提出「Observation–State–Reflection」框架用于相机可控的自由视角 4D 场景生成,通过时空对极因果注意力与重建驱动的几何反射闭环提升时空一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机可控视频生成模型虽视觉效果好,但难以保持内在的 4D 时空一致性(多视角几何与时间因果关系不一致),导致自由视角下出现漂移与几何崩坏。

Method: 提出 ChronoWorld 三阶段框架:1) Observation:给定上下文视频生成自由视角 4D 场景;2) State/Spatiotemporal Epipolar Causal Attention:在整个生成过程中施加多视角对极约束与时间因果性;3) Reflection:重建驱动的几何反射管线,配合 4D 检索策略对生成结果进行动态自评估与自校正。

Result: 大量实验表明在时空一致的电影级 4D 场景生成上达到 SOTA,跨多样场景具备强泛化性与高保真几何。

Conclusion: 将生成式视频建模与几何重建先验、自反思校正闭环结合,是提升自由视角动态场景时空一致性的有效路径。

Abstract: While existing camera-controllable video generation models can produce visually compelling sequences, preserving intrinsic 4D spatiotemporal coherence remains challenging. To address this limitation, we propose ChronoWorld, an "Observation--State--Reflection" framework that leverages spatiotemporal causal cues and reconstruction priors to generate globally consistent, free-view 4D scenes. Given a context video, we introduce a Spatiotemporal Epipolar Causal Attention mechanism that enforces multi-view epipolar constraints and temporal causality throughout the generation process. In addition, we develop a reconstruction-driven geometric reflection pipeline with a 4D retrieval strategy to enable dynamic self-assessment and correction of generated outputs, improving consistency and accuracy. Extensive experiments show that ChronoWorld achieves state-of-the-art performance in spatiotemporally consistent, cinematic-quality 4D scene generation, with strong generalization and high-fidelity geometry across diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [222] [GS-Pool: Object-Level Change Detection in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06688)
*Boaz Keren-Gil,James Gain,Patrick Marais*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GS-Pool将同一场景两次独立3DGS重建转化为"物体池"上的三维变化检测，用SAM2掩码提升+跨访摄影载体损失等证据判定变化物体并输出掩码与高斯子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工厂、博物馆、测绘场景中需对间隔数月两次扫描判断哪些物体发生变化。直接比较两次3DGS重建不可行：训练随机性使未变场景的重建也不重合，且二次访查照片稀少、质量低。因此需要一个基于物体级、跨重建可比的变化检测方法。

Method: GS-Pool输入同一空间两次独立重建的高斯场。将每次访查照片的SAM2掩码提升到渲染它们的高斯上并合并成物体池，使决策在3D中按物体进行一次。引入"摄影载体"：用一次重建的3DGS训练损失作为证据，即用另一访查照片对每个像素反向传播到渲染该像素的高斯，从而刻画该物体在新视角下的外观一致性。该载体与GS-Diff的几何、颜色项及蒸馏的DINOv3特征结合。把变化证据与两次访查共有物体的证据比较，为每个场景设定自适应变化阈值。输出为每个变化物体的高斯集合及其决策证据，便于三维中人工复核。

Result: 在PASLCD上，mIoU/F1达0.751/0.846，优于最强前作GS-Diff的0.644/0.758，提升17%/12%；mIoU分别高于O-SCD、PlenoCI、MV-3DCD达36%、40%、57%。在CL-Splats上达0.855 mIoU，高于MV-3DCD 33%。

Conclusion: GS-Pool以物体为中心、跨独立3DGS重建，用掩码提升、跨访训练损失载体与语义特征实现自适应阈值的三维变化检测，在多个基准上显著超越既有方法，并提供可解释、可三维复核的物体级结果。属于三维重建下游的增量式变化检测工作，偏域内映射类增量任务，非泛化性重建核心。

Abstract: Factories, museums and surveyors photograph the same space months apart and need to know which objects changed. When each visit is reconstructed with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), a direct comparison of the two reconstructions does not answer this. Training is stochastic, so two reconstructions of an unchanged space never coincide, and the second visit is often a quick re-scan with far fewer photographs. We propose GS-Pool, which takes two independently reconstructed Gaussian fields of the same space and returns the changed objects in each, together with their masks. SAM2 masks of each visit's photographs are lifted onto the Gaussians that render them and merged into an object pool, so every decision is taken once per object in 3D. We introduce a photographic carrier, the 3DGS training loss of each input reconstruction against the other visit's photographs, backpropagated to the Gaussians that rendered each pixel. We combine it with GS-Diff's geometry and colour terms and our distilled DINOv3 features. This evidence is compared with that of the objects present in both visits, which sets a change threshold for each scene. On PASLCD, GS-Pool reaches mIoU/F1 scores of 0.751/0.846 against 0.644/0.758 for GS-Diff, the strongest prior method, a gain of 17%/12%. Its mIoU is also 36%, 40% and 57% above that of O-SCD, PlenoCI and MV-3DCD, and it reaches 0.855 mIoU on CL-Splats, 33% above MV-3DCD. Each changed object is returned as a set of Gaussians with the evidence behind its decision, which an inspector can review in 3D.

</details>


### [223] [Extending Dynamic World Surface Water Mapping to Sentinel-1 with AlphaEarth Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06704)
*Rohit Mukherjee,Frederick Policelli,Beth Tellman,TC Chakraborty,Jonathan Giezendanner,Jonathan A. Sullivan,Ning Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用Dynamic World光学水体类别作为弱监督，结合Sentinel-1 SAR后向散射与AlphaEarth Foundations年度嵌入，实现全天候全球10m级地表水制图，在53个独立场景上IoU达0.85，超过OPERA产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dynamic World虽能全球提供10m土地覆盖/水体产品，但依赖Sentinel-2光学影像，仅限无云观测，导致地表水制图在时空覆盖上受限。作者希望借助SAR的全天候成像能力突破该瓶颈，并探索以现有光学产品作为弱监督标签的可扩展训练范式。

Method: 将DW水体类作为弱监督标签训练Sentinel-1 SAR水体分割模型；以Google AlphaEarth Foundations年度嵌入提供空间上下文，S1后向散射提供成像时刻观测；构建S1-only模型与融合模型（S1+AEF）。在53个全球分布场景上以S1过境48小时内3m PlanetScope独立标注评估，并额外在S1S2-Water基准上测试。

Result: S1-only模型池化水体制图IoU为0.77，与业务化OPERA DSWx-S1的0.75相当；加入AEF后提升至0.85。融合模型在53个场景中44个优于S1-only，48个超过OPERA；在S1S2-Water基准上达0.94，对比OPERA的0.87。

Conclusion: 光学土地覆盖产品可为SAR地表水制图提供可扩展的训练标签，弱监督+时空上下文融合显著提升全天候水体提取精度，兼顾覆盖广度与精度。

Abstract: Dynamic World (DW) maps land use and land cover globally at 10 m from Sentinel-2 (S2) imagery, but only for cloud-free observations, which limits where and when surface water can be mapped. We use the DW water class as weak supervision for a Sentinel-1 (S1) synthetic aperture radar (SAR) model so that DW-like water maps can be produced for every S1 acquisition. Google's AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF) annual embedding supplies spatial context, while S1 backscatter supplies the acquisition-time observation. On 53 globally distributed scenes with independent annotations of 3 m PlanetScope imagery acquired within 48 h of the S1 overpass, the S1-only model already reaches a pooled water intersection over union (IoU) of 0.77, comparable to 0.75 for the operational OPERA DSWx-S1 product, and adding AEF raises it to 0.85. The fused model improves on the S1-only model on 44 of 53 scenes and exceeds OPERA on 48, and on the independent S1S2-Water benchmark it reaches 0.94, compared with 0.87 for OPERA. Optical land-cover products can thus provide scalable training labels for SAR surface water mapping.

</details>


### [224] [MC-Sparse: Deconstructing and Closing the Dense-Sparse Attention Gap in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06801)
*Jiarui Chen,Zeqiang Lai,Jiangshan Wang,Ziheng Ouyang,Ye Huang,Xiangyu Yue,Cewu Lu,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MC-Sparse：免训练稀疏注意力框架，缓存query分组、精确注意力概率选出的KV索引及稠密-稀疏残差，在去噪步间复用，实现视频与3D生成的高保真加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在长序列生成（视频、高分辨率3D资产）中注意力开销大，现有稀疏注意力在高稀疏度下会损害生成质量与保真度。作者通过oracle对比实验定位退化来源：token分组带来的约束、交互选择不准确、以及被丢弃token的注意力贡献损失。

Method: 提出免训练的MC-Sparse：按tile对齐分组相似query，同时以单个KV token为粒度进行选择；利用精确注意力概率挑选KV索引，并缓存query组、KV索引以及稠密与稀疏注意力输出之间的残差，在后续去噪步中复用这些元数据。

Result: 在视频与3D生成模型上，相比现有稀疏注意力基线，更接近稠密注意力输出且去噪加速更大，无明显质量下降。相对稠密注意力，Minimax-H3-Base上取得1.80倍去噪加速，3D资产生成上取得2.32倍加速，质量损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 通过系统性的oracle分析指导设计，MC-Sparse在免训练前提下兼顾长序列扩散生成的质量与效率，为视频/3D生成的稀疏注意力提供有效方案。

Abstract: Sparse attention is a primary approach to reducing the latency of diffusion transformers in long-sequence generation tasks, such as video and high-resolution 3D asset generation. However, existing methods can degrade generation quality and fidelity at high sparsity levels. Through controlled oracle comparisons, we trace this degradation to three sources: constraints imposed by token grouping, inaccurate interaction selection, and the attention contributions lost when tokens are discarded. Guided by this analysis, we propose Meta-Cached Sparse Attention (MC-Sparse), a training-free framework that selects individual key-value (KV) tokens while organizing similar queries into tile-aligned groups for efficient GPU execution. MC-Sparse caches metadata comprising query groups, KV indices selected using exact attention probabilities, and residuals between dense and sparse attention outputs, and reuses them across subsequent denoising steps. Across video and 3D generation models, MC-Sparse achieves higher fidelity to dense-attention outputs and larger denoising speedups than existing sparse-attention baselines, without visible quality degradation. Relative to dense attention, it delivers a $1.80\times$ denoising speedup on Minimax-H3-Base and a $2.32\times$ speedup on 3D asset generation, both with negligible quality loss.

</details>


### [225] [Less Context, Better Geometry: Masked Geometric Encoder for Robust 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06813)
*Zhimin Shao,Xijun Liu,Zhaoliang Zhang,Yutao Tang,Abhay Yadav,Rama Chellappa,Cheng Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Masked Geometric Encoder (MGE)，通过训练时随机丢弃帧token并蒸馏全上下文教师模型，学习鲁棒几何表征，并配合Anchor-Guided Adaptive token merging提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D基础模型采用all-to-all全局注意力，复杂度呈二次方，限制长序列推理；且无约束的跨视角交互会传播来自遮挡或视觉相似但几何距离较远视角的不可靠信息。因此需要在不完整跨视角上下文下学习鲁棒几何表征。

Method: MGE在训练中策略性地从全局注意力中丢弃帧token，并从预训练的全上下文教师模型进行蒸馏，获得更丰富的单帧表征并提供中间监督。推理阶段提出Anchor-Guided Adaptive token merging，保留代表性锚帧并联合合并其余视角的冗余token。

Result: 在遮挡和doppelganger视角下性能显著更强，同时标准基准保持高性能；token reduction更有效，相比其他高效推理方法在有限视角设置下实现推理加速并持续保持更高重建质量。

Conclusion: MGE通过掩码几何编码与蒸馏提升遮挡鲁棒性和单帧表征质量，结合锚引导的自适应token合并，实现高效且高质量的前馈3D重建与相机标定推理。

Abstract: Recent progress in 3D foundation models has enabled rapid 3D reconstruction and camera calibration by leveraging learned 3D priors from vast amount of spatial data. However, the all-to-all global attention design leads to quadratic complexity and limits long-sequence inference; unconstrained cross-view interactions also can propagate unreliable evidence from occluded or visually similar but geometrically distant views. In this paper, We introduce a Masked Geometric Encoder (MGE), which promotes the learning of robust geometric representations under incomplete cross-view context. During training, MGE strategically drops frame tokens from global attention and distills from a pretrained full-context teacher model. This allows the model to learn an intrinsically richer per-frame representation while providing sufficient intermediate supervision to avoid performance degradation. Through extensive experiments, we show that MGE leads to much stronger performance under occlusion and doppelganger views while retaining high performance on standard benchmarks. Such a richer frame representation also leads to more effective token reduction during inference. To this end, we develop a novel Anchor-Guided Adaptive token merging technique that preserves representative anchor frames while jointly merging redundant tokens from the remaining views. Compared to other efficient inference approaches, we can achieve inference speedup while consistently maintaining higher reconstruction quality, particularly in limited-view settings.

</details>


### [226] [TAPDreamer: Transferable Adversarial Patches for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06814)
*Xuanyu Lu,Fengqing Jiang,Kaiyuan Zheng,Yichen Feng,Yaorui Ding,Yuetai Li,Zhen Xiang,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Luyao Niu,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TAPDreamer：仅用公开编码器构造固定局部扰动补丁，无需查询目标策略，即可跨任务、跨动作架构迁移，将世界动作模型（FastWAM、DreamWAM、Motus）成功率近乎清零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型依赖摄像头视觉表征，现有攻击需访问受害模型的动作或预测未来（目标模型输出），实用性与威胁分析受限；作者希望揭示共享视觉编码器这一薄弱环节，说明仅保护下游动作生成不足。

Method: 核心洞察：patch 引起的注意力权重与 value 向量交互会在 patch 覆盖范围之外广播近乎一致的表示偏移，且该偏移在不同任务观测下保持稳定。据此仅用公开编码器，取单一源任务的 6 帧，最大化干净与打补丁编码器表征的全局 L1 距离，得到一个冻结的局部扰动补丁（约覆盖 6.5% 输入），不做任何目标策略查询。

Result: 闭环评测中，每基准一个冻结 patch：FastWAM 在 40 个 LIBERO 任务上成功率 97.7%→0.0%，50 个 RoboTwin 任务 90.8%→0.0%；匹配的随机 patch 仍保持 81.5% 与 79.2%。同一 patch 使两种 DreamWAM 配置降至 2.1% 和 0.8%，Motus 降至 10.0%。

Conclusion: 世界动作模型的防御不能只保护下游动作生成，必须同样保护共享视觉编码器免受持久局部扰动；单一冻结 patch 即可跨任务与架构零查询迁移。

Abstract: World models learn to predict how their environment will evolve, making them an important foundation for general-purpose robotic control. Yet world action models depend on camera inputs whose manipulation can corrupt the visual representations used across tasks and action policies. Existing attacks on these models optimize against the victim's actions or predicted futures and therefore require access to target-model outputs. In this paper, we propose an attack, TAPDreamer, against world action models that instead uses a public encoder alone to construct a fixed local perturbation that transfers across tasks and action architectures. TAPDreamer requires no target-policy queries. Our key insight is that interactions between patch-induced changes in attention weights and value vectors broadcast a nearly identical representation shift far beyond the patch footprint, and this shift remains stable across task observations. Guided by this insight, TAPDreamer uses six frames from one source task to maximize the global L1 distance between clean and patched encoder representations. In closed-loop evaluation, one frozen patch per benchmark, covering about 6.5% of the input, reduces FastWAM's success rate from 97.7% to 0.0% across 40 LIBERO tasks and from 90.8% to 0.0% across 50 RoboTwin tasks; matched random patches retain 81.5% and 79.2% success. The same patches reduce success to 2.1% and 0.8% on two DreamWAM configurations and to 10.0% on Motus. These results show that protecting downstream action generation alone is insufficient: defenses for world action models must also secure shared visual encoders against persistent local perturbations.

</details>


### [227] [UniSlider: Perceptually Uniform Sliders for Continuous Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06831)
*David Serrano-Lozano,Duygu Ceylan,Yannick Hold-Geoffroy,Iliyan Georgiev,Javier Vazquez-Corral,Anna Frühstück*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniSlider 将滑杆值与生成强度解耦，要求感知距离随滑杆线性增长；用 few-step 编辑骨干上训练的轻量 LoRA 逼近该目标，并在推理时通过自适应采样对强度做重映射，在 300 个连续编辑基准上超越现有方法并经用户研究验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式连续图像编辑中，滑杆只是强度参数（adapter 系数、prompt 权重、插值因子）的重新缩放，与实际感知变化不匹配：移动滑杆时图像可能部分回退、长区间无可见变化、短区间又突变。重映射强度可缓解节奏不均，但前提是轨迹单调，而现有方法不保证单调性。

Method: 区分"滑杆值"与"生成强度"，要求感知距离与滑杆值线性增长。提出 UniSlider：在 few-step 编辑骨干上训练轻量 LoRA 使强度逼近理想滑杆；few-step 采样使其可在像素空间直接施加该目标而无需中间真值，且在满强度时保留骨干输出。由于低秩适配器无法使强度完全均匀，滑杆进一步被设计为推理时的强度重映射（自适应采样获得）；训练保证轨迹单调，故该重映射无需额外训练或参数即可补齐剩余差距。

Result: 在包含 300 个连续编辑的新基准上评估均匀性、单调性、编辑保真度与身份保持，UniSlider 优于所有先前方法，并在用户研究中获得偏好。

Conclusion: 将滑杆与强度解耦、以训练+推理重映射两阶段实现单调且感知线性的连续编辑是可行且有效的范式，对连续可控生成编辑具有参考价值。

Abstract: Sliders provide an intuitive interface for continuous image editing. In current generative approaches, however, the slider is simply a rescaling of the method's strength parameter, such as an adapter coefficient, a prompt weight, or an interpolation factor. This strength relates poorly to perceptual change. The image can partially revert as the slider moves, long stretches of the range produce no visible difference, and short intervals transform the image abruptly. Remapping the strength could fix this uneven pace, but only if the trajectory is monotone, which current methods do not enforce. We therefore distinguish the slider from the strength, and require perceptual distance from the input to grow linearly with the slider value. We introduce UniSlider, a lightweight LoRA trained on a few-step editing backbone so that its strength approximates this ideal slider. Few-step sampling lets us impose this objective in pixel space without intermediate ground truth, and the backbone's output is preserved at full strength. However, a low-rank adapter cannot make the strength fully uniform. Our slider is thus an inference-time remapping of the strength, obtained by adaptive sampling. Since training optmizes to make the trajectory monotone, this remapping closes the remaining gap without extra training or parameters. On a new benchmark of 300 continuous edits evaluating uniformity, monotonicity, edit fidelity, and identity preservation, UniSlider outperforms all prior methods and is preferred in a user study.

</details>


### [228] [Anatomy-aware Fine-grained Multimodal Fusion for Laryngopharyngeal Cancer T-Staging Prediction Using CT and Radiology Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06837)
*Xingyue Zhao,Yanzhou Su,Fang Zhang,Zhanghexuan Ji,Yirui Wang,Dazhou Guo,Sibo Ju,Yuehua Cheng,Yuzhen Chen,Ming Feng,Le Lu,Tsung-Ying Ho,Jian Wang,Dakai Jin,Na Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解剖感知多模态框架，通过解剖结构化器官图（AOG）、器官锚定跨模态对齐（OCA）与报告增强图精炼（REG），融合器官级CT上下文与放射报告，实现喉咽癌T分期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喉咽癌T分期需准确判断肿瘤侵犯范围以指导个体化治疗，但临床依赖有创活检，CT分期因侵犯模式复杂而困难。现有计算方法存在两点不足：一是结构关系建模不足，要么对整体CT体积处理缺乏肿瘤相关解剖约束，要么依赖费力的肿瘤分割；二是细粒度跨模态对齐困难，全局特征融合难以将单个解剖结构与报告中对应的器官级描述精确对齐。

Method: 构建解剖结构化器官图（AOG），建模原发灶与周围器官间的侵犯模式；执行器官锚定跨模态对齐（OCA），使每个器官节点从放射报告中聚合文本证据；再通过报告增强图精炼（REG）注入从报告中抽取的器官特异侵犯线索，精炼图表示；最终形成融合空间与文本证据的多模态器官图用于T分期。

Result: 大量实验表明该框架在喉咽癌T分期任务上取得优越性能（摘要未给出具体量化指标与数据集细节）。

Conclusion: 解剖结构先验与细粒度跨模态对齐可有效提升喉咽癌T分期，为无创影像分期提供可行路径；但缺乏具体指标、消融与临床验证细节。

Abstract: Accurate T-staging is crucial for guiding personalized treatment strategies for laryngopharyngeal cancer. However, current clinical practice relies on invasive biopsy procedures, whereas CT-based staging remains challenging due to the complex patterns of tumor invasion. Recent computer-aided approaches face two key challenges: 1) Structural relationship modeling: existing methods underrepresent anatomically structured patterns of tumor invasion, as they either process whole CT volumes without tumor-specific anatomical constraints or rely on labor-intensive tumor segmentation. 2) Fine-grained cross-modal alignment: while radiology reports contain organ-specific invasion details, current methods that apply global feature fusion struggle to accurately align individual anatomical structures with their corresponding textual descriptions. To address these issues, we propose an anatomy-aware multimodal framework that integrates organ-level CT context and radiology reports into a unified representation for laryngopharyngeal T-staging. The framework first constructs an Anatomy-Structured Organ Graph (AOG) that captures invasion patterns between primary sites and surrounding organs, then performs Organ-Anchored Cross-Modal Alignment (OCA) so that each organ node aggregates textual evidence from the radiology report, and finally refines this graph representation by injecting organ-specific invasion cues extracted from the report via Report-Enhanced Graph-Refinement (REG), yielding a multimodal organ graph that combines spatial and textual evidence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves superior performance in T-staging of laryngopharyngeal cancer.

</details>


### [229] [Learning to Read the Contextual Tokens in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06844)
*Omer Dahary,Etai Sella,Hadar Averbuch-Elor,Daniel Cohen-Or,Or Patashnik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个"阅读"MM-DiT语境token的框架：训练轻量瓶颈网络将中间语境token映射到冻结LLM的输入空间，用自然语言提问解码生成中图像的信息，发现语境token早期即编码全局语义并随去噪逐步细化，据此提出Contextual Alignment训练技术提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MM-DiT在生成过程中通过多模态注意力反复更新文本token，形成动态的"语境token"，但其功能与内含信息尚不清楚，缺乏可解释的解读手段，也未被用作改进生成的抓手。

Method: 训练一个轻量瓶颈网络，把MM-DiT中间层的语境token映射到冻结LLM的输入空间，使LLM仅凭这些隐藏表示即可回答关于正在生成图像的问答；基于可读性分析提出Contextual Alignment训练技术，显式强化语境token中的视觉-语义信息。

Result: 语境token携带丰富的全局场景表示：生成特定语义（含提示词未指定的属性）在去噪早期即可读取，细节随时间逐渐显现；即便输入空提示词仍可解码出图像特定信息，说明信息来自演化中的视觉表示本身；可读性更高的生成更易获得高人类偏好分数；Contextual Alignment提升了生成质量与分布覆盖度。

Conclusion: 语境token既是理解MM-DiT内部动力学的可解释窗口，也是改进生成模型的有效优化目标。

Abstract: Multimodal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiTs) jointly process visual and textual representations throughout generation. These models repeatedly update the text tokens through multimodal attention, forming dynamic contextual tokens whose function is not well understood. In this work, we introduce a framework for reading this contextual space through natural-language interrogation. We train a lightweight bottleneck network that maps intermediate contextual tokens into the input space of a frozen Large Language Model (LLM), allowing the LLM to answer questions about the emerging image directly from these hidden representations. Our reader reveals that contextual tokens encode a rich, global representation of the emerging scene: generation-specific semantics, including attributes left underspecified by the prompt, are accessible surprisingly early in denoising, while increasingly fine-grained details become readable over time. Remarkably, this information remains decodable even when the MM-DiT receives an empty prompt, showing that contextual tokens accumulate substantial image-specific information from the evolving visual representation itself. We further find that generations with more readable contextual representations tend to receive higher human-preference scores. Building on these observations, we introduce Contextual Alignment, a training technique that explicitly reinforces the visual-semantic information encoded in the contextual tokens, improving generation quality and distributional coverage. Together, our results establish contextual tokens as both an interpretable view into the internal dynamics of MM-DiTs and an effective target for improving generative models.

</details>


### [230] [S2PD: Serial-to-Parallel Diffusion for Physically and Logically Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06847)
*Jeffrey Hu,Daniel Olmeda Reino,Ayush Tewari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S2PD将视频扩散的采样过程分为两阶段：高噪声时自回归去噪以协调事件因果依赖，低噪声时切换为并行扩散联合细化整段视频，从而在遵守物理/符号规则的同时提升采样效率，兼容像素空间DiT与LoRA微调的预训练视频模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双向视频扩散模型虽可并行去噪整段视频，但即使在程序化生成器提供的近乎无限同分布数据上训练，仍违反物理规律与简单符号规则。作者认为根因是并行去噪缺少对相互依赖事件进行序列化协调的计算能力，因此需要在高噪声阶段引入自回归式的串行计算，同时保留并行阶段的高效率。

Method: 提出Serial-to-Parallel Diffusion（S2PD）：在高噪声区间执行自回归扩散，提供协调事件与保证有效状态转移所需的串行计算；在低噪声区间切换为并行扩散，对整段视频联合细化并缩短采样时间。两种实现：从零训练的像素空间diffusion transformer，以及对预训练视频模型用LoRA微调并加入causal attention的适配版本。在游戏、物理仿真与真实视频上，与匹配的双向基线及其他串行方法比较规则遵循度、时间稳定性与采样效率。

Result: 在游戏、物理仿真和真实视频域中，S2PD比同等条件的双向基线更可靠地遵循规则（物理规律与符号规则），比纯串行方法具有更好的时间稳定性，且采样效率更高。两种架构（像素空间DiT从零训练、LoRA适配预训练视频模型）均验证了该串行到并行调度策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 将扩散采样调度按噪声水平分解为串行（高噪声）与并行（低噪声）两阶段，可在不牺牲太多效率的前提下显著提升生成视频的物理与符号规则一致性，为视频世界模型与物理仿真类生成提供通用策略。

Abstract: Bidirectional video diffusion models denoise entire videos in parallel, yet when trained on effectively unlimited in-distribution data from procedural generators, continue to violate physical laws and simple symbolic rules. We introduce Serial-to-Parallel Diffusion (S2PD), which performs autoregressive diffusion at high noise before switching to parallel diffusion at low noise. The autoregressive phase provides the serial computation needed to coordinate interdependent events and produce valid state transitions while the parallel phase jointly refines the entire video and reduces sampling time relative to fully serial generation. We implement S2PD with two architectures: a pixel-space diffusion transformer trained from scratch and a pretrained video model adapted through LoRA fine-tuning with causal attention. Across games, physical simulations, and real video, S2PD follows rules more reliably than matched bidirectional baselines and generates videos with greater temporal stability and sampling efficiency than other serial methods.

</details>


### [231] [One Figure, Every Canvas: Editable Flowchart Relayout via Agentic Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06852)
*Shih-Chen Tseng,Chih-Hsuan Chen,Ryan Yang,Hsi-An Chen,Chun-Wei Tuan Mu,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"宽高比自适应流程图重排"任务：给定栅格流程图与目标比例，生成结构忠实、无幻觉、可编辑的布局；用Parse-Style-Layout三阶段智能体+批评者（确定性约束检查+VLM视觉反馈）输出可编辑mxGraph XML，在100张流程图×5种比例基准上内容保真度达68.6%（对比先前工作11.2-41.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML论文的流水线图需在论文栏、16:9幻灯片、竖版海报、1:1社交图、9:16手机预览等多种画布间复用，同一计算图在不同宽高比下若连接被静默破坏就会误述方法。现有方案各有典型失败：图到图模型拉伸方块且无法支持极端比例，文生图智能体产生幻觉内容，先解析后渲染的流程错接边。因此需要把该问题形式化为独立任务。

Method: 将任务形式化为宽高比自适应流程图重排，并提出智能体流水线，分解为Parse、Style、Layout三个阶段；每个阶段由一个主智能体搭配一个批评者组成，批评者结合确定性约束检查与VLM视觉反馈，从而显式检查连通性、避免连接被静默破坏。输出为可编辑的mxGraph XML。

Result: 在自建的100张流程图×5种宽高比基准上，由Gemini 3.1 Pro评估并经人工判断验证，方法达到68.6%的Content Fidelity，而先前工作仅为11.2-41.4%。

Conclusion: 该工作把流程图跨宽高比重排确立为独立任务，验证了"解析-风格-布局"分工加约束/VLM双重批评者的智能体方案在结构忠实与可编辑性上显著优于端到端图像生成与解析-渲染基线，但绝对保真度仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Pipeline figures in ML papers must be repurposed across many canvases, including paper columns, 16:9 slides, portrait posters, 1:1 social teasers, 9:16 phone previews. Each format imposes a different aspect ratio on the same computational graph, where any silently broken connection misrepresents the method. We formulate aspect-ratio-adaptive flowchart relayout as a distinct task: given a raster flowchart and a target ratio, produce a structurally faithful, hallucination-free, editable layout. Existing methods fail characteristically: image-to-image models stretch blocks and reject extreme ratios, text-to-image agentic systems hallucinate content, and parse-then-render systems mis-route edges. We propose an agentic pipeline factored into Parse, Style, and Layout stages, each pairing a main agent with a critic that combines deterministic constraint checks with VLM visual feedback so connectivity is explicitly checked and prevented from being silently broken. Outputs are this http URL -editable mxGraph XML. On a curated benchmark of 100 flowcharts at five aspect ratios, evaluated by Gemini 3.1 Pro and validated against human judgments, our method reaches 68.6% Content Fidelity versus 11.2-41.4% for prior work. Project page: this https URL

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [TACET: Context-Appropriate Acoustic-Social Navigation for Quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03828)
*Sungsan Park,Young-Sik Shin,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TACET提出一种"声学-社交"导航方法，用机器人自身视角推断社交情境，并通过单一紧凑行为token同时决定"去哪里走"（社交代价地图）与"走多响"（安静步态策略）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人进入医院、养老院与安静办公室等场所时，其足-地冲击主导的运动噪声本身是一种社交变量。既有社交导航只关注人类空间、将机器人视为声学均一；而安静运动方法则把噪声压到操作者指定的、与情境无关的水平，二者均缺乏情境适配性。

Method: 慢速微调的视觉-语言推理器与快速反应式控制器通过一个紧凑行为token <gait, speed, social_cost> 耦合；该token同时条件化社交代价地图（去哪）与安静运动策略（多响）；并采用结构化视野外记忆，在人物离开相机视野后仍保留其上下文。

Result: 在真实四足机器人上，情境条件化运动在同等速度下最多降低9.3 dBA噪声；在所测场景中，完整方法保持100%个人空间合规，声学侵入低（≤2.9 dBA），在空间与声学指标上联合提升。

Conclusion: 将运动噪声显式作为社交变量纳入导航决策，可同时改善空间合规性与声学侵入性，为社交导航提供新的情境化范式。

Abstract: Quadruped robots entering hospitals, care homes, and quiet offices must be context-appropriate not only in where they move but in how loudly they move: a legged robot's locomotion noise, dominated by foot-ground impacts, is itself a social variable. Prior social navigation respects human space but treats the robot as acoustically uniform, while quiet-locomotion methods reduce noise to an operator-specified, context-blind level. We present TACET, a context-appropriate acoustic-social navigation method that infers social context from the robot's egocentric view and decides both where it walks and how loudly, coupling a slow fine-tuned vision-language reasoner to a fast reactive controller through a single compact behavior token, <gait, speed, social_cost>. The same token conditions both a social costmap (where to go) and a quiet locomotion policy (how loudly to move), while a structured out-of-view memory keeps recently seen people in the reasoner's context after they leave the camera view. On a real quadruped, context-conditioned locomotion lowers locomotion noise by up to 9.3 dBA at matched speed, and across our scenarios the full method keeps personal-space compliance at 100% with low acoustic intrusion (<=2.9 dBA), jointly improving spatial and acoustic performance in the evaluated scenarios. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [233] [GOTT: Object-centric Dexterous Manipulation with a Reusable Cross-Embodiment Primitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03861)
*Yulin Liu,Lai Wei,Yen-Jen Wang,Akash Sharma,Pieter Abbeel,Henrik I. Christensen,Haozhi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GOTT 框架，以单一跨具身「接触获取原语」为核心，将物体轨迹与到达规格转化为多指手的稳健接触与运动跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型与大规模人类数据蕴含丰富的操作意图，但难以转化为多指机器人行为；灵巧手缺乏可复用、跨任务跨具身可靠建立接触的低层原语。

Method: GOTT 采用「到达-获取-移动」三段结构：先依机器人无关的物体轨迹与到达规格把手靠近任务相关接触区域；再由共享闭环接触获取原语从近似初始位姿建立稳定接触；最后由位姿条件控制器跟踪期望物体运动。到达规格可来自未来感知规划、外部模型或人类示范，而原语与跟踪后端保持不变。

Result: 仿真与真机实验表明，GOTT 能在多样物体、臂-手平台以及已见与未见手部形态上稳健建立接触，并相较开环抓取执行持续提升端到端任务成功率。

Conclusion: 以单一跨具身接触获取原语解耦高层意图与低层控制，可提升灵巧操作的可复用性与成功率。

Abstract: Foundation models and large-scale human data provide rich sources of manipulation intent, but translating this intent into multi-fingered robot behavior remains difficult. Dexterous hands still lack a reusable low-level primitive that reliably establishes contact across tasks and embodiments. We propose GOTT, a reach-acquire-move framework built around a single cross-embodiment contact-acquisition primitive. Given a robot-agnostic object trajectory and a reach specification, GOTT first brings the hand near a task-relevant contact region. The shared closed-loop primitive then establishes stable contact from this approximate initialization, and a pose-conditioned controller tracks the desired object motion. Reach specifications may come from future-aware planning, external models, or human demonstrations, while the primitive and tracking backend remain unchanged. Simulation and real-world experiments show that GOTT is able to establish robust contact across diverse objects, arm-hand platforms, and seen and unseen hand morphologies. It also consistently improves end-to-end task success over open-loop grasp execution.

</details>


### [234] [MOSAIC-SV: Real-Time Adaptive Identification of Vessel Dynamics for the Control and Deployment of Aquatic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03898)
*Wensen Liu,Jerry Peng,Shravani Vedagiri,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MOSAIC-SV 是一个可部署的实时自适应水动力学辨识与控制系统：以规格书工程先验为起点，无需专门辨识试验，在闭环任务每个控制步上用物理可容许的无迹卡尔曼滤波重估水动力、扰动与执行器参数，并用命令相关的 consider 投影抑制不可归因修正，MPPI 控制器基于当前估计规划。仿真（CyberShip II）与实船（BlueBoat 双推进器双体船、6.3 吨双舷外单体船）验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的水面机器人控制依赖水动力学模型，而该模型辨识成本高且强依赖于船体、载荷与测量工况：换了载荷或工况就失效。作者希望跳过专门的辨识试验，直接从规格书先验出发，在任务执行过程中在线获得"控制足够用"的动力学模型。

Method: 1) 规格书工程先验作为初始模型；2) 物理可容许的无迹卡尔曼滤波（UKF）在闭环每个控制步重估水动力、扰动、执行器参数；3) 命令相关的 consider 投影：当当前指令无法区分执行器效能与外力的贡献时，保留该修正不作更新；4) MPPI（模型预测路径积分）控制器基于当前参数估计在线规划，形成辨识-控制共设计闭环。

Result: 仿真（CyberShip II）：在静态失配与瞬态变化下恢复标定模型的航行性能；当惯性或阻尼先验偏差达一个数量级时，航行时间仍在其未缩放先验自身航行时间的 20% 以内。实船（BlueBoat 双推进器双体船）：相比冻结的工程先验，航行至少快 25%，自运动预测误差至少低 56%，且在未建模载荷下同样成立。同一系统概念在 6.3 吨双舷外单体船上可行部署。

Conclusion: 在线自适应动力学辨识可与 MPC 类控制器在闭环内实时协同，无需专门辨识试验，即可从粗糙工程先验恢复接近标定模型的性能，并具备跨船型（小型双体船到 6.3 吨单体船）与跨载荷条件的可迁移性与可部署性。

Abstract: Model-based control of an aquatic robotic platform depends on a hydrodynamic model that is costly to identify and specific to the hull, payload, and conditions it was measured in. Here, we present MOSAIC-SV, a deployable real-time adaptive dynamics identification and control system that identifies a control-sufficient dynamics model from a spec-sheet engineering prior, without dedicated identification trials, and re-estimates it at every control step of a closed-loop mission while the controller plans on it. A physically admissible unscented Kalman filter re-estimates hydrodynamic, disturbance, and actuator parameters at every control step of the closed-loop mission; while a command-dependent consider projection withholds corrections the current command cannot attribute between actuator effectiveness and external force; and a model predictive path integral controller plans on the current estimate. In simulation on a CyberShip II plant, MOSAIC-SV recovers the transit performance of the calibrated model under static mismatch and transient changes, and stays within 20% of its own transit time at the unscaled prior when its inertia or damping prior is wrong by an order of magnitude. In on-water field trials on the Blue Robotics BlueBoat, a twin-thruster catamaran, MOSAIC-SV transits at least 25% faster and predicts its own motion with at least 56% less error than its frozen engineering prior, including under an unmodeled payload. The same MOSAIC-SV system concept was also feasibly deployed on a 6.3-tonne dual outboard monohull.

</details>


### [235] [Sparse Calibration-Based Personalization of Kernel-Based Gait Phase and Speed Estimation Using Wearable IMUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03931)
*Myeongju Cha,Pilwon Hur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于稀疏标定的个性化核方法，用双侧大腿/小腿IMU轨迹构建28种速度的参考库，通过3个标定速度的个体基线与主成分（PC）偏离模型共估计步态相位与步行速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体平均核在个体差异下精度受限，需以极少量标定数据实现个性化步态相位与速度联合估计，并验证嵌入式的实时可行性。

Method: 20人运动数据集构建28速度参考库；用3个标定速度估计用户基线，叠加基线中心化运动学偏差的主成分模型生成个性化核；离线交叉验证与单被试在线嵌入式实时验证。

Result: 离线个性化核相对群体核降低相位误差；在线单被试试验中相位与速度平均误差数值上更低且实时运行；速度主效应不显著，多重比较校正后成对相位差异不显著。

Conclusion: 证明可穿戴实时实现的可行性，但存在参考库与在线数据分布不匹配问题，需更大规模队列验证。

Abstract: This paper presents sparse calibration-based personalization for kernel-based gait phase and walking speed co-estimation from wearable inertial measurement units (IMUs). A 28-speed reference library was constructed from bilateral thigh and shank trajectories in a 20-subject locomotion dataset. Rather than directly applying population-average kernels, the method combines a user-specific baseline estimated from three calibration speeds with a principal-component (PC) model of baseline-centered kinematic deviations. Offline validation showed that personalized kernels reduced phase error relative to the population kernel. In a single-participant online pilot evaluation, the personalized kernels produced numerically lower mean phase and speed errors and ran in real time on embedded hardware. However, the speed effect was not significant, and pairwise phase differences did not remain significant after multiple-comparison correction. The pilot evaluation establishes wearable implementation feasibility while indicating reference-to-online dataset mismatch and the need for larger-cohort validation.

</details>


### [236] [REDIRECT: A 1% Fix for Bad Robot Habits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03997)
*Yu Zhang,Jiazhuo Li,Yancong Wei,Kangkang Dong,Xiaojun Zhu,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REDIRECT 用仅 1% 的反向传播预算，基于情节级"保留/问题"标签定位混合质量遥操作数据中的局部不良行为分支，并为其指派一致的保留式后续动作，从而修复机器人"坏习惯"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作数据中少数缺陷片段会让机器人学到不良习惯；混合质量数据中正常与问题示范大部分行为共享，仅在局部动作续接处不同。全量重训代价高，仅用干净数据微调在小更新预算下恢复有限。

Method: 仅使用情节级（episode-level）的"保留/问题"标签，REDIRECT 定位行为分支点，将一致的保留式动作续接指派给分支周围的原始观测，并锚定共享行为，无需帧级标注或额外交互。

Result: 在 3 个随机化 ManiSkill 任务、3 个种子上，平均成功率从 68.2% 提升至 90.3%，恢复干净重训差距的 88.8%，而同等算力微调仅 22.1%；在 PiPER 真机上，杯子插入与毛巾折叠任务恢复 86.7–92.9% 的干净数据差距。

Conclusion: 局部机器人不良习惯可通过把更新预算花在行为差异处、而非重学共享行为来修复，且仅需极少算力与弱监督标签。

Abstract: Robots can acquire bad habits from a few defective moments in otherwise useful teleoperation. In mixed-quality robot data, normal and problematic demonstrations share most task behavior and differ only at a local action continuation. Full retraining is costly, while fine-tuning on clean data alone offers limited recovery under a small update budget. We ask whether the local difference can instead be fixed with only 1% of the full-training sample-backward budget. Using only episode-level retained/problematic labels, REDIRECT localizes the branch, assigns a coherent retained continuation to the original observations around it, and anchors shared behavior, without frame-level annotations or additional interaction. Across three randomized ManiSkill tasks and three seeds, REDIRECT raises the mean success rate from 68.2% to 90.3%, recovering 88.8% of the clean-retraining gap versus 22.1% for matched-compute fine-tuning. On a PiPER arm, it recovers 86.7-92.9% of the clean-only gap across cup insertion and towel folding. Local robot habits can therefore be repaired by spending the update budget on the behavioral difference rather than relearning shared behavior.

</details>


### [237] [SUAVE: Unified Video-Action Models via Masked Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04009)
*Rhythm Syed,Jean Mercat,Sedrick Keh,Kushal Arora,Paarth Shah,Aykut Onol,Mengchao Zhang,Tony Dear*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SUAVE 将语言、视频、动作统一为共享序列中的离散 token，用掩码扩散 Transformer 同时生成视频与动作；推理时通过选择掩码位置即可切换为世界模型、机器人策略或视频-动作模型，并在仿真与真机上验证了性能与协同训练收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 只预测动作、缺乏对未来观测的想象；视频扩散世界模型虽有想象能力但把语言当作冻结条件；统一模型要么逐 token 自回归解码，要么保持视频连续并外挂动作头，难以在同一架构内兼顾想象与行动。作者希望用单一离散词表 + 掩码扩散统一三种模态。

Method: 构建 SUAVE（Single vocabulary Unified Action-Video modEl）：语言、视频、动作全部离散化为 token 放入同一序列，由掩码扩散 Transformer 建模条件生成。推理时改变掩码模式即可充当世界模型、策略或联合模型；无动作视频协同训练时，把视频中的动作位置填为 mask token 并排除出损失。
