Since the guidance fits the target embodiment, the policy explores only the actions that the target robot can execute and thus achieves high success rates. PatternDex also requires only simple fine-tuning to train a new robot, since it can reuse the learned interaction pattern. We evaluate PatternDex with bimanual dexterous hands on human demonstrations from the ARCTIC dataset. PatternDex achieves, on average, a 92.8% success rate with Allegro hands, while the state-of-the-art baseline achieves 52.2%. Also, it achieves success rates above 70% with three other robot hands after fine-tuning alone. Furthermore, we verify that the learned policy transfers well to a real-world task of opening a microwave. Videos and additional results are available at this https URL

</details>


### [273] [Flow Policies as Actions of Skill-Level World Models: Learned and Symbolic Abstractions for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04767)
*Andreu Matoses Gimenez,Andrei-Carlo Papuc,Chris Pek,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作用flow-matching策略从演示中构造"技能级动作"(一次执行=一次世界模型转移),提出四种递进行为抽象(压缩噪声种子、两个离散码、符号标签),在最多14步的技能序列积木重排任务中评测。符号标签在≤6技能任务上成功率>90%,无标签的物体中心学得码在单技能上可匹敌、2-5技能保持50%-75%;消融显示标签优势主要来自规划器知道哪些动作可用,而非标签本身;超过6技能时瓶颈是搜索而非世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型让机器人通过预测动作后果来规划,但控制频率下的动作需大量预测步,搜索空间大且误差累积。技能级动作可缩短序列,但符号技能词表依赖领域知识与标注演示,难以自动获得。因此需要一种无需人工符号标注、直接从演示中提取技能级抽象的方法。

Method: 在分段为完整技能的演示上训练flow-matching策略,策略将噪声种子+观测(可选附带code或label)映射为一整套技能执行,使单次执行对应一次世界模型转移。基于此提出四种递增任务知识的动作抽象:(1)压缩的噪声种子;(2)从演示中学得的两个离散码(含物体中心码);(3)符号标签。四者使用统一的世界模型训练流程与规划框架对比。

Result: 在需多达14个连续技能的仿真积木重排任务上:符号标签在≤6技能任务成功率>90%,之后下降;无标签条件下物体中心学得码在单技能任务上与标签持平,在2-5技能任务上保持其成功率的1/2至3/4。消融表明标签的优势很大程度来自规划器知道哪些动作可用(适用性先验),而非标签本身;超过6技能后,限制成功的是搜索而非世界模型。

Conclusion: 技能级动作抽象可用flow-matching策略从演示中无监督/弱监督地构造,从而缩短规划序列并降低误差累积。符号标签带来的增益主要源于动作适用性信息而非语义本身,提示后续可显式建模动作可用性以弥补无标签抽象的差距;长序列瓶颈已从世界模型转移到搜索算法。

Abstract: Latent world models enable robots to plan by predicting the consequences of actions. Planning long tasks with control-rate actions requires many prediction steps, which enlarges the search space and accumulates error. Skill-level actions shorten these sequences, but a symbolic skill vocabulary requires domain knowledge and labeled demonstrations. We construct skill-level actions from the inputs of a flow-matching policy trained on demonstrations segmented into complete skills. The policy maps a noise seed and an observation, optionally with a code or label, to a complete skill execution, so one execution is one world-model transition. On this mechanism we propose four action abstractions with increasing task knowledge: a compressed seed, two discrete codes learned from the demonstrations, and a symbolic label. We evaluate them with a common world-model training procedure and planning framework on simulated block rearrangement tasks that require up to 14 sequential skills. The symbolic label succeeds in over 90% of the tasks that require up to six skills and degrades beyond. Without any label, an object-centric learned code matches it on single-skill tasks and retains half to three quarters of its success on tasks of two to five skills. Ablations attribute much of the label's advantage to its planner knowing which actions are applicable, rather than to the label itself. Beyond six skills the search, not the world model, limits success. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [274] [Learning Task and Motion Plans from Real Demonstrations with Hybrid Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04771)
*Zuleika Redondo Garcia,Andreu Matoses Gimenez,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合流匹配规划器，单网络同时生成符号计划（掩码离散流匹配）与运动轨迹（连续流匹配），仅需少量演示即可在闭环中执行长时程移动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式规划器依赖数千条固定基座机械臂的脚本演示，且只能开环执行，无法适应移动操作的长时程与动态环境，亟需小数据集学习与闭环重规划能力。

Method: 采用混合流匹配：掩码离散流匹配生成符号子任务计划，连续流匹配生成动作轨迹。利用两条数据特性弥补小样本：中间动作续接构成新演示（样本倍增并支持每步重规划）；同类物体可互换（目标置换扩增）。并提出离散计划的训练与生成改进方案。

Result: 基础实现在留出场景上生成68%有效计划，改进训练与生成方案提升至76%；每步重规划使运动学仿真任务完成率从40%升至53%；与仅运动流匹配策略完成率相当且额外提供符号计划，优于先前混合扩散方案；在真实移动机械臂上完成验证。

Conclusion: 混合流匹配可在少量演示下联合产出符号计划与轨迹，并通过数据扩增与闭环重规划显著提升长时程移动操作的有效性与完成率，具备实机可行性。

Abstract: Long-horizon mobile manipulation requires a task plan and the motion that executes it. Generative planners trained on demonstration produce both in one pass, requiring neither a symbolic domain nor search. To date, however, they have relied on thousands of scripted demonstrations of fixed-base arms and executed open loop. This paper presents a hybrid flow matching planner: a single network generates the symbolic plan with masked discrete flow matching and the motion trajectory with continuous flow matching. Unlike prior generative planners, we aim to learn from a much smaller set of demonstrations and to execute the plan in closed loop. Two properties of the data compensate for the small dataset. A demonstration resumed from any of its intermediate actions is itself a demonstration, which multiplies the training samples and enables replanning after every action. Objects of the same kind are interchangeable, which turns demonstrations of one goal into demonstrations of every permuted goal. Our base implementation produces valid plans on 68% of held-out scenes; a training and generation scheme for the discrete plan raises this to 76%, and replanning after every action raises the task completion rate from 40% to 53% in a kinematic simulation. The planner matches the task completion rate of motion-only flow matching policies while additionally providing the symbolic plan, and it outperforms previous hybrid diffusion formulations on both task completion and plan validity. We validate the planner on a real mobile manipulator. Videos and project page: this https URL

</details>


### [275] [Distributed Cascade Force Control of Soft-Tactile-Based Multi-robot System for Object Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04775)
*Duy Anh Nguyen,Nhat Minh Dinh Le,Nhan Huu Nguyen,Pham Duy Hung,Van Anh Ho,Trung Dung Ngo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分布式级联力控制系统（DCFC），多机器人无需机器人间通信即可将刚性物体推向期望的移动目标，内环实现接触力与位置控制，外环协调推动方向，并用Lyapunov理论验证稳定性，仿真与实机实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协同推动刚性物体到移动目标通常依赖机器人间通信，存在通信受限、延迟与可扩展性问题；同时物体质量未知，增加了控制难度。因此需要一种无通信、对未知质量鲁棒的分布式控制方案。

Method: 机器人装备360度视觉软触觉传感器，用于确定接触位置与合力；分析刚性物体在平面上的动力学，设计分布式级联控制：内环融合接触力与定位控制，确保接触并施加期望力；外环在无通信条件下协调机器人朝期望方向推动物体，不依赖物体质量先验；用Lyapunov稳定性理论验证稳定性与收敛性。

Result: 通过仿真与真实世界实验验证所提控制方法，实验结果显示多机器人能够成功协同，将物体推向移动的期望方向，且在无机器人间通信及未知物体质量条件下工作。

Conclusion: 该工作表明无需机器人间通信的分布式级联力控制可有效实现多机器人协同推动物体追踪移动目标，软触觉感知与级联控制结合提升了对未知物体质量的鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a distributed cascade force control system (DCFC) for multiple robots with the aim of pushing a rigid object towards a desired moving target without their inter-robot communication. These mobile robots are equipped with 360-degree vision-based soft tactile sensors utilized to determine contact location and resultant impact force. By investigating the dynamics of moving rigid objects on the flat, we proposed a distributed cascade control. The inner loop control incorporates contact force and positioning, ensuring the robots' pushing contact and application of the desired force to the object. The outer loop control coordinates the robots to push the object in a desired direction without inter-robot communication, regardless of the unknown object mass. The stability and convergence of the system are verified using the Lyapunov stability theory. We also conducted simulation and real-word experiments to validate the performance of the proposed control method, and the experimental results showcase the successful coordination of multiple robots in pushing an object towards a moving desired direction.

</details>


### [276] [ExStereo: Lifting 2D Vision-Language-Action Models to 3D with Explicit Stereo Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04805)
*I-Chun Arthur Liu,Jason Chen,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExStereo: 给预训练2D VLA加装立体（双目）感知模块，通过几何重建+多视角渲染生成显式立体表征，并用action-stereo cross-attention让动作token按需关注3D信息，配合大规模立体数据自监督mid-training，在π0.5与SmolVLA上仿真+真机双臂PiPER均超基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目RGB恢复度量深度与精确3D物体位姿本质上是病态问题，而多数VLA仅依赖RGB观测，导致高精度操作缺乏3D几何信息。

Method: 基于立体匹配基础模型构建ExStereo模块：从双目图像对重建场景几何并将多视角观测渲染为显式立体表征以提取立体token；提出action-stereo cross-attention，使action expert的动作token选择性关注立体token；在任务post-training前加入基于大规模立体数据自监督目标的mid-training以学习鲁棒3D表征。

Result: 微调π0.5与SmolVLA两个公开VLA，在仿真与真实双臂PiPER平台上ExStereo微调版本一致优于基线。

Conclusion: 将立体3D感知以模块化方式注入已有2D VLA，可有效提升机器人操作策略性能，且无需重训基座模型。

Abstract: Three-dimensional perception is critical for robotic manipulation, particularly for high-precision tasks, as recovering metric depth and precise 3D object positions from monocular RGB observations is inherently ill-posed. However, many Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models rely solely on RGB observations for perception. Leveraging recent advances in foundation models for stereo matching, we introduce ExStereo, a stereo module that augments pre-trained 2D VLAs with 3D perception. ExStereo reconstructs scene geometry from stereo image pairs and renders multi-view observations as an explicit stereo representation for stereo feature extraction. The action tokens from the action expert selectively attend to the resulting stereo tokens through our proposed action-stereo cross-attention mechanism, enabling the policy to generate robot actions conditioned on 3D scene information. To learn robust 3D representations, we introduce a mid-training stage before task-specific post-training, using a self-supervised learning objective on large-scale stereo data. We validate our approach by fine-tuning two publicly available VLAs, $\pi_{0.5}$ and SmolVLA, and evaluate them in simulation and on a real-world bimanual PiPER platform. Across both settings, VLAs fine-tuned with ExStereo consistently outperform baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of stereo perception for robotic manipulation. Our project website is at: this https URL .

</details>


### [277] [Tackling Sim-to-Real Mismatch Through Sampling-Based Disturbance Observers: From Analytical Models to Learned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04896)
*Tianqi Zhu,Jun Yang,Jianliang Mao,Cong Li,Shihua Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于采样的扰动观测器（SDOB），将经典扰动观测器原理从显式被控对象模型拓展到仿真器和学习世界模型，通过状态滚动或代价查询接口分离并估计状态效应扰动与代价扰动，从而补偿sim-to-real不匹配并提升控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人控制器日益依赖解析模型、仿真器、代价查询接口和学习世界模型，但实物部署常偏离名义假设，即使模型本身准确也可能出现额外扰动。经典DOB仅针对显式被控对象模型，无法用于仿真器或学习模型这类只能通过滚动/代价查询访问的模型。

Method: 提出采样式扰动观测器（SDOB）。它通过状态滚动或代价查询接口与模型交互，将可观测性分为两条通道：状态效应扰动（反馈状态与预测状态不一致）和代价扰动（同一查询状态在感知环境变化下获得不同代价），并分别进行估计与补偿。

Result: 在传统模型与学习模型上进行了多样化的仿真与真机实验，结果表明SDOB能有效补偿sim-to-real不匹配并改善控制性能。

Conclusion: SDOB把扰动观测从显式模型推广到仿真器与学习世界模型，提供了一种通用的扰动估计与补偿框架，适用于更广泛的机器人控制场景。

Abstract: Robotic controllers increasingly rely on analytical models, simulators, cost-query interfaces, and learned world models. However, physical deployment can deviate from nominal assumptions, and additional disturbances may arise even when the model itself is accurate. In control systems, disturbance observers (DOB) are widely used to estimate such unmeasured effects from nominal models and measured feedback. Classical DOB formulations are generally built around explicit plant models. This paper develops the sampling-based disturbance observer (SDOB), extending the DOB principle to a broader range of models, including simulators and learned world models, through state-rollout or cost-query interfaces. SDOB separates two observable channels: state-effect disturbances, for which the feedback state differs from its prediction, and cost disturbances, for which the same query state receives different costs as the perceived environment changes. Diverse simulation and real-robot experiments across traditional and learned models demonstrate the effectiveness of SDOB in compensating for sim-to-real mismatch and improving control performance.

</details>


### [278] [GJK-CBF: Control Barrier Functions for Convex Rigid Body Collision Avoidance on SE(3)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04910)
*Yi-Hsuan Chen,Shuo Liu,Wei Xiao,Michael Otte,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GJK-CBF：利用 GJK 算法实时获取最近点/穿透见证点对，直接由刚体相对运动构造 CBF 梯度，避免可微优化开销，实现凸体间无碰撞运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸体间避碰是机器人基础问题，CBF 因计算高效适合实时安全滤波。但一般凸体的精确分离度量需通过优化求解，现有 CBF 公式依赖可微优化（diffOpt）获取梯度，带来额外计算开销，限制实时性。

Method: 用 Gilbert-Johnson-Keerthi (GJK) 算法获取当前见证点对（实现最小距离或穿透深度的点对），据此构造 CBF（GJK-CBF），其梯度直接由见证点对的刚体相对运动构造，无需 diffOpt。适用于 2D/3D 及广泛凸体对，条件为每个一一交互中至少一方严格凸。

Result: 在多种场景验证：2D/3D 多机器人位置互换、3D 竖直窄缝穿越、机械臂应用。所有场景实现无碰撞运动，并减少几何近似带来的保守性，尤其在狭窄环境中改善明显。

Conclusion: GJK-CBF 提供了一种无需可微优化、计算高效的凸体避碰安全滤波方案，兼顾 2D/3D 与机械臂，在窄环境中降低保守性，具备实用价值。

Abstract: Collision avoidance among convex bodies is a fundamental problem in robotics. Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) provide a practical framework for real-time safety filtering due to their computational efficiency. For general convex bodies, exact separation measures, such as distance or scaling factor, are typically computed through optimization. Existing CBF formulations often obtain the required gradient via differentiable optimization (diffOpt), adding computational overhead. In contrast, we leverage the Gilbert-Johnson-Keerthi (GJK) algorithm to obtain the current witness pair---the pair of points realizing the minimum distance or penetration depth---and formulate a CBF, termed GJK-CBF, whose gradient is constructed directly from the relative rigid-body motion of the witness pair, without resorting to diffOpt. This formulation applies to both 2D and 3D environments across a broad class of convex body pairs, provided that at least one in each one-to-one interaction is strictly convex. The proposed GJK-CBF is validated in various scenarios, including multi-robot position swapping in both 2D and 3D, navigation through a vertical slit in 3D, and its applicability to manipulators. The results demonstrate collision-free motion across all scenarios while reducing the conservativeness introduced by geometric approximations, particularly in narrow environments.

</details>


### [279] [$R^2$-WAM: Repair-and-Reject Post-Training for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04913)
*Ruiyan Xu,Haisheng Su,Sixu Lin,Zhaokun Yue,Chengming Hu,Xin Jin,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 R²-WAM 两阶段“修复-拒绝”后训练框架：先以运动学对齐分数让视频预测忠实反映输入动作，再据此进行负样本拒绝式微调，实现无需环境交互的策略精炼，RoboTwin 2.0 平均成功率 93.8%，真实折叠衬衫任务 87.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）通过预测采样动作的后果来精炼策略，但视觉上逼真的预测可能并未真正反映输入动作，从而误导策略精炼。核心问题是预测未来与输入动作之间的不一致。

Method: 两阶段 repair-and-reject 后训练框架。修复阶段：用运动学对齐分数（衡量预测运动与演示运动的一致性）将想象锚定到真实机器人行为，使预测视频忠实反映输入动作。拒绝阶段：用修复后的视频模型比较采样动作与演示动作的想象后果，对任务进展低于演示参考超过预设阈值的样本施加负向微调。不增加环境交互，也不改变推理流程。

Result: RoboTwin 2.0 在干净与随机化设置下平均成功率 93.8%；长时域真实世界 Fold Shirt 任务平均成功率 87.5%，对照 Fast-WAM 为 0%。

Conclusion: 通过修复预测-动作一致性并据此做负样本拒绝，视频预测被从表征学习推进到基于后果的策略精炼，且无需额外环境交互或修改推理流程。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) emerge as a promising foundation for policy refinement by predicting the consequences of sampled actions. However, visually plausible predictions can mislead policy refinement if they fail to reflect the input actions. To address this mismatch, we introduce $R^2$-WAM, a two-stage repair-and-reject post-training framework that first improves the consistency of predicted futures with input actions, then uses these futures to select inferior action samples for negative fine-tuning. The repair stage grounds imagination in observed robot behavior through a kinematic alignment score that measures agreement between predicted and demonstrated motion, enabling the predicted video to faithfully reflect its input actions. Using the repaired video model, the rejection stage compares imagined outcomes of sampled and demonstrated actions, selectively applying negative fine-tuning to samples whose predicted task progress falls below the demonstrated reference by a prescribed margin. Together, the two stages extend video prediction from representation learning to consequence-based policy refinement without additional environment interaction or changes to the inference procedure. $R^2$-WAM achieves 93.8% average success on RoboTwin 2.0 across clean and randomized settings. On the long-horizon real-world Fold Shirt task, it achieves 87.5% average success, compared with 0% for Fast-WAM.

</details>


### [280] [PreAct-Nav: Agentic Reasoning Before Action for Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04916)
*Jing Xie,Shouwei Ruan,Yubin Wang,Yuxiang Zhang,Junwei Yang,Songchang Jin,Dianxi Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PreAct-Nav 智能体导航框架，为冻结策略配备"前瞻式"推理：用导航记忆维护中程子目标，用动作条件世界模型构建预测沙箱，再由 VLM 在当前子目标下审视预测后果并决定保留/修正动作，执行后用真实观测校正假设并更新记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市级导航要求具身智能体仅凭第一视角观测、通过局部决策完成长时程目标。现有方法依赖对瞬时观测或有限历史的语言推理，难以预见动作后果与未来环境状态，导致远景目标无法转化为连贯的局部决策，在长距离、多转弯路线中尤为突出。

Method: 1) 导航记忆模块：跨决策维持活跃中程子目标与相关经验，将远景目标分解为可执行中间目标；2) 预测式世界沙箱：以动作条件世界模型（AC-WM）对候选运动进行世界动态预测；3) VLM 推理器：在子目标上下文中解读预测，做出保留或修订动作的决策；4) 执行后用真实观测评估结果、修正不一致假设并更新记忆，以延续或重构子目标。整体为冻结策略 + 前瞻推理的智能体封装。

Result: 大量评测表明，PreAct-Nav 通过记忆更新与视觉预测改善了动作选择；在更长路线与转弯更多的路线上提升更为显著，说明前瞻式中程子目标与预测校验对长时程城市导航的增益随任务复杂度增加而放大。

Conclusion: 将局部决策锚定在持续的中程子目标上、在执行前评估预测动作后果、并用真实结果持续更新推理上下文，可显著提升大尺度物理环境中的具身导航鲁棒性。摘要未给出具体基线与数值指标，也未见真实城市部署证据。

Abstract: Urban navigation requires embodied agents to pursue long-horizon goals through local decisions based on egocentric observations. However, existing agentic navigation methods often struggle to translate distant goals into coherent local decisions in large-scale physical environments. Their reliance on linguistic reasoning over transient observations or limited history constrains anticipation of the consequences of actions and future conditions, despite the importance of such foresight for navigating long and complex urban routes. To bridge this gap, we propose PreAct-Nav, an agentic navigation framework that equips frozen policies with anticipatory reasoning for robust urban navigation. Our central idea is to anchor local decisions in persistent medium-horizon subgoals, assess the consequences of predicted actions before execution, and continually update the reasoning context using actual outcomes. At its core, a navigation memory module maintains the active subgoal and relevant experience across decisions, translating distant goals into actionable intermediate objectives. We further introduce a predictive world sandbox that uses an action-conditioned world model (AC-WM) to forecast world dynamics conditioned on candidate movements. A vision-language model (VLM) reasoner interprets these predictions under the current subgoal to retain or revise actions. After execution, real observations are used to assess outcomes, correct inconsistent assumptions, and update memory to continue or reformulate the subgoal. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that the proposed PreAct-Nav improves action selection through memory updates and visual prediction, with more pronounced gains on longer routes and routes with more turns.

</details>


### [281] [Nudge Before You Push: Physics-Aware Navigation via Tactile Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04924)
*Xianyao Li,Fang Xu,Ruitong Tian,Bowen Sun,Xiao Hu,Yang Ye,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TANav导航框架：用一次轻推测量容器推动阻力，TacPhys从力序列（可辅以RGB-D与运动学）估计质量以授权推动；重复巡逻规划器权衡探测与路径成本，可请求二次接触并跨次复用观测。仿真中降低漏推率（28.7%→5.5%）与边界违规率（4.3%→2.9%），四足机械臂上零样本MAE 0.28–1.07 kg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉上完全相同的容器可能装载不同重量的物品，需要不同的搬运/推动决策。仅靠视觉（RGB-D）无法可靠判断推动阻力，导致盲目绕行或误推高负载容器，产生安全边界违规与不必要的人工干预。

Method: TANav在巡逻中施加短暂轻推以测量推动阻力，在站点定义的操作边界下进行导航决策。TacPhys以力序列为输入（可选RGB-D与运动学），回归质量估计用于推动授权。重复巡逻规划器联合权衡探测成本与路径成本，必要时请求第二次接触，并在多次到访间复用观测。硬件为带霍尔阵列指尖的四足机械臂。

Result: 仿真中TacPhys逼近仅依赖阻力的贝叶斯参考；在同等低风险操作点下，相对峰值力阈值法将漏推率从28.7%降至5.5%。重复巡逻仿真中恢复oracle路径节省的90%，人工干预相比始终绕行减少一半以上，边界违规从4.3%降至2.9%（对比仅RGB-D探测）。真机上离线零样本MAE为0.28–1.07 kg（容器≤2.82 kg）；3 kg力上升标定给出93.5%汇总离线准确率（非立方体样本86.4%）；独立原始峰值规则在未见箱体上达20次中15次在线决策正确。

Conclusion: 一次轻推触觉探测即可有效区分视觉相同但负载不同的容器，将触觉质量估计与巡逻规划耦合，可同时降低漏推、人工干预与安全边界违规，并在四足机械臂上完成零样本与在线验证，体现触觉-导航融合的可行性。

Abstract: Visually identical containers can conceal loads that require different handling decisions. We present TANav, which uses a brief nudge to measure push resistance for navigation under a site-defined handling boundary. TacPhys reads the force sequence, with optional RGB-D and kinematics, into a mass estimate for push authorization. A repeated-patrol planner weighs probe and route costs, requests a second contact when useful, and reuses observations across visits. In simulation, TacPhys approaches a resistance-only Bayes reference and reduces missed pushes from 28.7% to 5.5% relative to peak-force thresholding at comparable low-risk operating points. In repeated-patrol simulation, TANav recovers 90% of the oracle's path saving, more than halves human interventions relative to always-detour, and reduces boundary violations from 4.3% to 2.9% relative to RGB-D-only probing. On a quadruped manipulator with a Hall-array fingertip, offline zero-shot MAE is 0.28-1.07 kg on containers up to 2.82 kg. Force-rise calibration at 3 kg gives 93.5% pooled offline accuracy (86.4% on non-cube episodes); a separate raw-peak rule gives 15 of 20 correct online decisions on unseen boxes.

</details>


### [282] [RobotUse: Allocating Computation, Context, and Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04929)
*Junhoo Lee,Injun Baek,Seungyeon Kim,Suhyun Jeon,Minkyu Kim,Baekseung Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RobotUse机器人agent框架，将计算、上下文与决策围绕物理动作的指定与修订组织，视觉选目标位姿、后端处理几何规划控制，子agent保留子目标交互细节，持续harnessing通过持久playbook学习，RoboLab上成功率45%超CaP-X 6.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人agent接口把动作选择埋在预定义工具中，或要求agent管理详细执行代码与不断膨胀的历史，导致难以将意图与观测结果连接、难以在重复尝试中修订决策。

Method: RobotUse harness：agent视觉选择目标和位姿，后端负责几何、运动规划与控制；子agent在子目标内保留详细交互并回传后续决策所需信息；continual harnessing利用执行经验更新持久playbook。

Result: RoboLab任务成功率45%，比CaP-X高6.7个百分点，决策上下文紧凑、对预定义动作抽象依赖降低；并展示在真实世界不完美反馈下仍能学习并迁移到后续任务。

Conclusion: 围绕动作指定与修订组织接口，可让机器人agent在紧凑上下文下持续从真实执行中学习并跨任务迁移，减少对预定义动作抽象的依赖。

Abstract: Robot agents must connect their intended actions to observed outcomes while retaining the context needed to revise their choices over repeated attempts. Existing interfaces often leave these choices inside predefined tools or require agents to manage detailed execution code and its growing history. We introduce RobotUse, a robot agent harness that organizes computation, context, and decisions around specifying and revising physical actions. Agents visually select targets and poses, while the backend handles geometry, motion planning, and control. Subagents retain detailed interactions within each subgoal and return the information needed for subsequent decisions. Continual harnessing lets agents learn from execution by updating a persistent playbook. On RoboLab, RobotUse achieves 45% task success, outperforming CaP-X by 6.7 percentage points while maintaining compact decision contexts and reducing reliance on predefined action abstractions. Furthermore, we show that RobotUse learns from real-world execution despite imperfect feedback and transfers what it learns to subsequent tasks. Project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [283] [DiVeR: Decision-Critical Verifier Learning for VLA Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04933)
*Seongheon Park,Heecheol Kim,Shulin Tian,Lilika Makabe,Namiko Saito,Katsushi Ikeuchi,Sharon Li,Yasuyuki Matsushita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiVeR 通过采样动作表示的离散度估计决策关键性，对 verifier 学习进行重加权，聚焦于对动作选择最具影响的稀疏关键状态，无需步级标注或额外环境交互，在 LIBERO、RoboCasa 与真实 Franka FR3 上提升 verifier 引导的动作选择成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略受限于机器人数据成本，verifier 引导的测试时扩展是高效替代方案，但现有基于分类的 verifier 从轨迹级结果学习，对所有访问状态一视同仁，而轨迹中只有稀疏的决策关键状态能提供有效的候选区分信号。

Method: 从采样动作表示的离散度（dispersion）估计每个状态的决策关键性，并用该信号对 verifier 训练损失进行重加权，使学习偏向动作选择后果最重大的状态；不需要步级标注，也不需要额外的环境交互。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboCasa 及 Franka Research 3 真实机器人实验上，DiVeR 通过更有效的 verifier 引导动作选择持续提升任务成功率，且 verifier 推理开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 状态级决策关键性重加权是提升 verifier 引导测试时扩展的有效且低成本手段，为缓解机器人数据瓶颈提供了可扩展方向。

Abstract: Scaling robot data and model capacity has improved Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies, but further progress is constrained by the high cost of robotic data. Verifier-guided test-time scaling offers an efficient alternative by sampling multiple action candidates and selecting the one most likely to lead to task success at inference time. Existing classification-based verifiers learn from trajectory-level outcomes but treat all visited states equally, even though their value for candidate discrimination can vary across a trajectory. At many states, plausible actions are similar and provide limited discrimination signal, while only a sparse set of decision-critical states admits meaningfully different actions that can substantially affect downstream outcomes. To address this, we propose DiVeR, which estimates decision criticality from the dispersion of sampled action representations. DiVeR then uses this signal to reweight verifier learning toward states where action selection is most consequential, without requiring step-level annotations or additional environment interaction. Across LIBERO, RoboCasa, and real-world experiments on a Franka Research 3 robot, DiVeR consistently improves task success through more effective verifier-guided action selection, while adding negligible verifier inference overhead.

</details>


### [284] [A Unified Dynamics Framework for Reinforcement Learning and Classical Control of a Six-DOF Pipeline-Tracking ROV in NVIDIA Isaac Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04949)
*Cheng Siong Chin,M. Venkateshkumar,Jianhua Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于单一 USD 场景统一供参的六自由度 ROV 管线跟踪架构，用 Fossen 方程在 NumPy 向量化环境与 Isaac Sim/PhysX 部署中逐步施加相同动力学，比较 5 种 RL 与 6 种经典控制器；Coriolis 项不破坏稳定性且轻微提升精度（TRPO 横向 RMS 误差降约 19%），PPO/TRPO/反馈线性化实现 100% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下航行器 RL 控制器通常在一种物理表示上训练、在另一种上部署，报告性能难以外推到训练之外的真实行为；需构建训练与部署同源动力学、可公平对比学习与经典控制的方法框架。

Method: 以单一 USD 场景为参数源，同时驱动向量化 NumPy Fossen 船舶运动方程实现与 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 交互式部署（同方程作为逐步 PhysX 力）；Coriolis-离心项在两支路中均作为主动力学模型，并对 PPO/TRPO 做受控消融；通过 checkpoint 兼容层用同一评测代码对 PPO、SAC、TD3、DDPG、TRPO 五算法统一环境/奖励/随机化 harness 打分；再扩展 PID、滑模、模糊、反馈线性化、MPC、ANFIS 六种经典基线，共享同一制导几何与推力分配。

Result: Coriolis 项未导致 PPO/TRPO 失稳，TRPO 横向 RMS 误差约降 19%；Coriolis 启用下 PPO、TRPO、反馈线性化取得 100% 成功率与有竞争力精度；PID、模糊、ANFIS 亦 100% 成功但跟踪较松；DDPG/TD3 呈现可解释的特定失效模式而非 off-policy 学习普遍弱点；经典控制仍为强基线。

Conclusion: 以单一物理参数源统一训练与部署是可信的 sim-to-sim/仿真-部署一致性方案，Coriolis 建模安全有益，经典控制在水下 ROV 任务中仍可与最优学习策略抗衡。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning controllers for underwater vehicles are usually trained against one physics representation and deployed against another, so reported performance does not always describe behavior outside training. This paper presents a pipeline-tracking architecture for a six-degree-of-freedom remotely operated vehicle (ROV) in which one Universal Scene Description (USD) scene supplies the real BlueROV2-Heavy mass, added-mass, damping, buoyancy, and thruster parameters to both halves of the system: a vectorized NumPy implementation of Fossen's marine-craft equations, and an interactive NVIDIA Isaac Sim deployment applying the identical equations as PhysX forces at every step. The Coriolis-centripetal term is the primary dynamics model in both branches; a controlled ablation on PPO and TRPO shows that including it does not destabilize either algorithm and modestly improves tracking, about 19 percent lower standoff RMS error for TRPO. Five reinforcement learning algorithms, PPO, soft actor-critic, TD3, DDPG, and TRPO, are trained against one environment, reward, and randomized evaluation harness through a checkpoint-compatibility layer scoring any policy with the same code. The pipeline is extended with six classical baselines, PID, sliding-mode, fuzzy logic, feedback linearization, model predictive control, and an adaptive neuro-fuzzy inference system, driven by the same guidance geometry and thruster allocation as the learned policies. Under Coriolis-enabled dynamics, PPO, TRPO, and feedback linearization reach the strongest combination of 100 percent success and competitive accuracy; PID, fuzzy control, and the neuro-fuzzy baseline also reach 100 percent success with looser tracking; DDPG and TD3 each show a specific, explainable failure mode rather than a general weakness of off-policy learning; and classical control remains a strong baseline against the best learned policies.

</details>


### [285] [FreeLoc: Online Floorplan Localization via Diffusion-Aided Pose Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05011)
*Haocheng Peng,Boyang Zhou,Jiarui Hu,Xiyue Guo,Ziyang Zhang,Boming Zhao,Yifan Gao,Xiao Li,Hujun Bao,Zhaopeng Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FreeLoc将平面图直接作为可查询几何地图，通过在线射线查询与扩散细化实现连续位姿估计，无需离线数据库，在单帧与序列室内定位上达到SOTA且支持实时在线推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于平面图的高性能室内定位方法仍需将平面图转换为密集的场景专属离线数据库，导致精度、存储与运行时强耦合于离散位姿空间的采样分辨率，限制了可扩展性与部署灵活性。作者希望把平面图保留为可直接查询的几何先验，避免数据库构建开销。

Method: FreeLoc是一个在线RGB平面图定位框架。核心包含：(1) 高效的在线几何查询与扩散辅助细化机制，通过即时平面图射线查询检索合理的位姿锚点，再借助扩散过程将锚点细化为连续精确位姿；(2) 面向序列定位的在线似然构建策略，从粗采样候选与细化后的位姿假设中构造似然，桥接单帧定位与概率时序融合，从而支持直方图滤波的时序融合而无需离线数据库。

Result: 实验表明FreeLoc支持实时在线推理，在单帧与序列定位任务上均取得当前最优性能；真实世界实验验证了其在室内机器人定位场景中的实际可部署性。

Conclusion: FreeLoc证明平面图可作为直接可查询的几何地图用于在线RGB定位，摆脱对密集离线数据库的依赖，兼顾精度、存储与运行时，为室内机器人定位提供了实用且可部署的方案。

Abstract: Floorplans provide compact and widely available geometric maps for indoor localization, but existing high-performing floorplan-based methods still convert them into dense scene-specific offline databases, tying accuracy, storage, and runtime to the sampling resolution of the discretized pose space. We present FreeLoc, an online RGB-based floorplan localization framework that treats the floorplan as a directly queryable geometric map. FreeLoc introduces an efficient online geometric querying and diffusion-aided refinement scheme, which retrieves plausible pose anchors through on-the-fly floorplan ray querying and refines them into accurate continuous pose estimates. For sequential localization, FreeLoc develops an online likelihood construction strategy that bridges single-frame localization and probabilistic temporal fusion by constructing likelihoods from coarse-sampled candidates and refined pose hypotheses, enabling histogram-filter-based temporal fusion without offline databases. Experiments demonstrate real-time online inference and state-of-the-art performance in both single-frame and sequential localization, while real-world results validate practical deployability in indoor robotic localization scenarios.

</details>


### [286] [Now You Feel It, Now You See Me: Digital-Twin-based Teleoperation Interface for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05081)
*Youngchan Shim,Kyutae Lee,JooYun Kim,Jaeseong Hwang,Harim Ji,Yongseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于数字孪生的AR遥操作界面，将裸手跟踪、双臂机械手与虚实物体表征结合，以力可视化（视触觉反馈）与遮挡感知透明/虚拟视角渲染缓解无力反馈与遮挡问题，并通过两项用户任务评估其在力调控与遮挡下演示采集中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 裸手跟踪遥操作可免手套/外骨骼地采集灵巧操作演示，但存在两个关键反馈缺失：无力反馈（操作者只能目测推断接触力，影响精度与安全）和被物体或机器人自身遮挡区域无可视反馈（难以判断手部位置、接近距离与适配物体形状的预抓取构型）。

Method: 构建数字孪生驱动的AR遥操作接口：集成裸手跟踪、双臂机械手、机器人与任务物体的虚拟表征；将机械手网格上的触觉测量渲染为“视-力”反馈（力可视化）；提供用户可控虚拟视角面板或遮挡感知透明渲染以缓解遮挡。

Result: 通过Task 1（细致力调控）与Task 2（遮挡下操作）的用户研究评估界面，考察力可视化与视觉辅助对演示采集的支持效果；原文未给出具体量化指标。

Conclusion: 数字孪生+AR的反馈增强方案可在无力反馈与强遮挡条件下提升演示采集的可操作性，为灵巧操作数据收集提供可行接口设计，但结论主要基于小规模用户研究。

Abstract: Teleoperation is becoming increasingly important for collecting high-quality demonstrations to teach robots dexterous manipulation skills. For dexterous manipulation, bare-hand tracking provides a practical way to control robotic hands and demonstrate coordinated finger movements without gloves or exoskeletons. However, this type of teleoperation faces two key feedback limitations: a lack of force feedback and visual feedback of occluded region. The absence of force feedback hinders precise and safe manipulation, as operators must infer contact force visually rather than feel them directly. Occlusion by objects or other robot parts impairs the assessment of hand positions, approach distances, and pre-grasp configurations suited to the object's shape. To address these limitations, we propose a digital-twin-based augmented reality (AR) teleoperation interface that integrates bare-hand tracking, bimanual robotic hands, and virtual representations of the robot and task objects. The interface renders tactile measurements on robot hand meshes as visuo-force feedback. Furthermore, it offers a user-controlled virtual view panel or occlusion-aware transparency rendering to provide occlusion-mitigating visual feedback. We evaluated the interface through user studies involving Task 1 and Task 2, examining how force visualization and visual assistance support demonstration collection in tasks requiring careful force regulation and manipulation under occlusion.

</details>


### [287] [PB-STDG: A Prediction-Based Short-Term Decentralized Greedy Guidance Algorithm for a Drone Road System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05122)
*Zhouyu Qu,Andreas Willig,Xiaobing Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PB-STDG去中心化贪心引导算法，通过信标包信息预测邻居无人机决策，缓解STDG过度保守问题，在结构化无人机道路系统中提升短期无冲突路径的交通效率并保持相当安全水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空域中多主体无人机数量激增，需协同避碰并确定无冲突短期路径；原始STDG算法存在过度保守问题，导致通行效率低下。

Method: 在结构化Drone Road System上扩展STDG：引入预测机制，利用信标包中共享的额外信息使无人机预判邻居的决策意向，据此进行去中心化贪心路径选择。

Result: 多场景仿真对比显示，PB-STDG在保持与STDG相当安全水平的同时提升了交通效率。

Conclusion: 通过在贪婪引导中嵌入邻居决策预测，可有效降低STDG的保守性，兼顾安全与效率，适用于城市无人机交通管理。

Abstract: In recent years, Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) or drones have been increasingly adopted in urban environments for applications such as parcel delivery, infrastructure inspection, emergency response, and drone light shows. A non-negligible issue is how to manage the increasing number of drones operated by different entities, to enable them to cooperatively avoid potential collisions and determine conflict-free short-term flight paths in urban airspace. This paper presents a Prediction-Based Short-Term Decentralized Greedy (PB-STDG) guidance algorithm for a structured Drone Road System (DRS). PB-STDG extends the original STDG algorithm by introducing a prediction mechanism that enables drones to anticipate the decisions of neighboring drones using additional information shared in beacon packets, aiming to address the over-conservative behavior observed in the STDG algorithm. Several simulation scenarios are conducted to evaluate and compare the proposed algorithm with STDG. The results show that PB-STDG improves traffic efficiency while maintaining a safety level comparable to that of STDG.

</details>


### [288] [Recursive Self-Improvement of Visuomotor Policies through Local Recovery Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05151)
*Yuzhi Zhang,Xinyu Liu,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于局部恢复监督的递归自我改进框架：离线审计定位失败状态、多模态教师智能体生成修复动作、冻结学生验证端点可续性，从而构建带质量权重与有效性掩码的连续训练窗口，仅部署学生策略。LIBERO-Goal 上 100 个验证场景成功 88（基线 78），robomimic Can 由 102/130 提升至 112/130，均等量 2000 步优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA/视觉运动策略能完成已学会的任务，但缺乏对自身失误的纠正行为。现有模仿学习数据只覆盖成功轨迹，失败状态处无监督信号，导致策略一旦偏离分布无法恢复。作者希望在不引入额外在线交互成本、不部署教师的前提下，通过离线递归的自改进循环补足恢复能力。

Method: 1) 循环式自我改进：每轮审计当前策略、在"可支撑的失败状态"生成纠正演示、用其更新策略以驱动下一轮采集。2) 离线审计器结合粗粒度与细粒度时间证据定位未解决失败并给出可观测的修复目标。3) 固定多模态智能体作为工具型教师，通过观察—计算—执行—反馈循环生成恢复动作。4) 冻结学生检验教师终点的可继续性，若续行失败则回滚该端点并延长演示。5) 动作级质量评估定义连续训练窗口，配以对齐观测、质量权重与有效性掩码。6) 仅学生策略部署。

Result: LIBERO-Goal 初步研究：恢复增强后训练在 100 个验证场景中成功 88 次，同一 π0 检查点原始数据继续训练为 78 次。robomimic Can 的 BC-RNN 研究用 26 段局部恢复片段将成功率从 102/130 提升至 112/130。两组对比优化步数均为 2000 步，属同类对照。作者自述为 preliminary study，未报告真机实验，未提及代码或开源释放。

Conclusion: 局部恢复监督可作为策略自我改进的有效信号源：将失败状态转化为带质量标注的纠正数据，能在不部署教师、不增加优化预算的条件下提升 VLA 策略的成功率，且循环结构支持多轮迭代改进。局限在于验证仅限仿真基准（LIBERO-Goal、robomimic Can）的小规模初步实验，真机泛化与大规模场景仍待验证。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies can execute familiar tasks yet lack the corrective behavior needed after their own mistakes. We present a framework for recursive self-improvement through local recovery supervision. Each round audits the current policy, generates corrective demonstrations at supported failure states, and uses them to update the policy that drives the next round of collection. An offline auditor locates unresolved failures using coarse and dense temporal evidence and specifies observable repair goals. A fixed multimodal agent acts as a tool-using teacher, generating recovery actions through observation, computation, execution, and feedback. The frozen student tests whether each teacher endpoint supports further progress. If continuation fails, the system restores that endpoint and extends the demonstration. Action-level quality assessment then defines continuous training windows with aligned observations, quality weights, and validity masks. Only the student is deployed. In a preliminary LIBERO-Goal study, recovery-augmented post-training achieves 88 successful episodes out of 100 validation scenes, compared with 78 for original-data continuation from the same $\pi_0$ checkpoint. An earlier BC-RNN study on robomimic Can improves success from 102/130 to 112/130 using 26 local recovery segments. Both comparisons match 2,000 additional optimization steps.

</details>


### [289] [VICON: Visual-Inertial-Contact based Hand-Object Tracking for Manipulation Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05180)
*Yubin Jeon,Uiseong Shin,Hwanchul La,Jaeseong Kang,Hyelim Choi,Yongseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VICON：视觉-惯性-接触融合的手-物联合追踪框架，用改造的力敏电阻手套+RGB-D相机同步采集手物运动、接触点与法向力，并提出可见性加权的因子图轨迹估计，在遮挡下实现低失败率与毫米级精度，并发布数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作学习需要包含接触点与接触力的自然人演示数据，而仅靠运动轨迹无法提供"在哪接触、用多大力"的监督；现有方法难以同时采集手、物体、接触点与力，且手物交互导致的严重遮挡使手与物体追踪都很困难。

Method: 1) 硬件：视觉-惯性手套+RGB-D相机，按人类抓握频率在手套上布置FSR，同步记录接触状态与标定后的法向力；2) 软件：无需CAD模型，用RGB-D图像与单目视频重建网格，提出因子图物体轨迹估计，融合可见性加权（遮挡下）的物体位姿估计、FSR测量与手部运动先验。

Result: 在5个物体、40个动作捕捉会话上，失败帧率2.5%，基线为50.9%–64.6%；遮挡条件下中位误差3.9 mm与3.0°。基于该系统构建了同步手物运动、接触点与法向力的数据集，并将公开覆盖10类物体的扩展数据集。

Conclusion: VICON以低成本传感器融合方案实现了遮挡鲁棒的手-物-接触联合追踪，为灵巧操作提供了含接触力监督的演示数据，并开源扩展数据集，利于后续模仿学习与接触感知研究。

Abstract: Learning dexterous manipulation benefits from human demonstration datasets that capture diverse and natural hand-object interactions. In particular, contact points and forces provide supervision on where and how strongly to interact, which cannot be fully captured by motion trajectories alone. However, methods for jointly capturing hand and object motion, contact points, and forces remain limited. Moreover, severe occlusion from hand-object interaction challenges accurate tracking of both hands and objects. To address these limitations, we present a Visual-Inertial-CONtact based hand-object tracking (VICON) framework. It holistically captures both hand and object motion along with contact information during manipulation, even under severe occlusion. First, we adopt a visual-inertial glove and an RGB-D camera for accurate hand tracking, and redesign the glove to incorporate contact sensing. Specifically, force-sensitive resistors (FSRs) are placed on the glove based on human grasp frequency to synchronously record contact states and calibrated normal forces. Second, without requiring pre-existing CAD models, we estimate object poses using RGB-D images and a mesh reconstructed from a monocular video. We propose factor-graph-based object trajectory estimation that fuses object-pose estimates weighted by visibility under hand-object occlusion, FSR measurements, and a hand-motion prior. Across 40 motion-capture sessions with five objects, VICON achieves a 2.5% failed-frame rate compared with 50.9-64.6% for the baselines, with median errors of 3.9 mm and 3.0 degrees under occlusion. Using VICON, we construct a dataset containing synchronized hand-object motion, contact points, and normal forces, and will publicly release an expanded dataset covering 10 object categories at this https URL .

</details>


### [290] [Vela: Scaling Vision-Language-Action Models with Adaptive Action Curve Parametrization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05230)
*Yifan Li,Jiaxu Wang,Dongming Wu,Yicheng Jiang,Ryan Ji,Xiangyu Yue,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Vela 提出用连续轨迹（样条表示 + 运动相关时间支撑）替代固定速率动作块，构建视觉-语言-动作基础模型，在多实体数据上预训练并在仿真与真实长时程任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 将未来运动表示为固定频率的动作块，把时间分辨率与预测时域绑定在固定输出预算上。这种逐点表示对高度相关的相邻动作造成容量浪费，使时间连续性与平滑性只能隐式学习，并在长时程覆盖与接触丰富操作所需的局部精度之间形成取舍。

Method: Vela 将未来机器人行为建模为连续轨迹：结合紧凑的样条动作表示、运动相关的时间支撑（motion-dependent temporal support）以及面向异构实体的共享动作接口，使固定输出预算能够随运动自适应调整时间分辨率；并在大规模多实体机器人数据上预训练。

Result: 在 LIBERO-X、EBench 以及两个真实世界长时程任务（蛋cake 烹饪、土豆切丝）上取得有前景的结果，仿真与物理操作均表现良好。

Conclusion: 结果显示了连续动作表示作为未来具身基础模型基础的潜力。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action models represent future motion as fixed-rate action chunks, tying temporal resolution and prediction horizon to a fixed output budget. This pointwise representation wastes capacity on highly correlated neighboring actions, leaves temporal continuity and smoothness to be learned implicitly, and forces a tradeoff between long-horizon coverage and the local precision required for contact-rich manipulation. To address these limitations, we introduce Vela, a vision-language-action foundation model that represents future robot behavior as continuous trajectories. Vela combines a compact spline-based action representation with motion-dependent temporal support and a shared action interface for heterogeneous embodiments, allowing a fixed output budget to adapt its temporal resolution across motions. We pretrain Vela on large-scale multi-embodiment robot data and evaluate it on LIBERO-X, EBench, and two real-world long-horizon tasks, egg-cake cooking and potato shredding, obtaining promising results across simulation and physical manipulation. These results highlight the potential of continuous action representations as a foundation for future embodied foundation models. Project page and more results: this https URL .

</details>


### [291] [Safe Ergodic Control for Multi-Robot Systems via Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05258)
*Yo Toyomoto,Mohamed Elobaid,Bryce L. Ferguson,Alberto Quattrini Li,Shinkyu Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于QP的统一框架，将遍历性(ergodicity)目标与安全性联合优化，实现机器人实时稠密时空环境监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全遍历控制依赖离线轨迹优化或分层架构，限制实时性，并将遍历目标与安全约束解耦。

Method: 引入时间可微的高斯核遍历度量，将度量的指数衰减作为时变控制屏障函数(CBF)约束并引入松弛变量，结合区域约束与机器人间避碰硬CBF约束，构建单一QP控制器。

Result: 证明QP在任意安全初始配置下可行，且(除松弛项外)遍历度量指数衰减。仿真优于基线，并在三架无人机上完成实机验证。

Conclusion: 联合遍历性与安全的QP控制器可实时运行，具备理论保证并获真实硬件验证，适用于密集时空环境监测。

Abstract: Ergodic control drives robots to spend time in each region in proportion to a spatial distribution of interest, making it well suited for dense spatiotemporal environmental monitoring. Existing safe ergodic controllers rely on offline trajectory optimization or hierarchical architectures, which limit real-time applicability and decouple the ergodicity objective from the safety constraint. This paper presents a quadratic programming (QP)-based controller that treats ergodicity and safety jointly. We first introduce a Gaussian-kernel ergodic metric that, unlike the classical indicator-based metric, is time differentiable. This allows the exponential decay of the metric to be imposed as a time-varying control barrier function (CBF) constraint, relaxed by a slack variable, alongside hard CBF constraints for region containment and inter-robot collision avoidance. We establish that the resulting QP remains feasible from any safe initial configuration and that, up to the slack term, the ergodic metric decays exponentially. Simulations show improved performance over baseline methods, and experiments with three aerial vehicles validate the work on real hardware.

</details>


### [292] [CoDance: Learning Reactive and Compliant Human-Humanoid Interaction from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05324)
*Zhuoqun Chen,Shucheng Jia,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoDance：从单人视频学习人形机器人跟随舞伴、保持双手接触的反应式柔顺共舞框架，含多连杆柔顺增强与力感知训练，真机实现前后交替的持续双人舞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人–人形机器人的伴随交互需要在运动过程中维持连续物理接触，机器人既要跟随人的运动、又要对交互力做出柔顺响应并保持稳定自然的步态。现有方法多针对孤立动作模仿或非接触式跟随，缺少"从视频中学习带力的接触式双人协调"的方案；双人舞是可同时考验脚步协调与双手持续接触的困难实例。

Method: 给定一段两名人类舞者的单目视频，CoDance 将动作重定向为机器人参考轨迹与一个"移动舞伴"参考；提出多连杆柔顺增强（multi-link compliance augmentation），在双手处施加结构化力并据此调整机器人参考，把纯运动学演示转化为带力感知的训练数据；在此数据上训练策略，使其跟随观察到的舞伴、保留演示的运动风格并对物理交互力作出柔顺响应。

Result: 仿真中策略能随舞伴变化调整脚步，复现约 80% 的增强演示所编码的手腕位移；在真实人形机器人上实现与人舞伴持续双手共舞，并可重复进行前进—后退动作切换。

Conclusion: 视频演示加结构化力增强可有效为零样本的真机人–人形接触式交互提供可训练信号，视频到机器人参考的转换与柔顺增强是关键；该范式可扩展到更一般的伴随接触式全身协调任务。

Abstract: Partnered human-humanoid interaction couples locomotion with continuous physical contact. A humanoid needs to coordinate with a person's motion while responding to interaction forces and maintaining stable and natural movement. We present CoDance, a framework for learning reactive and compliant human-humanoid interaction from video. We study partnered dancing as a challenging instantiation, where a humanoid coordinates its footsteps with a moving partner and maintains continuous two-hand contact. Given a single video of two human dancers, CoDance retargets their motions into a robot reference and a moving partner. We introduce a multi-link compliance augmentation that transforms the kinematic demonstration into force-aware training data by adapting the robot reference under structured forces at both hands. Policies trained on this data follow the observed partner while preserving the demonstrated locomotion style and responding compliantly to physical interaction. In simulation, the policies adapt their footsteps to changes in the partner and reproduce approximately 80% of the wrist displacement encoded by the augmented demonstrations. On a physical humanoid, CoDance enables sustained two-hand dancing with a human partner including repeated transitions between forward and backward motions.

</details>


### [293] [VAMPS: Visual and Motor Policies from Sampling-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05331)
*Mohamed Yassine Kabouri,Pietro Noah Crestaz,Quang-Nam Nguyen,Qilong Cheng,Ludovic Righetti,Nicolas Mansard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VAMPS 用 MPPI 采样规划为策略提供监督信号，支持仿真迭代训练本体感知策略并迁移至 Unitree Go2，也支持真机数据上直接训练视觉运动策略（ACT + 力感知），完成抓放与力控擦白板任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在物理系统上学习机器人策略面临数据采集昂贵与探索不安全的问题；希望无需人类示教即可利用采样规划生成可复用策略，并兼顾仿真到真机迁移与真机自主数据两条路径。

Method: 核心为 MPPI 规划器：本体感知一侧在仿真中迭代运行，策略热启动 MPPI、精炼轨迹作为新监督，配合学习到的终端价值改善短时域规划、IQL critic 指导策略更新；视觉运动一侧在真机（Flexiv Rizon 10S）由 MPPI 用任务特定状态估计规划执行并录制 RGB 与传感数据，再用 ACT 预测动作块降低有效预测时域，离线训练固定数据集，擦白板任务额外引入 6 维力/力矩与期望法向力。

Result: 迭代精炼优于在冻结 MPPI 数据上一次性训练；学习到的运动策略成功迁移到 Unitree Go2；真机上实现视觉运动抓取放置与力感知白板擦拭两类任务。

Conclusion: 采样规划可作为策略学习的数据与监督来源，VAMPS 既能走"仿真训练 + 硬件迁移"路线，也能直接利用真机自主采集数据训练可复用策略，无需人类示教。

Abstract: Learning robot policies directly on physical systems remains difficult because data collection is costly and policy exploration can be unsafe. We introduce Visual and Motor Policies from Sampling-Based Planning (VAMPS), a framework that uses Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control to train reusable policies without human demonstrations. VAMPS supports two training modes. For one-step proprioceptive policies, it operates iteratively in simulation: the policy warm-starts MPPI, and the refined trajectories provide new supervision as the policy changes. A learned terminal value improves short-horizon planning, while an Implicit Q-Learning (IQL) critic guides the policy update. Iterative refinement outperforms training once on frozen MPPI data, and we transfer the learned locomotion policy to a Unitree Go2. For visuomotor policies, VAMPS operates directly from real-robot data. MPPI uses task-specific state estimates to plan and execute trajectories while recording RGB and sensor observations on a Flexiv Rizon 10S. Action Chunking with Transformers predicts action chunks, reducing the effective prediction horizon, and is trained offline on this fixed dataset. We demonstrate visuomotor pick-and-place and force-aware whiteboard erasing. In the latter task, the policy additionally observes the measured $6$-D wrench and desired normal force. These results show that VAMPS can learn policies either in simulation followed by hardware transfer or directly from autonomously collected real-robot data.

</details>


### [294] [Optimal Control with Learned Critics under Unmodeled State Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05359)
*Philipp Schoch,Markus Ryll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出学习型 MPC 框架:在名义解析模型上叠加残差动力学网络补偿建模误差,并用学习到的动作价值 critic 为局部 iLQR 注入长时域 MDP 结构;配套 GPU 批量 iLQR 求解器并行求解数千条轨迹优化问题,实现高效基于模型的强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPC 依赖易优化的解析动力学模型,难以处理接触等难建模的状态依赖;而无模型 RL 数据效率低。作者希望在保留 MPC 结构化、约束感知优势的同时,用学习组件弥补动力学不完整与有限时域短视问题。

Method: 在名义解析动力学模型上并联残差动力学网络,从数据中学习缺失的状态依赖效应;将规划器与学习到的动作价值 critic 结合,把长时域 MDP 结构注入局部 iLQR 优化;开发 GPU 加速的批量化 iLQR 求解器,在最优控制循环内前向评估学习到的动力学与 critic 网络,并行求解数千条轨迹优化问题;整体系统集成于机器人仿真器。

Result: 在有偏与建模不完整的控制任务上,闭环控制性能优于基线,同时保持了基于模型的结构,可进行高效的约束轨迹优化;系统支持规模化基于模型的强化学习。

Conclusion: 学习型 MPC 结合局部基于模型规划的结构化优势与学习组件的数据效率,是处理不完整动力学下机器人控制的一种可行且可扩展的路径。但实验仅限仿真,缺乏真实机器人验证。

Abstract: Model Predictive Control (MPC) provides a structured and constraint-aware mechanism for decision-making, but its reliance on optimization-friendly analytical dynamics models limits its use in tasks with contacts and other hard-to-model state dependencies. Model-free reinforcement learning avoids explicit modeling assumptions but typically requires large amounts of interaction data. We present a learning-based MPC framework that combines the data efficiency and structure of local model-based planning with learned components that compensate for incomplete dynamics and finite-horizon myopia. The method augments a nominal analytical model with a residual dynamics network that learns missing state-dependent effects from data and combines the resulting planner with a learned action-value critic that injects long-horizon MDP structure into the local iLQR optimization. To make this practical at reinforcement-learning scale, we develop a GPU-accelerated batched iLQR solver that evaluates learned dynamics and critic networks inside the optimal-control loop and solves thousands of trajectory-optimization problems in parallel. The complete system is integrated into a robotics simulator, enabling scalable model-based reinforcement learning under incomplete dynamics. Experiments on biased and incompletely modeled control tasks show that the approach improves closed-loop control performance while preserving the model-based structure needed for efficient constrained trajectory optimization.

</details>


### [295] [TUCO: Curating Simulation Demonstrations for Sim-to-Real Robot Policy Co-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05407)
*Ning Zhu,Mengfei Zhao,Yikai Tang,Zhangyujie Sun,Peihao Li,Dongyue Ni,Jindou Jia,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TUCO: 首个面向sim-to-real策略协同训练的仿真数据筛选系统研究，用影响函数将每条源示教对目标域评分rollout的影响分解为“目标回报贡献”与“rollout间方差”，统一度量轨迹效用与集合覆盖，并用性能对齐子集优化器选取互补示教，在RoboMimic与OmniReset上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真示教可补充稀缺真实数据用于策略协同训练，但如何主动筛选（curation）这些示教以提升sim-to-real迁移尚缺系统研究；现有筛选方法也缺少基于闭环目标行为的统一准则，无法同时衡量轨迹级效用与集合级覆盖度。

Method: TUCO基于影响函数（influence functions）追踪每条源域示教对目标域评分rollout的影响；核心洞察是可将影响分解为对目标回报的整体贡献和跨rollout的方差，从而在闭环基础上统一度量轨迹效用与集合覆盖；进而提出性能对齐的子集优化器，将两类度量融合为统一筛选目标，以降低冗余、选出互补示教。

Result: 在RoboMimic与OmniReset上的大量实验验证了主动仿真数据筛选对sim-to-real策略协同训练的价值，TUCO在单仿真器、sim-to-sim与sim-to-real三种设定下均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 仿真示教数据筛选是提升sim-to-real协同训练的有效杠杆；以闭环影响分解为基础的效用-覆盖统一目标可指导互补子集选择，但真实机器人验证与开源情况在摘要中未充分说明。

Abstract: Simulation demonstrations can supplement scarce real-world data for robot policy co-training. However, the value of using data curation to actively select these demonstrations for sim-to-real co-training remains underexplored. Existing curation methods also lack a unified criterion for measuring trajectory-level utility and set-level coverage from closed-loop target behavior. To address these gaps, we present the first systematic study of data curation for sim-to-real robot policy co-training and propose Trajectory-level Utility and set-level Coverage Optimization (TUCO). TUCO uses influence functions to trace how each source demonstration affects target-domain scoring rollouts. Our key insight is that these effects can be decomposed into an overall contribution to target return and variation across rollouts, providing a common closed-loop basis for measuring trajectory utility and set coverage. We further propose a performance-aligned subset optimizer that combines these measures in a unified curation objective to reduce redundancy and select complementary demonstrations. Extensive experiments on RoboMimic and OmniReset establish the value of active simulation data curation for sim-to-real policy co-training and show that TUCO achieves state-of-the-art performance across single-simulator, sim-to-sim, and sim-to-real settings.

</details>


### [296] [RMMBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Robotic Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05414)
*Huapeng Li,Fuxiang Feng,Jinqiu Fan,Shuo Yang,Fengjiao Chen,Xuezhi Cao,Ran Song,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RMMBench，一个将高层与低层具身任务统一到同一框架的评测基准，包含70个"导航-操作"规范任务场景，覆盖从局部操作到长程复合导航的任务谱系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM提升了机器人的环境理解与任务推理，但现有基准缺乏对多样化机器人任务的综合评测方法，评价指标也较受限，难以细致刻画VLM的具身能力。

Method: 构建RMMBench：要求机器人在连续空间中理解语言指令并执行长程任务，将高层与低层具身任务无缝整合为统一框架，设计含70个规范场景的"导航-操作"任务套件，任务跨度从局部操作到长程复合移动操作。

Result: 评测显示主流VLM在移动操作任务中的空间定位仍面临重大挑战，并凸显长程交互中增强机器人空间感知能力的必要性。

Conclusion: RMMBench为VLM在机器人导航与操作中的整合提供了更细粒度的评测路径，并指出空间定位与空间感知是当前具身VLM的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Although the advancement of vision-language models (VLMs) has endowed robots with enhanced environmental understanding and task reasoning, a comprehensive evaluation methodology is important to advance the integration of VLMs in robotic navigation and manipulation. However, current benchmarks lack a comprehensive method to evaluate diverse robotic tasks, and evaluation metrics remain relatively constrained, making it difficult to assess the embodied capabilities of VLMs in a thorough and fine-grained manner. To address this issue, we propose RMMBench, an evaluation benchmark that requires robots to understand language instructions and perform long-horizon tasks in continuous spaces. RMMBench seamlessly integrates high- and low-level embodied tasks into a unified framework, constructing a "navigation-manipulation" task suite comprising 70 canonical task scenarios that range from localized manipulation to long-horizon composite navigation. The results reveal that leading VLMs still face major challenges in spatial localization when performing mobile manipulation tasks, and also highlight the necessity of enhancing the spatial perception capability of robots during long-horizon interactions. RMMBench can be accessed at this https URL

</details>


### [297] [EvoMem-VLA: State-Evolution Memory for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05418)
*Yuheng Na,Zhide Zhong,Junjie He,Junfeng Li,Haodong Yan,Jiaan Wang,Jiaguan Zhu,Yangyang Zheng,Tianyu Huang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoMem-VLA 通过显式编码历史状态间的变化来构建"状态演化记忆"，使 VLA 策略在长时程、依赖记忆的任务中保持对任务进度的追踪，并在 RMBench、RoboMME 与真实机器人任务上大幅超越此前 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型依赖当前观测，一旦与任务相关的证据离开视野即丢失，难以应对长时程、依赖记忆的任务。已有方法采用压缩历史特征或稀疏视觉关键帧，但孤立快照无法让策略判断过去交互中"发生了什么变化"以及"下一步该执行什么动作"。

Method: 提出 EvoMem-VLA，显式编码并保留历史状态之间的观测变化，形成状态演化记忆。核心是条件式 delta 分词（conditional delta tokenization）：将有序帧对编码为有方向、以源状态为条件的 delta token，并关联对应的状态证据。共享 VLM 主干支持任务自适应路由：普通长时程任务走直接动作路径，多阶段任务走子任务路径，先生成可执行子任务作为动作生成的额外输入。每个仿真基准训练单一联合策略。

Result: 在 RMBench 上成功率 80.7\%，RoboMME 上 82.0\%，跨两个机器人本体的四个真实世界任务上平均 83.8\%，在三种评测设置下均显著优于此前 SOTA。

Conclusion: 显式建模状态演化（变化）记忆优于孤立快照式记忆，能有效支撑长时程、记忆依赖的机器人操作任务，并在仿真与真实平台上一致取得显著提升。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action (VLA) models rely on current observations and lose task-relevant evidence once it leaves view, limiting performance on long-horizon, memory-dependent tasks. Existing efforts incorporate compressed historical features or sparse visual keyframes. However, isolated snapshots can leave the policy uncertain about what changed during past interactions and which action should follow. To overcome this limitation, we propose EvoMem-VLA, which constructs state-evolution memory by explicitly encoding and retaining observed changes between historical states. These change representations preserve evidence of interaction outcomes, allowing the policy to track task progress beyond isolated snapshots. Specifically, we introduce conditional delta tokenization to encode ordered frame pairs into directional, source-conditioned delta tokens, each associated with its corresponding state evidence. A shared VLM backbone supports task-adaptive routing: normal long-horizon tasks follow a direct action route, whereas multi-stage tasks use a subtask route that generates an executable subtask as an additional input for action generation. With a single jointly trained policy for each simulation benchmark, EvoMem-VLA achieves success rates of 80.7\% on RMBench, 82.0\% on RoboMME and 83.8\% across four real-world tasks spanning two robot embodiments. These results represent substantial improvements over the previous state of the art in all three evaluation settings.

</details>


### [298] [Social Navigation for Tour-guide Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05455)
*Vikram Shree,Jose Nino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于"社会力"的行人跟随导览机器人导航模型，将障碍物、用户位置与朝向等因素建模为力，并通过用户跟随实验（主观量表）评估其对跟随性、安全感和智能感的影响，与A*最短路径基线对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导览机器人在人群中导航时不仅需避障，还需让用户愿意跟随、感到安全与自然，传统最短路径（A*）规划缺乏社会性建模，因此需要引入社会力来提升机器人对用户的社会可接受性。

Method: 构建社会力导航模型：将障碍物、用户位置、用户朝向等分别表示为力矢量并叠加，用于机器人运动规划；设计实验，用户跟随自主机器人在已知地图中到达目标，中途完成若干简单干扰子任务；每位被试进行多轮实验，每轮使用不同的力组合以及A*最短路径基线模型；通过每轮主观指标测量跟随性、感知安全、感知智能等构念。

Result: 摘要仅描述实验设计，未给出实证结果；预期通过主观指标比较揭示各社会力对跟随性、感知安全与感知智能的具体影响。

Conclusion: 社会力建模有望提升导览机器人的社会可接受性，但该工作目前停留在模型提出与实验设计阶段，尚无量化结论。

Abstract: We propose a force-based model for social navigation of a tour-guide robot. Social forces due to various factors like obstacles, user position and heading, have been accounted for in the model. We claim that each one of these forces makes the robot more sociable to the user and we design an experimental setup for evaluation. In the experiment, the user follows an autonomous robot to a destination in a known map, while undertaking a few simple sub-tasks in the middle, which serve as distractions. For each participant, we run several rounds of the experiment, each with different forces and a shortest path, A*-search baseline model. Using per-round subjective indicators, we propose to study the effect of our force model on constructs such as: follow-ability, perceived safety, and perceived intelligence.

</details>


### [299] [FLEX-WAM: Flexible Block-Causal World-Action Models for Long-Horizon Imagination and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05483)
*R. Khorrambakht,Joseph Amigo,Félix Lebel,Leon Seetoo,Jean Ponce,Zhenzhen Li,Ludovic Righetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLEX-WAM：块因果、可KV缓存的世界-动作模型，支持变长上下文、非因果预测与无限自回归 rollout；通过平衡 state/action flow-matching 梯度与 Forward-Dynamics 弹性正则解决\"预测合理但动作响应弱\"的问题，在仿真与真机上实现数千步稳定联合 rollout 与 MCTS 想象规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合视频-动作世界模型多采用计算量大、固定视野的主干，不适合流式推理与稳定的长时程开环 rollout；且联合训练常出现\"未来看似合理但对指令动作响应微弱\"的失效模式。

Method: 块因果 + 可 KV 缓存架构，结合轴向注意力与分块 diffusion forcing，支持变长上下文、非因果预测视野，以及逐帧/逐块的无限自回归生成，并可在部署时权衡延迟-吞吐而无需重训。训练上用 state-action 扩散噪声网格上的梯度贡献平衡，并用 Forward-Dynamics (FD) 弹性作为动作响应性的高效代理来调控世界模型采样。

Result: 在仿真与真实数据集上，多步预测质量与延迟均优于基线，并支持上千步稳定联合状态-动作 rollout；作为 MCTS 中的联合动作提议器与模拟器，纯想象中求解长时程 PushT 与全部五个 OGBench Puzzle-4x4 任务；在双臂 OpenArm 平台上单一 checkpoint 同时充当 play policy 与预期结果预测器，实时识别并采集模型-现实不匹配样本。

Conclusion: FLEX-WAM 证明块因果、可缓存的世界-动作联合建模能在兼顾实时性的同时实现长时程稳定 rollout，并为想象规划与自主改进提供统一基础设施。

Abstract: World--action models (WAMs) promise a unified model that predicts action-conditioned futures, generates feasible actions, and supports planning in imagination. However, existing joint video--action models often use computationally heavy, fixed-horizon backbones ill-suited to streaming inference and stable long-horizon open-loop rollouts. We introduce FLEX-WAM, a Flexible and Efficient Block-Causal World--Action Model for unified simulation and policy inference. FLEX-WAM supports variable-length contexts and non-causal prediction horizons, as well as infinite autoregressive generation frame by frame or block by block. Its block-causal, KV-cacheable architecture combines axial attention and blockwise diffusion forcing to enable efficient real-time rollout and deployment-time latency--throughput tradeoffs without retraining. Joint training can nevertheless produce plausible futures that weakly respond to commanded actions. We address this failure mode by balancing state and action flow-matching gradient contributions across the state--action diffusion-noise grid and regulating world-model sampling using Forward-Dynamics (FD) elasticity, an efficient training-time proxy for action responsiveness. Across simulated and real-world datasets, FLEX-WAM achieves superior multi-step prediction quality and latency while producing stable joint state--action rollouts for thousands of steps. As a joint action proposer and simulator within MCTS, it solves long-horizon PushT and all five OGBench Puzzle-4x4 tasks entirely in imagination. On a bimanual OpenArm-based robot, a single checkpoint jointly serves as a play policy and expected-outcome predictor, enabling real-time identification and collection of model--reality mismatches for future self-improvement.

</details>


### [300] [Robust Surgical Robotic Instrument Tracking via Sequential Multi-Cue Fusion and Sim-to-Real Self-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05491)
*Hanyang Hu,Zekai Liang,Florian Richter,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出跟踪器引导的自训练框架：用机器人关节状态驱动的EKF+RTS平滑伪标签，把合成数据预训练的特征检测器适配到无标注真实手术视频，提升器械关键点跟踪精度与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人器械跟踪受复杂手术场景与器械非常规几何形状影响而困难。关键点方法高效但依赖可靠的特征检测，而真实世界精确标注（尤其位姿级标注）难以大规模获取，因此需要一种无需人工标注的自监督适配途径。

Method: 以合成图像预训练的检测器为起点，在无标注真实视频上自训练。利用测得的机器人关节状态，用不确定性感知EKF递归校正器械位姿与可观测关节角，将投影模型特征与检测到的关键点、轴杆边界及掩膜线索比对；随后用RTS平滑器精化轨迹，将轨迹投影为伪标签，用于微调特征检测器，全程避免繁琐的位姿标注。

Result: 在真实世界视频上，自训练使所有评估的关键点指标一致提升；所得模型在精度与运行时间上均优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 关节状态与几何模型约束可提供有效监督信号，替代人工位姿标注，实现合成到真实的特征检测器自适配；该方法兼顾效率与鲁棒性，代码与数据将公开。

Abstract: Efficient and robust tracking of surgical robotic instruments is important for robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery, yet remains challenging due to the complexity of surgical scenes and the unconventional geometry of surgical instruments. Keypoint-based approaches are efficient, but their performance depends on reliable feature detection. Improving these detectors with real-world supervision is difficult because accurate real-world annotations are costly to obtain at scale. To address this limitation, we introduce a tracker-guided self-training framework that adapts a model pretrained on synthetic images to unlabeled real-world videos. Given measured robot joint states, an uncertainty-aware EKF recursively corrects the instrument pose and the observable joint angles by comparing projected model features with detected keypoints, shaft boundaries, and mask-derived cues. An RTS smoother subsequently refines the resulting trajectory, which is projected into pseudo-labels for fine-tuning the feature detector without laborious pose annotations. Experiments on real-world videos demonstrate consistent improvements from self-training across all evaluated keypoint metrics, and the resulting model outperforms prior approaches in both accuracy and runtime. The code and data will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [301] [Robust 2D Traversability Mapping for Construction AMRs via Failure-Mode-Aware Fusion of LiDAR Geometry and Monocular Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05505)
*Manoj Karnekar,Om Mandhane,Gautham Ramkumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向活跃施工现场AMR的实时、故障模式感知多模态可通行性流水线：LiDAR为主几何安全估计，单目语义作类别与置信度门控的纠错信号，并发布多模态施工数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活跃施工现场存在两类互补的几何失效模式：几何平坦但视觉危险的表面（湿泥、积水混凝土）被LiDAR漏检；减速带、斜坡等可行驶突兀几何产生"幻影障碍"。单一几何可通行性映射无法同时处理这两类问题。

Method: 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上构建多模态可通行性流水线：保留平坦道路、地形、岩石地形等可通行类别并标记施工危险；将LiDAR投影到稠密语义掩码，用形态学修复解决稀疏性，结合Patchwork++做故障模式感知融合，修正互补几何失效并生成局部AMR代价地图。

Result: 发布了来自定制AMR的施工现场多模态数据集：两个活跃场地的四条闭环ROS 2序列（RGB、深度、LiDAR、IMU、GPS-RTK、里程计），以及跨28个语义类的506帧标注。系统实现了对互补几何失效模式的修正，用于局部AMR代价地图。

Conclusion: 以LiDAR为主、语义为门控纠错的融合范式可有效应对施工现场的可通行性失效模式；所发布数据集与实时边缘部署为后续施工机器人导航研究提供基础。

Abstract: Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs) on active construction sites face severe navigational challenges: geometry-based traversability mapping (e.g., LiDAR) misses visually hazardous but geometrically flat surfaces like wet mud and ponding concrete, while abrupt geometry on drivable speed-breakers and inclines produces phantom obstacles. We propose a real-time, failure-mode-aware multimodal traversability pipeline on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, where LiDAR is the primary geometric safety estimate and monocular semantics act as a selective, class- and confidence-gated corrective signal. The representation retains distinct traversable classes, namely flat road, terrain, and rocky terrain, while flagging construction hazards. We also release a multimodal construction-site dataset from a custom AMR: four closed-loop ROS 2 sequences from two active sites (RGB, depth, LiDAR, IMU, GPS-RTK, odometry) plus 506 annotated frames across 28 semantic classes. By projecting LiDAR onto dense semantic masks, resolving sparsity via morphological in-painting, and applying failure-mode-aware fusion with Patchwork++, the system corrects complementary geometric failure modes for a local AMR costmap.

</details>


### [302] [Neural Barriers: An Online Certifiable Learning-enhanced Adaptive High Order Safety Critical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05542)
*Lishuo Pan,Mattia Catellani,An Cao,Lorenzo Sabattini,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可认证的在线学习增强鲁棒自适应控制障碍函数(CBF)方法，用Neural ODE在线学习未知时变扰动，用共形预测量化自适应不确定性，从而在模型扰动下为机器人系统提供概率化的全时段安全保证，并随数据增加逐步降低保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBF是形式化验证系统安全的有效模型化工具，但其理论保证依赖高保真模型；实际机器人系统（如无人机受载荷或风扰）存在显著模型摄动，导致安全保证失效。因此需要一种能在未知时变扰动下在线适应、同时保持可证明安全性的控制方法。

Method: 将Neural ODE用于在线学习未知时变模型扰动，用共形预测(conformal prediction)量化学习自适应的不确定性；在不确定性高时采取保守控制策略，随数据累积逐步收紧估计以降低保守性；在模型与轨迹满足Lipschitz平滑假设下，推导出带概率界的全时段安全保证。

Result: 方法在未知时变模型扰动下实现了全时段安全保证，并给出具概率界的安全认证；随着数据增多，控制器保守性得到有效降低，验证了其作为面向模型摄动的机器人安全控制器的实用性潜力（摘要未给出具体实验数值或真机部署细节）。

Conclusion: 该工作将在线学习与共形不确定性量化引入鲁棒自适应CBF，在理论保证与工程适应性之间取得平衡，为存在模型摄动的机器人系统提供了一种可认证的实用安全控制方案。

Abstract: Control barrier functions are an effective model-based tool to formally certify the safety of a system. However, transferring their theoretical guarantees to real-world robotics systems requires high model fidelity. For example, payloads or wind disturbances can cause significant model perturbations to an aerial vehicle, leading to safety compromises. In this work, we propose a certifiable online learning-enhanced robust adaptive control barrier function, which adapts to disturbances using a Neural ODE and quantifies its adaptation uncertainty with conformal prediction. Our approach guarantees safety at all time under unknown time-varying model disturbances. It adopts a conservative strategy when the adaptation uncertainty is high; and efficiently adapts to reduce controller conservativeness as it receives more data. Our approach provides a provable safety guarantee with a probability bound under suitable Lipschitz smoothness assumptions on the underlying model and trajectory. These results demonstrate the potential of our method as a practical safety controller for robotics system operating under model perturbations.

</details>


### [303] [GR-LIO: A Local Ground-Aware LiDAR-Inertial Odometry System Using Body-to-Ground Height](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05546)
*Zhixin Zhang,Yang Song,Liang Zhao,Nathan Shankar,Barry Lennox,Pawel Ladosz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于滤波器的局部地面感知LiDAR-惯性里程计框架，将地-体高度与朝向参数化的局部地平面纳入状态估计，实现在线标定、地平面分割与平面运动约束，提升定位精度与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR-惯性里程计（LIO）系统对局部地面提供的几何约束利用不足，垂直漂移与地面点云处理效率问题未被充分挖掘。

Method: 用机器人朝向与body-to-ground高度参数化局部地平面并在滤波状态中连续传播；基于该B-G几何模型进行高效可靠的地面分割，将地面点以点到面约束引入滤波更新；引入平面运动更新作为额外几何约束抑制垂直漂移；设计B-G高度初始化策略与在线标定。

Result: 在多个公开基准数据集与自采真实场景数据集上，定位精度与计算效率均稳定优于代表性LIO方法。

Conclusion: 显式建模局部地面几何可同时提升地面移动机器人状态估计的精度与效率，是一种轻量且有效的LIO改进方向。

Abstract: LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO) is widely used for state estimation in ground-based autonomous mobile robots. However, the geometric constraints provided by the local ground surface remain largely underexploited in existing LIO systems. This paper proposes a filter-based local ground-aware LIO framework that explicitly incorporates local ground plane geometry into the state estimation process to improve both localization accuracy and computational efficiency. Specifically, a local ground plane is parameterized by the robot orientation and the body-to-ground (B-G) height and continuously propagated within the state estimation process. Based on the proposed B-G geometry model, a propagated local ground plane enables efficient and reliable ground segmentation. The segmented ground points are then incorporated into the filter update through point-to-plane geometric constraints, improving both state estimation accuracy and efficiency. Furthermore, a planar motion update is introduced to exploit the propagated local ground plane as an additional geometric constraint, effectively suppressing vertical drift and improving estimation robustness. To address the initially unknown B-G height, an efficient initialization strategy is developed, followed by an online calibration procedure for continuous refinement. The proposed system is evaluated on several public benchmark datasets and self-collected real-world datasets covering diverse operating scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms representative LIO methods in terms of both localization accuracy and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [304] [TacGELLO: Tactile Contact Feedback for a 3D-Printed Teleoperation Leader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05583)
*Younsoo Kim,Sangmin Song,Sarath Kodagoda,Marc Carmichael,Karthick Thiyagarajan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacGELLO 利用 3D 打印主臂自身的舵机与电流信号，实现夹爪触觉反馈与关节负载反馈，显著提升遥操作命令精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动式主臂遥操作缺乏远端接触与负载力反馈，操作者难以感知夹持与抬升过程中的交互状态，导致夹持过紧或过松、命令跟踪偏差大。

Method: 复用 3D 打印主臂的现有舵机：夹爪扳机处通过 AnySkin 接触检测触发位置保持（trigger position hold），关节处利用从臂关节电流变化调整主动舵机输出限幅，从而在抬升阶段产生阻力感；在一台 UR3 从臂上做 6 次单操作者会话验证。

Result: 发送命令跟踪误差 0.15--0.36° RMSE，估计轨迹滞后 82--98 ms；62 个分割抓取区间中 60 个记录到触觉起始；触觉/宽度接触参考后中位命令闭合量为 0.7--2.1 mm（TacGELLO）对 13.0--13.9 mm（原接口）。

Conclusion: 以低成本、无需额外硬件的电流式反馈即可在主臂上实现触觉与负载提示，显著改善无接触感知遥操作的抓取闭合精度，为触觉遥操作提供可复用的改造路径。

Abstract: Passive leader arms transmit an operator's motion without returning remote contact or load cues. TacGELLO uses the existing servos of a 3D-printed leader for tactile contact feedback at the gripper trigger and current-based load cues at the arm joints. AnySkin contact detection activates a trigger position hold, while changes in follower joint current adjust leader-servo output limits to command resistance during lifting. Across six UR3 sessions with one operator, sent-command tracking error was 0.15--0.36$^\circ$ RMSE, with estimated trajectory lag of 82--98\,ms. Tactile onset was logged in 60 of 62 segmented TacGELLO grasp intervals. Median commanded closure after tactile or width-based contact references was 0.7--2.1\,mm with TacGELLO and 13.0--13.9\,mm with the original interface.

</details>


### [305] [Building A Multi-Sensor Platform For Autonomous Driving Research: Challenges and Lessons Learned](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05604)
*Paulo Ricardo Marques de Araujo,Eslam Mounier,Qamar Bader,Emma Dawson,Aboelmagd Noureldin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文总结了自主驾驶研究中一款灵活多传感器平台的开发与部署经验，涵盖机械设计、传感器标定、电源管理与时间同步等共性挑战，并分享了相应缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠且多样的数据集需求日益增长，需要新的环境与传感配置来应对真实运行挑战，因此许多研究组自建多传感器采集平台，但无论传感器类型如何，都会遇到机械设计、标定、供电、时间同步等基础性问题。

Method: 以经验报告（experience report）形式，回顾自研柔性多传感器平台在开发与实地部署中遇到的问题，并归纳采用的缓解方案，如基于GNSS授时与NTP协议的系统级时间同步、面向不同传感器配置的定制标定流程，以及提升可靠性与数据完整性的设计实践。

Result: 梳理出一系列共性技术挑战与关键洞察，形成可用于指导未来平台设计的经验总结，提升自主驾驶测试的可复现性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为开发新型多传感器系统的研究者提供参考，强调设计规范与工程实践对数据采集质量与平台可靠性的重要作用。

Abstract: This paper reports on the challenges encountered and lessons learned during the development and deployment of a flexible multi-sensor platform for autonomous driving research. It aims to serve as a reference for researchers developing new multi-sensor systems. As the need for reliable, diverse datasets increases, novel environments and sensing configurations are essential to tackle real-world operational challenges. Consequently, many research groups create custom multi-sensory data collection platforms, where fundamental issues, such as mechanical design, sensor calibration, power management, and time synchronization, arise regardless of sensor types. We reflect on these challenges and share key insights to guide future platform designs, enhancing reproducibility and robustness in autonomous vehicle testing. We also summarize the mitigation strategies we adopted, for instance, system-wide time synchronization using GNSS timing and NTP protocols, custom calibration routines for different sensor configurations, and design practices to improve reliability and data integrity during field deployments.

</details>


### [306] [Emergency Obstacle Avoidance Maneuvers in Differential-Drive Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05607)
*Kautik Mandve,Nilay Kant*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究差速驱动机器人在紧急避障中，机动性能与里程计误差对接近速度的依赖关系：通过足印间隙分析与归一化轮运动指数量化五种制动/转向机动，并在瓷砖地面上完成540次区组随机试验（保留539次），以顶视相机为独立位姿参考。发现两种反转自旋机动的编码器航向偏差随速度升高增大4.7–5.1°，而弧线与制动辅助枢轴机动变化小于0.4°；编码器偏差越大，避障成功率越低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧急避障要求机器人在可用距离内完成制动或转向，而机动能力与里程计误差往往随接近速度变化。现有工作缺乏对速度依赖性的系统实验刻画，且编码器误差、恢复运动与测量窗口错配三者常被混淆，导致避障成功率难以预测。作者希望建立"速度特异"的经验性机动表征，为紧急机动的反应距离与成功率提供量化依据。

Method: 以差速驱动机器人为平台，提出足印间隙（footprint-clearance）分析与归一化轮运动指数，对五种制动与转向机动做运动学描述。主实验为瓷砖地面540次区组随机试验（保留539次），指令接近速度45/55/65 cm/s，用顶视相机提供独立位姿参考，与编码器、陀螺仪航向变化在完整运动记录上对比。统计上控制距离、速度、机动类型、实验日与转向方向后，分析编码器偏差与避障成功率的关系，并估计90%成功率所需反应距离及其不确定性。

Result: 在完整运动记录上评估航向变化时，两种反转自旋机动从最低到最高速度的编码器平均偏差增加4.7–5.1°，弧线与制动辅助枢轴机动变化小于0.4°；陀螺仪在反转自旋中偏差约为3°。编码器偏差越大，在控制距离、速度、机动、实验日与转向方向后避障成功率越低。给出了90%成功率下的估计反应距离及其不确定性，量化了各机动的权衡。

Conclusion: 结果支持对紧急机动进行速度特异的经验性表征，并将编码器误差与恢复运动、测量窗口错配区分开来。该分析为差速机器人紧急避障的距离预算与成功率预测提供了可操作的运动学与误差量化框架，强调里程计误差应作为速度相关的性能指标纳入机动设计。

Abstract: Emergency obstacle avoidance requires a mobile robot to brake or change its heading within the available distance. This paper investigates the dependence of maneuver performance and odometric error on approach speed for a differential-drive robot. A footprint-clearance analysis and a normalized wheel-motion index provide a kinematic description of five braking and turning maneuvers. The principal experiment comprises 540 block-randomized trials on tile, of which 539 are retained, at commanded approach speeds of 45, 55, and 65 cm/s. An overhead camera provides an independent pose reference. When encoder, gyro, and camera heading changes are evaluated over complete motion records, the mean encoder discrepancy increases by 4.7-5.1 degrees for the two reverse-spin maneuvers between the lowest and highest speeds; the arc and brake-assisted pivot change by less than 0.4 degrees. Larger encoder discrepancy is associated with lower avoidance success after adjustment for distance, speed, maneuver, day, and turn direction. Gyro discrepancies remain approximately 3 degrees for the reverse spins. Estimated reaction distances for 90% success and their uncertainty quantify the maneuver trade-offs. The results support speed-specific empirical characterization of emergency maneuvers and distinguish encoder error from recovery motion and measurement-window mismatch.

</details>


### [307] [FOCUS: Fine-Grained Open-Vocabulary Change Detection for Uncertainty-Aware Semi-Static Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05639)
*Can Xu,Mingfeng Yuan,Mahan Mohammadi,Steven L. Waslander*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FOCUS：面向半静态环境的物体级变化检测与地图维护框架，将记忆维护建模为概率推断，融合几何似然与3D高斯地图渲染的外观似然，用递归三状态估计器判定物体为PERSISTED/REPLACED/REMOVED，并在Isaac Sim仓库基准与TorWIC真实数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期自主机器人在多次访问之间世界会发生变化，需要保持环境记忆最新。在半静态环境中，物体可能被几何与语义相似的其他实例原地替换，仅靠几何或粗粒度语义难以识别身份变化，因此需要细粒度的外观证据与不确定性建模。

Method: 将半静态记忆维护构造为概率推断，融合几何似然与由3D高斯地图渲染得到的外观似然；采用递归三状态估计器为每个已建图物体维护PERSISTED/REPLACED/REMOVED状态；针对3DGS渲染不完美，把渲染外观证据概率化建模而非直接作为变化分数，模型参数通过初始建图会话的无变化重放自动校准。

Result: 在包含歧义性原地替换的新Isaac Sim仓库基准与真实TorWIC数据集上评估，物体级替换F1从概率基线的0.20提升到0.84，且无需人工重调参数即可迁移到真实数据。

Conclusion: 显式建模渲染不确定性并融合几何与外观似然，可显著提升半静态环境中物体级替换检测与长期地图维护的可靠性，并具备跨仿真到真实的迁移能力。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating over long periods must keep their environmental memory up to date as the world changes between visits. In semi-static environments, an object may be replaced in place by a different but geometrically and semantically similar instance, making the identity change difficult to detect from geometry or coarse semantics alone. We present FOCUS, an uncertainty-aware framework for object-level change detection and map maintenance. We formulate semi-static memory maintenance as probabilistic inference that fuses geometric likelihood with appearance likelihood rendered from a 3D Gaussian map. A recursive three-state estimator maintains whether each mapped object is PERSISTED, REPLACED, or REMOVED. To account for imperfect 3DGS rendering, we model the rendered appearance evidence probabilistically rather than using it as a direct change score, with the model parameters automatically calibrated from a change-free replay of the initial mapping session. We evaluate our method on a new Isaac Sim warehouse benchmark with ambiguous in-place replacements and on the real-world TorWIC dataset. It improves object-level replacement F1 from 0.20 to 0.84 over a probabilistic baseline and transfers to real-world data without manual parameter retuning.

</details>


### [308] [Spacecraft Rendezvous Trajectory Generation with Modular Constraints via Diffusion Model Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05642)
*Mariko A. Storey-Matsutani,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于扩散模型的在轨交会近距操作(RPO)轨迹生成方法，通过组合多个能量约束模型实现无需重训的约束重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨服务、卫星巡检、主动碎片清除等任务需要能适应多种场景的轨迹设计方法，现有方法难以灵活配置不断变化的约束组合。

Method: 先分别用优化轨迹训练针对单一约束（如接近锥、传感器视线）的能量型扩散模型；推理时将这些学习到的能量模型相互组合，或与解析定义的能量场（如避障）组合，从而施加任意约束组合。

Result: 在"学习接近锥+学习视线"以及"学习接近锥+合成避障"两组组合中，约束满足率达到单一约束模型的1个百分点以内或更高。

Conclusion: 该组合式扩散框架为RPO轨迹设计提供了模块化方案，可在不重新训练的情况下实现新约束组合的重构。

Abstract: Emerging mission classes such as on-orbit servicing, satellite inspection, and active debris removal require trajectory design methods that are adaptable to a variety of mission scenarios. We present a diffusion-based trajectory generation approach for rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) that enables flexible configuration of mission constraints. First, individual energy-based diffusion models are trained to satisfy distinct constraints such as approach cone and sensor line-of-sight from a set of optimized trajectories. Then, at inference time, the learned energy models can be composed with one another, or with an analytically defined energy field, to enforce specific constraint combinations.
